   kūṭi eḻuttu nāṟpatu3 eṉṟalum poruntiṟṟu. oṟṟaḷapeṭai ceyyuṭkē varutaliṉ
   īṇṭuk kūṟārāyiṉār.
   -----------
   1 aḷapeṭutta neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ ēḻum tamittup poruḷtariṉ collām;
       tarāviṭiṉ eḻuttām.
       2 icaiviḷi paṇṭamāṟṟu mutaliyavaṟṟil nīṇṭolikkum ulakiyalaḷapeṭaiyē
       iyaṟkai yaḷapeṭaiyām. iṉi makaṭūu marūu eṉa eṉṟum aḷapeṭuttē
       niṟpavaṟṟaiyum iyaṟkaiyaḷapeṭai yeṉpar oru cār āciriyar.
       3 muppattumūṉṟeḻuttukkaḷuṭaṉ aḷapeṭutta neṭilēḻuṅkakūṭa nāṟpatu.

   avvaḷapuṭaiya eṉap paṉmaiyākak kūṟiyavataṉāṉ ivarum nāṉku māttiraiyuṅ
   koṇṭār. eṉṉai? ivvāciriyarai "muntunūlkaṇṭu" eṉṟārākaliṉ. māpurāṇattu,
   "ceyyuṭka ḷōcai citaiyuṅkāl īraḷapum
   aiyappā ṭiṉṟi yaṇaiyumām-maitīroṟ
   ṟiṉṟiyuñ ceyyuṭ ceṭiṉoṟṟai yuṇṭākku
   kuṉṟumē loṟṟaḷapuṅ koḷ"
   eṉṟa cūttirattāṉ avar koṇṭa nāṉku māttiraiyum ivvāciriyarkku
   nērtalvēṇṭutaliṉ. atu "ceṟāaay vāḻiya neñcu" (kuṟaḷ-1200) "tūuut
   tīmpukait tolvicumpu" (malaipaṭu-iṟuti veṇpā) "pēeert tukkol"
   "ilāaarkkillai tamar" (nālaṭi-283) "virāayc ceyyāmai naṉṟu'
   (nālaṭi-246) 'marīiippiṉṉaip pirivu' (nālaṭi-220) eṉac cāṉṟōr
   ceyyuṭkellām nāṉku māttirai peṟṟuniṉṟaṉa. aṉṟi mūṉṟu māttirai
   peṟṟaṉavēl āciriyattaḷai taṭṭuc ceppalōcai keṭumāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam
   aḷapeṭātu āciriyattaḷaitaṭṭu niṟpaṉa kalikku uṟuppākiya koccaka
   veṇpākkaḷ; avai aṉṉavaṉṟeṉa
   uṇarka.
   kōṭṭunūṟum mañcaḷuṅ kūṭiyavaḻip piṟanta cevvaṇṇampōla neṭiluṅ kuṟiluṅ
   kūṭiya kūṭṭattup piṟanta piṉṉarp piḷavupaṭā vōcaiyai aḷapeṭai yeṉṟu
   āciriyar vēṇṭiṉār. ivai kūṭṭic colliya kālattallatu pulappaṭā,
   eḷḷāṭṭiyavaḻiyallatu eṇṇey pulappaṭāvāṟu pōla eṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi
   aḷapeṭaiyallāta ōcaikaḷellām icaiyōcaiyātaliṉ avaṟṟai 'aḷapiṟan
   tuyirttalum' (eḻu - 33) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṟ kūṟupa.
   ------------
   māttiraikku aḷavu
   7. kaṇṇimai noṭiyeṉa avvē māttirai
   nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭa vāṟē.
   itu māttiraikku aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: kaṇṇimai noṭi eṉa avvē māttirai - kaṇṇimai yeṉavum noṭi yeṉavum
   avviraṇṭē eḻuttiṉ māttiraikku aḷavu, nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭavāṟē -
   nuṇṇitāka nūlilakkaṇattai uṇarnta āciriyar kaṇṭa neṟi; e - ṟu.
   'eṉa' eṇṇiṟ pirintu iraṇṭiṭattuṅ kūṭiṟṟu. kaṇṇimai noṭi eṉṉum
   palaporuḷoru coṟkaḷ īṇṭut toḻiṉmēlum ōcai mēlum muṟaiyē niṉṟaṉa.
   āciriyar ellārum eḻuttiṟku ivaiyē aḷavākak kūṟaliṉ ivaruṅ kūṟiṉār.
   iyaṟkai makaṉ(1) taṉ kuṟippiṉaṉṟi iraṇṭimaiyum orukāṟ kūṭi nīṅkiṉa
   kālakkaḻivum a-eṉap piṟanta ōcaiyatu tōṟṟak kēṭṭuk kālakkaḻivum okkum.
   ikkaṇṇimaiyiṉatu pākam meykkuñ cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟattiṟkum itaṉ pākam makarak
   kuṟukkattiṟkuṅ koḷka. ikkaṇṇimai iraṭṭittu varutal neṭiṟkum atu mūṉṟum
   nāṉkumāy varutal aḷapeṭaikkuṅ koḷka. atupōlavē noṭittaṟ ṟoḻiliṟ piṟanta
   ōcaiyatu tōṟṟakkēṭṭuk kālakkaḻivum a eṉap piṟanta ōcaiyatu tōṟṟakkēṭṭuk
   kālakkaḻivum okkum. ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkuṅ kūṟiyavāṟē
   koḷka.
   iṉi avvaḷavaitāṉ niṟuttaḷattal, peytaḷattal(2), cārttiyaḷattal(3),
   nīṭṭiyaḷattal, teṟittaḷattal(4), tēṅkamukantaḷattal(5), eṇṇiyaḷattal
   eṉa ēḻuvakaittu. avaṟṟuḷ itu cārttiyaḷattalām.
   -------
   (1) iyaṟkaimakaṉ - uṭalnalattōṭiruntu iyalpāy imaippavaṉ.
       (2) peytaḷattal - eṇṇeypōṉṟa nīrpporuḷaip paṭiyil ūṟṟiyaḷattal.
       (3) cārttiyaḷattal - oṉṟaṉ aḷavōṭu maṟṟoṉṟiṉ aḷavai oppiṭṭaḷattal.
       (4) teṟittaḷattal - icaikkaruviyaip puṭaittu ataṉ oliyaic ceviyāṟ
       kēṭṭu matittal.
       (5) tēṅkamukantaḷattal - aricipōṉṟavaṟṟai paṭiyāl kumpa
       mukantaḷattal.

   kaṇṇimaikkum noṭikkum aḷavu ārāyiṉ varampiṉṟi ōṭumeṉṟu karuti ‘nuṇṇiti
   ṉuṇarntōr kaṇṭavāṟu’ eṉṟu muṭintatu kāṭṭaleṉṉum utti kūṟiṉār. iḵtu āṇai
   kūṟutalumām. eṉavē eḻuttiṟkē aḷavu kūṟi māttiraikku aḷavu kūṟiṟṟilar.
   noṭiyiṟ kaṇṇimai ciṟappuṭaittu, uḷḷattāṉ niṉaittu nikaḻāmaiyiṉ. (7)
   ----------

                         1.3. eṇ - paṉṉīruyir

   8. aukāra iruvāyp
   paṉṉī reḻuttum uyireṉa moḻipa.
   itu kuṟilaiyum neṭilaiyun tokuttu vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: aukāra iṟuvāyp paṉṉīreḻuttum-akaram mutalāka aukāram īṟākak
   kiṭantu paṉṉiraṇṭeḻuttum, uyireṉa moḻipa-uyireṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
   eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
   ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiyatoru kuṟi. mey patiṉeṭṭiṉaiyum iyakkit
   tāṉ aruvāy vaṭiviṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ uyirāyiṟṟu. ivai meykku uyirāy niṉṟu
   meykaḷai iyakkumēl uyireṉa vēṟōr eḻuttiṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ, meyyi ṉiṟkum
   uyirun taṉiyē niṟkum uyirum vēṟeṉa uṇarka. eṉṉai? ‘akara mutala’
   (kuṟaḷ-1) eṉpuḻi akaran taṉiyuyirumāyk kakaravoṟṟu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku
   uyirumāy vēṟu niṟṟaliṉ. avvakaran taṉiyē niṟṟalāṉum pala meykkaṇ niṉṟu
   avvam meykaṭku icainta ōcaikaḷaip payantē niṟṟalāṉum vēṟupaṭṭatākaliṉ
   oṉṟēyāyum palavēyāyum niṟpatōr taṉmaiyaiyuṭaitteṉṟu kōṭum; iṟaivaṉ
   oṉṟēyāy niṟkun taṉmaiyum palluyirkkun tāṉēyāy avaṟṟiṉ aḷavāy niṟkun
   taṉmaiyum pōla. atu a eṉṟa vaḻiyum ūra eṉa viḷiyēṟṟa vaḻiyum ‘akara
   mutala’ eṉṟavaḻiyum mūviṉaṅkaḷil ēṟiṉa vaḻiyum ōcai vēṟupaṭṭavāṟṟāṉ
   uṇarka. iṅṅaṉam icaittuḻiyum māttirai oṉṟēyām. iḵtu ēṉai uyirkaṭkum
   okkum.
   aukāra iṟuvāy eṉpatu paṇputtokai. ummai muṟṟummai. ‘akaramutal’ eṉa
   muṟkūṟip pōntamaiyiṉ īṇṭu īṟē kūṟiṉār. (8)
   ----------
   patiṉeṉ mey
   9. ṉakāra iṟuvāyp
   patiṉeṇ eḻuttum meyyeṉa moḻipa.
   iḵtu uyirallaṉavaṟṟait tokuttu ōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ṉakāra iruvāyp patiṉeṉ eḻuttum- kakāram mutal ṉakāram īrāyk
   kiṭanta patiṉeṭṭu eḻuttum, meyyeṉa moḻipa-meyyeṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
   eṉṟu
   kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
   ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. eṉṉai? paṉṉīruyirkkuntāṉ
   iṭaṅkoṭuttu avaṟṟāṉ iyaṅkun taṉmai peṟṟa uṭampāy niṟṟaliṉ.
   ṉakāra iṟuvāy eṉpatu paṇputtokai. ummai muṟṟummai. muṉṉar ṉakāra
   iṟuvāyeṉpuḻi muppateḻuttiṟkum īṟākumeṉṟār, īṇṭup patiṉeṭṭeḻuttiṟkum
   īṟāmeṉṟārātaliṟ kūṟiyatu kūṟiṟṟaṉṟu.
   -------------
   uyirmeykku aḷavu
   10. meyyō ṭiyaiyiṉum uyiriyal tiriyā.
   iḵtu uyirmeykku aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷai: uyir meyyōṭu iyaiyiṉum – paṉṉīruyirum patiṉeṭṭu meyyōṭuṅ kūṭi
   niṉṟaṉa vāyiṉum, iyal tiriyā - tam aḷavuṅ kuṟiyum eṇṇun tirintu nillā;
   e-ṟu.
   itu 'puḷḷi yillā' (eḻu - 17) eṉpataṉai nōkki niṟṟaliṉ etiratu pōṟṟalām.
   uyirum meyyum atikārap paṭutaliṉ īṇṭuvaittār. a eṉpuḻi niṉṟa aḷavuṅ
   kuṟiyum oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇuṅ ka eṉa niṉṟa iṭattum okkum; ā eṉpuḻi niṉṟa
   aḷavuṅ kuṟiyum oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇuṅkā eṉa niṉṟa iṭattum okkum eṉpatu itaṉ
   karuttu. piṟavum aṉṉa. āyiṉ oṉṟarai māttiraiyum iraṇṭarai māttiraiyum
   uṭaiyaṉa oru māttiraiyum iraṇṭu māttiraiyum āyavāṟu eṉṉai yeṉiṉ, nīr
   taṉittu aḷantuḻiyum nāḻiyāy araināḻiyuppiṟ kalantuḻiyuṅ kūṭi
   oṉṟaraināḻiyāy mikātavāṟu(1) pōlvatōr poruṭ peṟṟi yeṉṟu koḷvatallatu
   kāraṇaṅ kūṟalākāmai uṇarka. āciriyaṉ āṇai eṉpārum uḷar.
   'viḷaṅkāy tiraṭṭiṉā rillai kaḷaṅkaṉiyaik
   kāreṉac ceytāru mil.' (nālaṭiyār - 103)
   eṉpatē kāṭṭiṉār uraiyāciriyarum.
   ---------
   (1) oru nāḻi nīril araināḻi yuppaik kalappiṉ oṉṟarai nāḻi yākāviṭiṉum
       oru nāḻikkuk kūṭutal tiṇṇam. ātalāl, itai meyyōṭu cērnta uyir
       uyiraḷavāyē olippataṟku uvamai kūṟal poruttamiṉṟu.

   -----------
   taṉimeykku aḷavu
   11. meyyiṉ aḷavē araiyeṉa moḻipa.
   itu taṉimeykku aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: meyyiṉ aḷavē araiyeṉa moḻipa – meyyiṉatu māttiraiyiṉai orōvoṉṟu
   araimāttiraiyuṭaiyaveṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar; e- ṟu.
   av varaimāttiraiyun taṉittuk kūṟik kāṭṭalākātu, nāc ciṟitu puṭaipeyarun
   taṉmaiyāy niṟṟaliṉ. iṉi ataṉaic cila moḻimēṟ peytu kākkai kōṅku kavvai
   yeṉak kāṭṭupa. meyyeṉpatu aḵṟiṇaiyiyaṟ peyarātiliṉ meyyeṉṉum oṟṟumai
   paṟṟi arai yeṉṟār. (11)
   ----------------
   cārpeḻuttukku aḷavu.
   12. avviyal nilaiyum ēṉai mūṉṟē.
   itu cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟattu mūṉṟaṟkkum aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ : ēṉai mūṉṟu - cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟattu mūṉṟum, avviyal nilaiyum - muṟkūṟiya
   arai māttiraiyākiya iyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṟkum; e - ṟu.
   kēṇmiyā nāku eḵku eṉa varum. (12)
   --------------
   makarak kuṟukkam.
   13. araiyaḷavu kuṟukal makaram uṭaittē
   icaiyiṭaṉ arukun teriyuṅ kālai.
   itu meykaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
   i - ḷ : icai uṭaṉ makaram araiyaḷavu kuṟukaluṭaittu - vēṟōr eḻuttiṉatu
   ōcaiyiṉkaṇ makara voṟṟu taṉ arai māttiraiyiṟ kuṟukik kāl māttirai
   peṟutalai yuṭaittu, teriyuṅkālai arukum - ārāyuṅ kālattut tāṉ
   ciṟupāṉmaiyāy varum; e - ṟu.
   e - ṭu: pōṉm varumvaṇṇakkaṇ eṉa oru moḻikkaṇṇum iru moḻikkaṇṇuṅkoḷka.
   it piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal eṉṉum utti.1 (13)
   ---------------
   1. tolkāppiyar makarak kuṟukkattai oru cārpeḻuttākak koṇṭilar.
       naṉṉūlārō ataic cārpeḻuttākak koṇṭār. nacciṉārkiṉiyarum ataic
       cārpeḻuttākak karutikkoṇṭu itu 'piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal' eṉṟār.

   -----------

                               1.4. vaṭivu.

   makarattiṉ vaṭivam.
   14. uṭpeṟu puḷḷi uruvā kummē.
   itu pakarattōṭu makarattiṭai varivaṭivu vēṟṟumai ceykiṉṟatu. makaram
   atikārappaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār.
   i - ḷ : uṭpeṟupuḷḷi - puṟattuppeṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟpeṟum puḷḷi,
   uruvākum - makarattiṟku vaṭivām; e - ṟu.
   eṉavē puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyāvatu mēṟcūttirattāṉ meykaṭkuk kūṟum puḷḷi.
   īṇṭu uruveṉṟatu kāṭcipporuḷai uṇartti niṉṟatu.
   e - ṭu: kappi kappi (kammi) eṉavarum, iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟal. (14)
   ------------
   meyyeḻuttiṉ iyalpu.
   15. meyyiṉ iyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal.
   itu taṉimeykkum uyirmeykkum oppumaimēl vēṟṟumai ceytal kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   eṉṉai? uyirmeyyāṉa kakara ṅakaraṅkaṭkun taṉimeyyāṉa kakara
   ṅakaraṅkaṭkum vaṭivu oṉṟāka eḻutiyavaṟṟai oṟṟākkutaṟkup piṉpu puḷḷi
   peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ.
   i-ḷ: meyyiṉ iyaṟkai puḷḷiyōṭu nilaiyal- patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷiṉ taṉmaiyāṉatu
   puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟṟalām; e-ṟu.
   eṉavē uyirmeykaṭkup puḷḷiyiṉṟāyiṟṟu. k-ṅ-ṟ-ṉ eṉa varum. ivaṟṟaip
   puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭavē puḷḷi peṟuvataṟku muṉṉar akaram uṭaṉiṉṟatōr
   meyvaṭivē peṟṟu niṉṟaṉavaṟṟaip piṉṉar appuḷḷiyiṭṭu taṉimey yākkiṉa
   reṉpatūum peṟutum. itaṉāṉē kakaram ṅakaram mutaliyaṉa puḷḷipeṟuvataṟku
   muṉṉar iyalpāka akaram peṟṟē niṟkum eṉpatūum puḷḷi peṟuṅkālattu
   avvakaram nīṅkum eṉpatūum piṉṉar ap puḷḷi nīṅki uyirēṟumiṭattut taṉkaṇ
   akaram nīṅkiyē pōka varukiṉṟatōr uyir yātāṉum oṉṟu ēṟi niṟkum eṉpatūum
   peṟṟām. ‘meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum’ (eḻu-46) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉum
   ituvē itaṟkuk karuttātal uṇarka. (15)
   ------------
   ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpu
   16. ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: ekara okarattu iyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē-ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉatu nilaiyum
   meypōlap puḷḷipeṟum iyalpiṟṟu; e-ṟu.
   eṉavē ekāra okāraṅkaṭkup puḷḷi yiṉṟāyiṟṟu. e-o- eṉa varum. iḵtu
   uyirmeykkum okkum. makaram ārāyccippaṭṭatu kaṇṭu makarattiṟku vaṭivu
   vēṟṟumai ceytu, atikārattāṉ meyyiṉ taṉmai kūṟi, ataṉpiṉ māṭṭēṟṟaliṉ
   ekara okarattaiyuṅ kūṟiṉār.
   ------------
   uyirmeyyeḻuttiṉ iyalpu
   17. puḷḷi yillā ellā meyyum
   uruvuru vāki akaramō ṭuyirttalum
   ēṉai uyirō ṭuruvutirin tuyirttalum
   āyī riyala uyirtta lāṟē.
   itu meyyum uyiruṅ kūṭip puṇarumāṟum āṇṭu avai tiriyātun tirintum
   niṟkumāṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: puḷḷi illā ellā meyyum-uyiraip peṟutaṟkup puḷḷiyaip pōkkiṉa ellā
   meykaḷum, uru uruvāki akaramōṭu uyirttalum-puḷḷi peṟukiṉṟa kālattu
   iyalpākiya akara nīṅkiya vaṭivē tamakku vaṭivāki niṉṟu piṉṉar ēṟiya
   akarattōṭukūṭi olittalum, ēṉai uyirōṭu uruvu tirintu uyirttalum --
   oḻinta patiṉōruyirōṭuṅkūṭi av vaṭivu tirintu olittalum, āyīriyala
   uyirttalāṟē -- eṉa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya avai olikkum muṟaimai;
   e-ṟu.
   puḷḷiyillā peyyeṉavē muṉpeṟṟuniṉṟa puḷḷiyai uyirēṟṟutaṟkup pōkkiṉamai
   peṟutum. uruvuruvākiyeṉavē puḷḷi peṟutaṟkāka iyalpākiya akaram nīṅkiya
   vaṭivē piṉṉar akaram peṟutaṟku vaṭavāmeṉpatu
   kūṟiṉār.
   ka - ṅa - ya eṉa varum.
   uruvu tirintu uyirattalāvatu mēluṅ kīḻum vilaṅku peṟṟuṅ kōṭu peṟṟum
   puḷḷipeṟṟum puḷḷiyuṅ kōṭum uṭaṉpeṟṟum uyirttalām. ki kī mutaliyaṉa
   mēlvilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ku kū mutaliyaṉa kīḻvilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ke kē
   mutaliyaṉa kōṭu peṟṟaṉa. kā ṅā mutaliyaṉa puḷḷi peṟṟaṉa. arukē peṟṟa
   puḷḷiyai ikkālattār kālāka eḻutiṉār. makaram uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyai
   vaḷaitteḻutiṉār.1 ko kō ṅo ṅō mutaliyaṉa puḷḷiyuṅ kōṭum uṭaṉpeṟṟaṉa.
   iṅṅaṉan tirintu olippavē uyirmey paṉṉirupatiṉeṭṭu
   irunūṟṟorupattāṟāyiṟṟu. ākavē uyir meykku vaṭivum oruvāṟṟāṟ
   kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē mey taṉakku iyalpākiya akarattai nīṅki niṟpatōr
   taṉmaiyum piṟitōruyirai ēṟkun taṉmaiyum uṭaiya teṉpatūum, uyirmeykkaṭ
   pulappaṭātu iyalpākiya akaramāy niṟkun taṉmaiyum mey puḷḷipeṟ
   ṟaḻintavaḻi avaṟṟiṟkut takka uyirāyp pulappaṭṭu varuntaṉmaiyum uṭaiya
   teṉpatūum peṟṟām. uyirmey eṉpatu ummait tokai. (17)
   -----
   1 puḷḷi mutalāvatu oru ciṟu naṭṭukkōṭāyiruntu piṉpu kuttāyk kuṟukiṟṟu
       eṉapar.

   -----------
   uyirmey olikkumāṟu
   18. meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu nilaiyē.
   itu meyyum uyiruṅ kūṭiyavaḻi avaṟṟiṉ ōcai niṟkum muṟaimai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu - meyyiṉatu ōcai tōṉṟiya piṉṉatām, uyir tōṉṟu
   nilaiyē -- uyiriṉatu ōcai tōṉṟum nilai; e - ṟu.
   muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ mey muṉṉar niṟpa uyir piṉ vantu ēṟu meṉṟār.
   ammuṟaiyē ōcaiyum piṟakku meṉṟār. itaṉāṉē māttirai koḷḷuṅkāl uppum
   nīrum pōla oṉṟēyāy niṟṟalum peṟṟum. nīr uppiṉ kuṇamēyāyavāṟu pōla
   uyirum meyyiṉ kuṇamēyāy vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimai eyti niṟṟal koḷka.
   e - ṭu: ka - ṅa - ya eṉak kūṭṭamum pirivum mūvakai yōcaiyuṅ kāṇka. (18)
   ---------------
   valliṉamey
   19. valleḻut teṉpa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa.
   itu taṉimeykaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa - ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṉun taṉi meykaḷai,
   valleḻutteṉpa-valleḻutteṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar;
   e-ṟu.
   iḵtu āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi, oḻinta melleḻuttaiyum
   iṭaiyeḻuttaiyum nōkkit tām valleṉṟicaittalāṉum valleṉat talaivaḷiyāṟ
   piṟattalāṉum valleḻuttāyiṟṟu. (19)
   -------------
   melliṉamey
   20. melleḻut teṉpa ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa - ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṉun taṉimeykaḷai,
   melleḻutteṉpa--melleḻutteṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar;
   e-ṟu.
   ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. melleṉṟicaittalāṉum melleṉṟu
   mūkkiṉ vaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum melleḻuttāyiṟṟu.
   -----------
   iṭaiyiṉamey
   21. iṭaiyeḻutteṉpa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: ya ra la va ḻa ḷa -- ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉun taṉimeykaḷai,
   iṭaiyeḻutteṉpa--iṭaiyeḻutteṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar;
   e-ṟu.
   ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. iṭainikarttāy olittalāṉum
   iṭainikarttāya miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum iṭai yeḻuttāyiṟṟu.
   valliṉattu ka-ca-ta-pa eṉṉum nāṉkum melliṉattukku ña-na-ma eṉṉum mūṉṟum
   iṭaiyiṉattu ya-va eṉṉum iraṇṭum moḻikku mutaḷātal nōkki immuṟaiyē
   vaittār. ippeyarāṉē eḻutteṉṉum ōcaikaḷ uruvāyiṉa. itu cārpiṟ
   ṟōṟṟattiṟkum okkum. (21)
   -----------

                                1.5. mayakkam

   mey mayaṅkumāṟu
   22. ammū vāṟum vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ
   meymmayaṅ1 kuṭaṉilai teriyuṅ kālai.
   -------------
   1. (pāṭam) meymmayak

   itu taṉi mey piṟameyyōṭun taṉmeyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkamum uyirmey
   uyirmeyyōṭun taṉi meyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkamuṅ
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : avmūvāṟum--aṅṅaṉam mūṉṟu kūṟākap pakutta patiṉeṭṭu meyyum,
   vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ -- vaḻakkiṭattuñ ceyyuḷiṭattum eḻuttukkaḷaik kūṭṭi
   moḻippaṭuttu vaḻaṅkutal uḷatāmiṭattu, mey mayaṅkum nilai -- taṉi mey
   taṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa piṟameyyōṭun taṉ meyyōṭum mayaṅkum nilaiyum,
   uṭaṉmayaṅkum nilai -- appatiṉeṭṭum uyiruṭaṉē niṉṟu taṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa
   uyirmeyyōṭun taṉi meyyōṭum mayaṅkum nilaiyumeṉa iraṇṭām, teriyuṅkālai
   -- avai mayaṅkum moḻiyān taṉmai ārāyuṅkālattu; e-ṟu.1
   -----------
   1. ammūvāṟum......meymmayaṅkuṭaṉilai' eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟiyiruppatāl,
       meyyoṭu mey mayaṅkum uṭaṉilai
       eṉavē, taṉittu niṉṟa eḻuttuṭaṉ muṉṉiṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ tāṅ kūṭumāṟu
       kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. kaṭka eṉṟāl iṭai niṉṟa taṉimey muṉṉar niṉṟa taṉṉiṉ
       vēṟāya kakaravoṟṟōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. kākkai eṉṟāl iṭainiṉṟa kakaravoṟṟu
       muṉṉar niṉṟa taṉṉoṟṟōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu.karu eṉa īreḻuttorumoḻiyuṅ
       karutu eṉa mūveḻuttorumoḻiyum uyir mey niṉṟu taṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa
       uyirmeyyōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. tuṇaṅkai eṉa uyirmey niṉṟu taṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa
       taṉimeyyōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. kal vil eṉa uyirmey niṉṟu taṉimeyyoṭu
       mayaṅkiṟṟu.

   teriyuṅkālai eṉṟataṉāṉ uyir muṉṉar uyirmeymayakkamum uyir muṉṉart
   taṉimeym mayakkamuṅ koḷka. avai aḷai āmpal eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa.
   meymmayakkaṅkaḷuḷ taṉimey muṉṉarp piṟamey niṉṟu mayaṅkutal palavātaliṟ
   pala cūttirattāṟ kūṟit taṉ muṉṉart tāṉ vantu mayaṅkutalai oru
   cūttirattāṟ kūṟupa. avai mayaṅkuṅkāl valliṉattil ṭakaramum ṟakaramum
   melliṉamāṟum iṭaiyiṉamāṟum piṟa meyyoṭu mayaṅku meṉṟum valliṉattiṟ
   kacatapakkaḷ taṉ meyyōṭaṉṟip piṟa meyyōṭu mayaṅkāveṉṟum uyttuṇarak
   kūṟumāṟu uṇarka.
   mūvāṟum eṉṉum ummai muṟṟummai.
   iccūttiram mutalāka 'meynilai cuṭṭiṉ' (eḻu - 30) īṟāka mēṟkūṟum
   moḻimarapiṟkup poruntiya karuvi kūṟukiṉṟateṉṟuṇarka; eḻuttukkaḷ tammiṟ
   kūṭi puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatātaliṉ, (22)
   --------------
   taṉimey piṟameyyuṭaṉ mayaṅkumāṟu
   23. ṭa ṟa la ḷa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉark
   ka ca pa eṉṉum mūveḻut turiya.
   itu taṉimey piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkum mayakkam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   meymmayakkamum vēṟṟunilai meymmayakkamumē kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉavām. uyirmey
   iraṭṭai yeḻuttātaliṉ, atu meyyōṭum uyir meyyōṭum
   mayaṅkum eṉak kūṟuvatu poruntātu.
   i-ḷ: ṭaṟalaḷa eṉṉumpuḷḷimuṉṉar-moḻiyiṭai niṉṟa ṭaṟalaḷa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum
   nāṉku puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, kacapa eṉṉum mūveḻuttu uriya-kacapa eṉṟu
   kūṟappaṭum
   mūṉṟeḻuttum vantu mayaṅkutaṟku uriya: e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: kaṭka kaṭci kaṭpa eṉavuṅ kaṟka muyaṟci kaṟpa eṉavuñ celka valci
   celpa eṉavum koḷka. nīḷciṉai koḷpa eṉavun taṉimey piṟa meyyōṭu
   mayaṅkiyavāṟu kāṇka. kaṭciṟār kaṟciṟār eṉpaṉa irumoḻip puṇarcciyākaliṉ
   īṇṭaik kākā. (23)
   ------------
   24. avaṟṟuḷ,
   laḷa ḵkāṉ muṉṉar ya va vun tōṉṟum.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: avaṟṟuḷ-muṟkūṟiya nāṉkaṉuḷ, laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar-lakāra ḷakāramākiya
   puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yavavun tōṉṟum-kacapakkaḷē yaṉṟi yakara vakaraṅkaḷum
   vantu mayaṅkum; e-ṟu.
   kolyāṉai celvam veḷyāṟu kaḷvaṉ eṉa varum.
   ivaṟṟuṭ kolyāṉai eṉa viṉaittokaiyum veḷyāṟu eṉap paṇputtokaiyum
   nilaimoḻi varumoḻi ceyvataṟku iyaiyāmaiyiṉ "maruviṉ pāttiya" eṉṟu
   kūṟuvarātaliṉ ivvāciriyar ivaṟṟai oru moḻiyākak koḷva reṉṟu uṇarka.
   ikkaruttāṉē mēlum viṉait tokaiyum paṇputtokaiyum orumoḻiyākakkoṇṭu
   utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭutum,. aṉṟi ivvāciriyar nūl ceykiṉṟa kālattu viṉait
   tokaikkaṇṇum paṇputtokaikkaṇṇu maṉṟi orumoḻikkaṇṇē mayaṅkuvaṉavum
   uḷavātaliṉ, avaṟṟaikkaṇṭu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉār. avai piṉṉar iṟantaṉa veṉṟu
   oḻittu utāraṇamillaṉavaṟṟiṟku utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭāmaṟ pōtē naṉṟeṉṟu
   kūṟalumoṉṟu. (24)
   -----------
   25. ṅañaṇa namaṉa eṉumpuḷḷi muṉṉart
   tattam *icaikaḷ ottaṉa nilaiyē.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar-ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṟu
   kūṟappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, tattam icai kaḷ-tamakkiṉamāy muṉṉiṉṟa ka
   ca ṭa ta pa ṟakkaḷ, ottaṉa nilaiyē-piṉṉiṟṟaṟkup poruntiṉa mayaṅki
   niṟṟaṟkaṇ; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: kaṅkaṉ kañcaṉ kaṇṭaṉ kantaṉ kampaṉ maṉṟaṉ eṉa varum. teṅku piñcu
   vaṇṭu pantu kampu kaṉṟu eṉak kuṟṟukaramuṅ kāṭṭupa. (25)
   -------------
   26. avaṟṟuḷ,
   ṇa ṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉark
   ka ca ña pa ma ya va ēḻu muriya.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: avaṟṟuḷ-mēṟkūṟiya melloṟṟu āṟaṉuḷ, ṇa ṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉar-ṇakāra
   ṉakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ka ca ña pa ma ya va ēḻum uriya-ṭa ṟakkaḷē yaṉṟik
   ka ca ña pa ma ya va eṉṉum ēḻeḻuttum vantu mayaṅkutaṟkē uriya; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: eṇku veṇcāntu veṇñāṇ paṇpu veṇmai maṇyāṟu eṇvaṭṭu eṉavum, puṉku
   puṉcey meṉñāṇ aṉpu vaṉmai iṉyāḻ puṉvaraku eṉavum varum. eṇvaṭṭu
   viṉaittokai. eṇku puṉku peyar. (26)
   -------------
   27 ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
   yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal meypeṟ ṟaṉṟē
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar-ña na ma va eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum
   puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal mey peṟṟaṉṟē-yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal poruṇmai
   peṟṟatu; e-ṟu.
   iṅṅaṉam āciriyar cūttirañ ceytaliṉ, akkālattu orumoḻiyāka vaḻaṅkiya
   coṟkaḷ uḷaveṉpatu peṟṟām. avai ikkālattu iṟantaṉa.
   iṉi uraiyāciriyar uriñyātu porunyātu tirumyātu tevyātu eṉa irumoḻikkaṇ
   varuvaṉa utāraṇamākak kāṭṭiṉārāleṉiṉ, āciriyar oru moḻiyāmāṟu īṇṭuk
   kūṟi, ataṉkaṇ irumoḻipuṇarttaṟkup puṇariyaleṉṟu vēṟōr iyaluṅ
   kūṟi,ataṉkaṇ "meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum" (eḻu.-107) eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
   kūṟiyavaḻip piṉṉum "ukaramoṭu paṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti" (eḻu.163) eṉṟum
   piṟāṇṭum īṟukaṭōṟum eṭuttōtip puṇarppar. ātaliṉ īṇṭu irumoḻip puṇarcci
   kāṭṭiṟ kūṟiyatu kūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām1. ataṉāl avai kāṭṭutal poruntāmai
   uṇarka. (27)
   -----------
   1. mayakkam vēṟu puṇarcci vēṟātaliṉ, īṇṭuirumoḻip puṇarcci kāṭṭiṟ
       kūṟiyatukūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām" eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatu
       poruntātu.

   ----------
   28. maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ vavvun tōṉṟum
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ-muṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ makaramākiya puḷḷi muṉṉar, vavvun
   tōṉṟum-pakara yakaramē yaṉṟi vakaramum vantu mayaṅkum; e-ṟu.
   itaṟkum utāraṇam ikkālattu iṟantaṉa. aṉṟi, vaṇṇakkaṉ eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa
   kāṭṭiṉ "vakāra micaiyum makāraṅ kuṟukum" (eḻu- 330) eṉṟa viti vēṇṭāvām.
   -------------
   29. ya ra ḻa eṉṉum puḷḷimuṉṉar
   mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: ya ra ḻa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar -ya ra ḻa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟu
   puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, mutalākeḻuttum- moḻikku mutalāmeṉa mēṟkūṟum
   oṉpateḻuttukaḷum, ummaiyāṉ moḻikku mutalākāta piṟa eḻuttukaḷum
   ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum-ṅakāramum vantu mayaṅkum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: āyka ārka āḻka, āytal ārtal āḻtal, āynar ārnar āḻnar, āypavai
   ārpavai āḻpavai, vāymai nērmai kīḻmai, eycilai vārcilai vāḻcēri, teyvam
   cērvatu vāḻvatu, pāyñekiḻi nērñekiḻi vāḻñeṇṭu, ceyyāṟu pōryāṉai
   vīḻyāṉai eṉa moḻikku mutalām oṉpatum vantu mayaṅkiṉa. ceyyāṟu eṉa
   yakarattiṉ muṉṉar yakaram vantatu taṉmuṉṉart tāṉ vantatām.
   iṉi ummaiyāṟ koṇṭa moḻikku mutalākātavaṟṟiṉkaṇṇuñ cila kāṭṭutum: ōyvu
   cōrvu vāḻvu, ōyvōr cōrvōr vāḻvōr, āyñar cērñar āḻñar eṉa varum. piṟa
   eḻuttukaḷōṭu varuvaṉa uḷa vēṉum vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum nōkkik kūṟikkoḷka.
   iṉi vēyṅṅaṉam vērṅṅaṉam vēḻṅṅaṉam eṉa moḻikku mutalākāta ṅakaram
   iṭaivanta coṟkaḷ akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟu uṇarka, āciriyar
   ōtutaliṉ.itaṉai "akaramoṭu tōṉṟum" eṉap pirittōtiṉār akkālattum aritāka
   vaḻaṅkaliṉ.
   iṉi vēykaṭitu vērkaṭitu vīḻkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu
   māṇṭatu yātu valitu eṉpaṉa kāṭṭiṉ avai irumoḻiyāka nilaimoḻi varumoḻi
   ceytu mēṟpuṇarkiṉṟaṉa īṇṭaikkākā eṉa maṟukka.
   -----------
   taṉimey taṉmeyyōṭu mayaṅkumāṟu
   30 meynnilai cuṭṭi ṉellā veḻuttun
   tammuṟṟām varūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭaiyē.
   itu niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē taṉimey taṉṉoṟṟoṭu mayaṅkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ-poruṇilaimaiyaik karutiṉ, ellā
   eḻuttum-patiṉeṭṭumeyyum, tammuṉ tām varūum-tammuṉṉē tām vantu
   mayaṅkum,ra ḻa alaṅkaṭaiyē- rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷallāta iṭattu; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: kākkai eṅṅaṉam paccai maññai paṭṭai maṇṇaitattai venney appu
   ammai veyyar elli evvi kaḷḷi koṟṟi kaṉṉi eṉa varum.
   meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ eṉṟataṉāl taṉimey muṉṉar uyirmey varumeṉṟu koḷka. ellā
   meṉṟatu rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷ oḻintaṉavaṟṟait taḻuviṟṟu (30)
   -----------

                 1.6. eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟa marapukaḷ

   cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ
   31. a i u am mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu.
   itu kuṟṟeḻut teṉṟavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: a i u ammūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu - a i u eṉṟu kūṟiya ammūṉṟuñ cuṭṭeṉṉuṅ
   kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya; e-ṟu.
   ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiyatōr kuṟi, cuṭṭi aṟiyappaṭum poruḷai
   uṇarttaliṉ. taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṉ ekaram viṉāpporuḷ uṇarttutaluṅ
   koḷka.
   e-ṭu: akkoṟṟaṉ ikkoṟṟaṉ ukkoṟṟaṉ epporuḷ eṉa varum. ivai peyaraic
   cārntu tattaṅ kuṟippiṟ poruḷ ceyta iṭaiccol. iccūttiram orutalaimoḻita
   leṉṉum utti1. ituvum mēlaiccūttiramum eḻuttāntaṉmai-yaṉṟi
   moḻinilaimaippaṭṭu vēṟōr kuṟipeṟṟu niṟṟaliṉ moḻimarapiṉaic cēra
   vaittār.. (31)
   ---------
   1 orutalaimoḻital-ōr atikārattiṟ collavēṇṭiya oru poruṭku vēṟōr
       atikārattil ilakkaṇaṅkūṟi ataṉaiyē maṟṟaviṭattum peṟavaittal.
       collatikārattiṟ collavēṇṭiya cuṭṭai eḻuttatikārattiṟ kūṟi, ataiyē
       collatikārattiṟkum peṟavaittamai kāṇka.

   ------------
   viṉā veḻuttukkaḷ
   32. ā ē ō am mūṉṟum viṉā.
   itu neṭṭeḻutteṉṟavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉā-ā ē ō eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa ammūṉṟum viṉā eṉṉuṅ
   kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya; e-ṟu.
   ituvumāṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi., viṉāpporuḷ uṇarttaliṉ.
   e-ṭu: uṇkā uṇkē uṇkō eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuḷ ākāram ikkālattu viṉāvāy varuta
   laritu. nīyē nīyō eṉpatu ikkālattu varum. ivaṟṟuḷ ē ō eṉpaṉa
   iṭaiccollōttiluḷḷuṅ kūṟiṉār, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ tarum poruṭṭokaipaṟṟi. itu
   moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭitta leṉṉum
   uttikku2 iṉamām, yakaraākāramum viṉāvāy varutaliṉ.
   ------
   2 moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa av*vayiṉa moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu
       muṭittal-eṭuttukkūṟiya poruḷilēyē ataṟkiṉamāṉatum eṭuttuk
       kūṟātatumāṉa poruḷaippeṟavaittal. "ā" viṉāvil "yā" viṉāvaiyum "ē"
       viṉāvil "e" viṉāvaiyum aṭakkiyatu kāṇka.

   ---------
   icainūṉ muṟaiyil eḻuttolikaḷ
   33. aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟicai nīṭalum
   uḷaveṉa moḻipa icaiyoṭu civaṇiya
   narampiṉ maṟaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
   itu piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟaleṉṉum uttipaṟṟi icainūṟku varuvatōr ilakkaṇamāmāṟu
   kūṟi, avvilakkaṇam innūṟkuṅ koḷkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: aḷapiṟantu uyirttalum oṟṟicai nīṭalum naram piṉ maṟaiya eṉmaṉār
   pulavar-muṟkūṟiya uyirum uyirmeyyum māttiraiyai iṟantolittalum
   oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ arai māttiraiyiṉ nīṇṭolittalum yāḻnūliṭattaṉa eṉṟu
   kūṟuvar pulavar, icaiyoṭu civaṇiya uḷaveṉ moḻipa- aṅṅaṉam aḷapiṟantum
   nīṇṭum icaittal ōcaiyōṭu poruntiya nālvakaic ceyyuṭkaḷukkum uḷaveṉṟu
   kūṟuvar āciriyar; e-ṟu.
   eḻuttukkaḷ muṟkūṟiya māttiraiyai iṟantolikkumāṟu kaṇṭu, aḵtu
   iṟantolikkum iṭaṅ kūṟiṉār. eḻuttuñ collum poruḷuṅ kiṭakkum iṭañ
   ceyyuḷiṭamātaliṉ . atu mikkolittalaic ceyyuḷiyaliṉkaṇ "māttirai
   yeḻuttiya lacaivakai yeṉāa" (cey.-1) eṉa irupattāṟu uṟuppiṟkuñ
   ciṟappuṟuppāka muṟkūṟip, piṉṉar,
   "māttirai yaḷavu meḻuttiyal vakaiyu
   mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa veṉmaṉār pulavar" (cey.-2)
   eṉa iccūttirattōṭu māṭṭeṟintu, piṉṉum
   "eḻuttaḷa veñciṉuñ cīrnilai tāṉē
   kuṉṟalu mikutalu milleṉa moḻipa" (cey-3)
   eṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉār. itu etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum uttiyuṅ kūṟiṟṟu.
   e-ṭu: "varuvarkol vayaṅkiḻāay valippalyāṉ kēeḷiṉi" (kali-11) eṉpuḻi
   ḻakara ākāramum kakara ēkāramum māttirai iṟan tolittavāṟu uṇarka.
   "piṭiyūṭṭip piṉṉuṇṇuṅṅ kaḷiṟeṉavum uraittaṉarē" (kali-11) eṉpuḻi
   ṅakaravoṟṟu aḷaviṟantavāṟu kāṇka. oḻinta mūvakaic ceyyuṭkum ivvāṟē
   tattamakkuriya pā eṉṉum uṟuppiṉai naṭātti aḷavu mikumāṟu kāṇka.
   civaṇiya eṉpatu toḻiṟpeyar.1 icaiyoṭu civaṇiya eṉavē ceyyuḷātal peṟṟām.
   narampu eṉṟatu ākupeyarāy yāḻiṉai uṇarttiṟṟu. maṟai eṉṟatu nūlai.
   moḻipa eṉṟum eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉṟum irukāṟ kūṟiyavataṉāl, iṅṅaṉam poruḷ
   kūṟalē āciriyarkkuk karuttāyiṟṟu. eṉṉai? ceyyuḷiyaluṭ kūṟiya "māttirai
   yaḷavum" eṉṉuñ cūttirattil "mēṟkiḷantaṉṉa" (cey-2) eṉṉum māṭṭēṟṟiṟku
   ivvōttiṉuḷ vēṟōr cūttiram iṉmaiyiṉ. ivvilakkaṇaṅ kūṟākkāṟ ceyyuṭkup pā
   veṉṉum uṟuppu nikaḻātu, avai uraicceyyuṭ pōla niṟṟaliṉ ivvilakkaṇaṅ
   kūṟavē vēṇṭumeṉṟu uṇarka.
   ----------
   1 toḻiṟpeyar eṉṟatu viṉaiyālaṇaiyum peyarai.

   "cūttirat tupporu ḷaṉṟiyum yāppuṟa
   iṉṟi yamaiyā tiyaipavai yellām
   oṉṟa uraippa turaiyeṉap paṭumē" (marapiyal-103)
   eṉṉum marapiyaṟ cūttirattāṉē ivvāṟē cūttiraṅkaḷai nalintu
   poruḷuraippaṉa vellāṅ koḷka.
   nūṉmarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.
   --------------

                               2. moḻimarapu
       (moḻikkaṇ niṉṟa eḻuttiṉ ilakkaṇam uṇarttuvatu)
           2.1. cārpeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikaḷiṟ payilumāṟu

   kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum
   34. kuṟṟiya likaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum
   yāveṉ ciṉaimicai yuraiyacaik kiḷavik
   kāvayiṉ varūum makaram ūrntē.
   eṉpatu cūttiram. mēl eḻuttu uṇarttiya piṉṉar, avai tammuḷ toṭaṟumāṟum
   uṇartti avveḻuttāṉām moḻiyatu marapu uṇarttu kiṉṟamaiyiṉ, ivvōttu
   moḻimarapeṉak kāraṇap peyarttāyiṟṟu. iccūttiram muṉṉarc cārntu
   varumeṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuṭ kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uraiyacaik kiḷavikku varūum-tāṉ kūṟum poruḷaik kōṭaṟku oruvaṉai
   etirmuka mākkuñ colliṟkup poruntavarum, āvayiṉ-am miyā eṉṉum iṭaic
   collaic collumiṭattu, yāveṉ ciṉaimicai makaram ūrntu- yāveṉṉum uṟuppiṉ
   mēlatāy mutalāy niṉṟa makaravoṟṟiṉai yēṟi, kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟal
   vēṇṭum-kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟalai virumpum āciriyaṉ; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: kēṇmiyā ceṉmiyā eṉa varum.kēḷ eṉṟatu uraiyacaik kiḷavi; ataṉaic
   cārntu taṉakku iyalpiṉṟi niṉṟatu miyā eṉṉum iṭaiccol. avviṭaiccol
   mutalum ataṉiṟ piriyum yā ataṟku uṟuppumām eṉṟu karuti yāveṉ ciṉai
   eṉṟār. miyā iṭam; makaram paṟṟukkōṭu.; yāvum ikaram arai
   māttiraiyātaṟkuc cārpu. ivviṭaiccol taṉittu niṟṟal āṟṟāmaiyiṟ kēḷ
   eṉpataṉōṭu cārntu oru collāyē niṉṟuḻi iṭainiṉṟa ikaram orumoḻi yiṭattuk
   kuṟṟiyalikaramāy varutalāṉum āṇṭu uṇarttaṟkuc ciṟappiṉmaiyāṉum īṇṭup
   pōttantu kūṟiṉār. ūrnteṉavē kuṟṟiya likaramum uyireṉpatu peṟṟām,
   uyirkkallatu ēṟutaliṉmaiyiṉ. (1)
   -----------
   35. puṇariyal nilaiyiṭaik kuṟukalum urittē
   uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum.
   itu kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumeṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: puṇariyal nilaiyiṭaik kuṟukalum urittē- akkuṟṟiyalikaram
   puṇariyaluḷ orumoḻikkaṇ ṇaṉṟi irumoḻitammiṟ puṇartaliyaṉṟa
   nilaimaikkaṇṇuṅ kuṟukutalurittu, uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum-ataṟku
   iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarak kūṟattoṭaṅkiṉ avai kuṟṟiyalukarap
   puṇariyaluḷḷē kūṟappaṭum; e-ṟu.
   kuṟukalum eṉṉumiṭattu ummaiyai nilaiyiṭaiyum eṉa māṟik kūṭṭuka.
   "yakaram varuvaḻi" (eḻu-410) eṉṉuñ cūttirattu yakaram iṭam, ukarañ
   cārnta valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu.
   e-ṭu: nākiyātu varakiyātu teḷkiyātu eḵkiyātu kokkiyātu kuraṅkiyātu eṉa
   varum. itu moḻivā meṉṉum utti. (2)
   ------------
   taṉimoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum
   36. neṭṭeḻut timparun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟuṅ
   kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē.
   iḵtu orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: kuṟṟiyalukaram vallāṟu ūrntē-kuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttukkaḷ
   āṟiṉaiyum ūrntu, neṭṭeḻuttimparun toṭarmoḻi īṟṟum-neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum
   aivakait toṭarmoḻiyiṉ iṟutiyilum niṟṟal vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ; e-ṟu.
   neṭṭeḻuttiṉatu piṉ toṭarmoḻiyiṉatu īṟeṉa nilatta takalam pōla1
   oṉṟiyaṟkiḻamaippaṭṭu niṉṟatu, ammoḻiyiṟ ṟīrntu kuṟṟiyalukaram
   nillāmaiyiṉ, vallāṟu paṇputtokai. muṟṟummai tokkuniṉṟatu.
   atikāramuṟaimai eṉṉum uttiyāṉ niṟṟal vēṇṭu meṉpatu varuvikka,
   e-ṭu: nāku varaku teḷḷu eḵku kokku kuraṅku eṉa varum. ivvāṟuvakaiyum
   iṭam; valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu. eṉavē, moḻikku īṟātalum peṟṟām.
   perumuracu tirumuracu eṉpaṉa irumoḻikkaṇ vanta muṟṟukaram. pā*cu iṅku
   ētu eṉṉum muṟṟukaraṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻic citaivu; tarukku aṇukku eṉpaṉa
   viṉaikkaṇ vanta muṟṟukaram. kuṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉar vanta uyirēṟi muṭiya
   araimāttiraiyāy niṟṟalum muṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉarvanta uyirēṟi
   muṭiyāmaiyun tammuḷ vēṟṟumai2. (3)
   -------
   1. nilatta takalam pōla-nilattiṉiṉṟum pirikkappaṭāta akalampōla.
       2. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar iṅkukkūṟiya kuṟṟiyalukara ilakkaṇappaṭi.
       perumuracu tirumuracu tarukku aṇukku eṉpaṉavum ikkālattuk
       kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷām .

   -----------
   puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram taṉmāttiraiyiṟ kuṟukal
   37. iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟukum iṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
   kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa.
   itu kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻikkaṇ taṉ araimāttiraiyiṟ kuṟukivarum
   eṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟukum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē-avvukaram orumoḻiyuḷaṉṟip
   puṇarmoḻi yiṭaippaṭiṉ taṉ arai māttiraiyiṉuṅ kuṟukum iṭaṉum uṇṭu.
   kaṭappāṭu aṟinta puṇariyalāṉa-ataṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum yāṇṭup
   peṟuvateṉiṉ, ataṉ puṇarcci muṟaimai aṟiyuṅ kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ;
   e-ṟu.
   "valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi" (eḻu-406) eṉpataṉuḷ valleḻuttut toṭarmoḻiyum
   valleḻuttu varum vaḻiyum iṭam; īṟṟu valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu.
   e-ṭu: cekkukkaṇai cukkukkōṭu eṉa varum. ivai araimāt tiraiyiṟ
   kuṟukiyavāṟu ēṉaiyavaṟṟōṭu paṭuttu uṇarka. iṭaṉu meṉavē itu
   ciṟupāṉmaiyāyiṟṟu. (4)
   ----------
   āytam taṉimoḻiyuḷ varumāṟu
   38. kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar āytap puḷḷi
   uyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micaittē.
   itu niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē āytam orumoḻiyuḷ varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: āytappuḷḷi-āytamākiya oṟṟu, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar uyiroṭa puṇarnta
   vallā ṟaṉ micaittu-kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉatāy uyirōṭukūṭiya valleḻuttāṟiṉ
   mēliṭatta tāy varum; e-ṟu.
   vallāṟaṉ micaittu eṉṟataṉāṉum īṇṭup puḷḷi eṉṟataṉāṉum "āytat toṭarmoḻi"
   (eḻu-406) eṉa mēṟkūṟutalāṉum uyireṉṟatu īṇṭup perumpāṉmaiyuṅ
   kuṟṟukaramēyām. ciṟupāṉmai ēṉai uyirkaḷaiyuṅ koḷka.
   e-ṭu: eḵku kaḵcu kaḵṭu kaḵtu kaḵpu kaḵṟu aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu eṉavarum,
   kaḵṟītu muḵṭī*tu eṉpaṉavaṟṟai meypiṟitākiya puṇarcci eṉṟataṉāṉum īṇṭup
   puḷḷi eṉṟataṉāṉum āytamum meyyāyiṟṟu. aḵkāmai veḵkāmai aḵki veḵki aḵkam
   eṉap piṟavuyirkaḷōṭum vantatu. kaḵciyā teṉat tirintatuvuṅ
   kuṟṟiyalukarattōṭu puṇarntatām. (5)
   -----------
   āytam puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷum varumāṟu
   39. īṟiyal maruṅkiṉum icaimai tōṉṟum
   iḵtu avvāytam puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: īṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉum-nilaimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutaloṭu puṇarntu
   naṭakkum iṭattum, icaimai tōṉṟum- ataṉ araimāttiraiyē icaikkun taṉmai
   tōṉṟum; e-ṟu:
   e-ṭu: kaḵṟītu muḵṭītu eṉa varum. ivvīṟu iyalumāṟu puḷḷi mayaṅkiṭaluṭ
   peṟutum. īṇṭum iṭam kuṟṟeḻuttumēl varum valleḻuttu.
   -------------
   āytattiṉ iyalpu
   40. uruviṉum icaiyiṉum arukit tōṉṟum
   moḻikkuṟip pellām eḻuttiṉ iyalā
   āytam aḵkāk kālai yāṉa.
   iḵtu etiratupōṟṟa leṉṉum uttiyāṟ ceyyuḷiyalai nōkki āytattiṟku
   eytiyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uruviṉum icaiyiṉum arukittōṉṟuṅ kuṟippumoḻiyum-niṟattiṉkaṇṇum
   ōcaiyiṉkaṇṇuñ ciṟu pāṉmai āytan tōṉṟum poruḷ kuṟittalaiyuṭaiya collum,
   ellāmoḻiyum-avai yoḻinta ellā moḻikaḷum, eḻuttiṉiyalā-oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷpōla
   arai māttiraiyiṉkaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmai mikkum naṭantu, āytam aḵkāk
   kālaiyāṉa-āytañ curuṅkāta iṭattāṉa coṟkaḷām1; e-ṟu.
   eṉavē, īṇṭu ārāycciyiṉṟēṉañ ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟum "oṟṟaḷa peṭuppiṉum
   aṟṟeṉamoḻipa" (ceyyuḷiyal-18) eṉṉuñ cūttirattuk "kaṇṭaṇṇeṉak
   kaṇṭuṅkēṭṭum" (malaipaṭu-352) eṉpuḻik kaṇṇeṉpatu cīrnilai eytiṉāṟpōlak,
   "kaḵḵṟeṉṉuṅ kallatarattam" eṉa niṟattiṉkaṇṇum "cuḵḵṟeṉṉun taṇṭōṭṭup
   peṇṇai" eṉa icaiyiṉkaṇṇum vanta āytam oru māttairai peṟṟuc cīrnilai
   yeytuṅkāl, āṇṭup peṟukiṉṟa oru māttiraikku īṇṭu etiratupōṟṟi viti
   kūṟiṉār. āytam atikārappaṭṭamai kaṇṭu. "eḵḵ kilaṅkiya kaiyarā
   yiṉṉuyir-veḵḵ kuvārkkillai vīṭu" eṉṟu ēṉaiyiṭattum vantaṉa.
   oṟṟaḷapeṭukkumāṟu ivvatikārattuk kūṟiṟṟilar, aḵtu uyiraḷapeṭai pōlac
   cīrnilai yeytalum acainilaiyān taṉmaiyu muṭaiyavāyc ceyyuṭkē varutaliṉ.
   itaṉāṉē oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyum oru māttirai peṟu meṉpatu peṟṟām.
   eḻuttiṉeṉṟa iṉ uvamapporuḷ. iyalāveṉṟatu ceyyāveṉṉum viṉaiyeccam.
   ivvāṟaṉṟi ik kuṟippuccoṟkaḷ āytam iraṇṭiṭṭu eḻutappaṭāveṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṟ
   ceyyuḷiyalōṭu māṟupaṭṭu māṟukoḷak kūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟan taṅku meṉṟu uṇarka.
   (7)
   -----------
   1. "moḻikkuṟippellām" eṉpataṟku "kuṟippumoḻiyellām eṉa orē
       poruḷ koḷḷātu" kuṟippu moḻiyum "ellāmoḻiyum" eṉa iru
       poruḷ koḷvatu attuṇaic ciṟantataṉṟu.

   -----------

                              2.2. aḷapeṭai

   aḷapeṭaiyiṉ nilai
   41. kuṉṟicai moḻivayiṉ niṉṟicai niṟaikkum
   neṭṭeḻut timpar ottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.
   iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟa leṉṉum utti paṟṟic ceyyuḷiyalai nōkki "nīṭṭam
   vēṇṭiṉ" (eḻu-6) eṉa muṟkūṟiya aḷapeṭaiyāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: kuṉṟicai moḻivayiṉ niṉṟu icai niṟaikkum- aḷapeṭuttuk kūṟākkāṟ
   kuṉṟuvatāṉa ōcaiyaiyuṭaiya av vaḷapeṭaic coṟkaṇṇē niṉṟu avvōcaiyai
   niṟaikkum, avai yāvaiyō veṉiṉ, neṭṭeḻuttimpar otta
   kuṟṟeḻuttē-neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṉṉākat tamakku iṉamotta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ;
   e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: āa īi ūu ēe ōo eṉa varum. kuṉṟicai moḻi eṉṟataṟku icaikuṉṟu moḻi
   eṉṟumām. iṉamottalāvatu piṟappum puṇarcciyum ōcaiyum vaṭivum ottal.
   īṇṭu moḻi yeṉṟatu "aḷa peṭai yacainilai" (ceyyuḷiyal-17) eṉṉuñ
   ceyyuḷiyal cūttirattu eṭṭu iyaṟcīriṉpāṟ paṭukiṉṟa eṇvakai aḷapeṭaic
   coṟkaḷaiyum avai āa kaṭāa āaḻi paṭāakai āaṅku āavatu pukāarttu virā
   ayatu eṉpaṉavām. kaṭṭaḷai1koḷḷā āciriyar ivaṟṟait taṉinilai mutaṉilai
   iṭainilai iṟutinilai yeṉṟum aṭakkupa. iṉi moḻi yeṉṟataṟkut taṉinilai
   ēḻaṉaiyumē koḷḷiṉ, oḻinta iyaṟcīrppāṟpaṭum aḷapeṭai kōṭaṟku iṭamiṉmai
   uṇarka. (8)
   ---------
   1. kaṭṭaḷai-kaṭṭaḷaiyaṭi; atāvatu eḻutteṇṇi vakukkum aṭi.

   ------------
   aikāra aukāra aḷapeṭai
   42. ai au eṉṉum āyī reḻuttiṟ
   kikara ukaram icainiṟai vākum.
   iḵtu ottakuṟṟeḻuttu illātaṉa aḷapeṭukkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   ituvum etiratu pōṟṟal.
   i-ḷ : ai au eṉṉum āyīreḻuttiṟku-tamakku iṉamillāta aikāra aukārameṉṟu
   kūṟappaṭum avviraṇṭeḻuttiṟku ikara ukaram icainiṟai vākum-īkāra
   ūkāraṅkaṭku iṉamākiya ikara ukaraṅkaḷaic cārttikkūṟa, avai akkuṉṟicai
   moḻikkaṇ niṉṟu ōcaiyai niṟaippaṉavām; e-ṟu.
   aii auu eṉa niraṉiṟaiyākak koḷka. ivaṟṟai muṟkūṟiya iyaṟ cīreṭṭiṟkum
   ēṟpaṉavaṟṟōṭu utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭik koḷka.
   ittuṇaiyum nūṉmarapiṉ oḻipu.
   ------------

                 2.3. eḻuttukkaḷ toṭarntu moḻiyātal

   ōreḻut torumoḻi
   43. neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōreḻut torumoḻi
   iḵtu ōreḻuttorumoḻi uṇarttutal nutaliyavaṟṟuḷ neṭṭeḻuttāṉām moḻiyākkaṅ
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē-neṭṭeḻuttākiya uyirkaḷēḻum,
   ōreḻuttorumoḻi-ōreḻuttāṉākum oru moḻiyām e-ṟu.
   muṟṟummai tokuttu īṟṟacai yēkāram virittār.
   e-ṭu: ā ī ū ē ai ō eṉa varum. aukāram uyirmeykkaṇṇallatu varātu, ū
   eṉpatu tacai. iḵtu uyirkkum uyirmeykkum viti. kā tī pū cē tai kō kau
   eṉa varum ivai tammai yuṇaraniṉṟa vaḻi eḻuttām. iṭainiṉṟu
   1poruḷuṇarttiyavaḻic collām. neṭṭeḻut tēṟiya mey neṭṭeḻuttāyuṅ kuṟṟeḻut
   tēṟiya mey kuṟṟeḻuttāyum niṟṟalēyaṉṟi meykku neṭumaiyuṅ kuṟumaiyum
   iṉmai uṇarka. (10)
   ---------
   kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ iyalpu.
   44. kuṟṟeḻut taintum moḻiniṟai pilavē.
   itu kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum moḻiyākā. avaṟṟuṭ cila moḻiyākumeṉpatu
   uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum-kuṟṟeḻuttākiya uyiraintum, moḻiniṟaipu
   ilavē-tāmē niṟaintu niṉṟu moḻiyātal ila; cila meyyōṭukūṭi niṟaintu
   niṉṟu moḻiyām; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: tu ko eṉa varum.ivai uyirmeykkaṇṇallatu vārāmaiyāṉum,
   uyirkkaṇṇum, ēṉai akaramum e*karamum, akkoṟṟaṉ ep poruḷ eṉat taṉittu
   niṉṟu uṇarttalāṟṟātu iṭaic collāyp peyaraiccārntu niṉṟu cuṭṭupporuḷum
   viṉāpporuḷum uṇarttutalāṉum "niṟaipila" veṉṟār. muṟṟummai īṇṭu
   eccappaṭṭu2 niṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka. (11)
   -------
   1. iṭainiṟṟal-neṭṭeḻuttukaḷ tammai yuṇartti eḻuttātaṟkum poruḷai
       yuṇarttic collātaṟkum iṭaiyāka niṟṟal.
       2 īṇṭu muṟṟummaikku muṟṟupporuḷ koḷḷiṉum kuṟṟamiṉṟu.

   ------------
   moḻiyiṉpeyar muṟai tokai
   45. ōreḻut torumoḻi īreḻut torumoḻi
   iraṇṭiṟan ticaikkun toṭarmoḻi uḷappaṭa
   mūṉṟē moḻinilai tōṉṟiya neṟiyē.
   itu muṉṉar meymmayakkam uṭaṉilaimayakkaṅ kūṟalāṉum īṇṭu "neṭṭeḻuttēḻē"
   (eḻu-43) eṉpataṉālum eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu kūṟiṉār, ammoḻikku
   iccūttirattāṟ peyarum muṟaiyun tokaiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟār.
   i-ḷ: ōreḻut torumoḻi īreḻut torumoḻi0 iraṇṭiṟantu icaikkun toṭarmoḻi
   uḷappaṭa-ōreḻuttāṉākum orumoḻiyum iraṇṭeḻuttāṉākum orumoḻiyum iraṇṭaṉai
   iṟantu palavāṟṟāṉ icaikkun toṭarmoḻiyuṭaṉēkūṭa, moḻi nilai
   mūṉṟē-moḻikaḷiṉ nilaimai mūṉṟēyām, tōṉṟiya neṟiyē-avai tōṉṟiya vaḻakku
   neṟikkaṇ; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: ā kā nā ōreḻuttorumoḻi, maṇi varaku koṟṟaṉ īreḻuttorumoḻi, kuravu
   arava mūveḻut torumoḻei. kaṇaviri nāleḻuttorumoḻi akattiyaṉār
   aiyeḻuttorumoḻi tirucciṟṟam palam āṟeḻuttorumoḻi perumpaṟṟappuliyūr
   ēḻeḻuttorumoḻi1.
   ----------
   1 iṅku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urainaṭaikkēṟpak koḷḷātu ceyyuḷiyaṟkēṟpak koṇṭu
       coṟkaḷaik kuṟṟukaramum meyyum nīkki eḻutteṇṇuvatu poruntātu.

   ōreḻuttorumoḻiyan toṭarmoḻiyum eṉṉātu īreḻut toru moḻiyum ōtiṉār, cila
   pala eṉṉun tamiḻ vaḻakku nōkki.
   āciriyar oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum eḻutteṉṟu koṇṭaṉarātaliṉ mā kā eṉa niṉṟa
   coṟkaḷ māl kāl eṉa oṟṟaṭuttuḻi oṟṟiṉāṉ vēṟu poruḷtantu niṟṟaliṉ ivaṟṟai
   īreḻut torumoḻi yeṉṟum nāku varaku eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṟ
   kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ colloṭu kūṭipporuḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ ivaṟṟai īreḻuttorumoḻi
   mūveḻut torumoḻi yeṉṟuṅ kōṭumeṉpārkku, āciriyar poruḷaik karutātu
   māttirai kuṟaintamaipaṟṟi "uyiri leḻuttu meṇṇappaṭāa" (ceyyuḷiyal-34)
   "kuṟilē neṭilē kuṟiliṇai" (ceyyuḷiyal-3) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷāl
   ivaṟṟai eḻutteṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟā eṉṟu vilakkuvarātaliṉ, avaṟṟāl
   īṇṭu īreḻuttorumoḻiyum mūveḻut torumoḻiyuṅ koḷḷiṉ, māṟukoḷakkūṟal eṉṉuṅ
   kuṟṟan taṅkumeṉṟu maṟukka.
   iṉi "neṭṭeḻut tēḻē yōreḻut torumoḻi" (eḻu-43) "kuṟṟeḻut taintu
   moḻiniṟai pilavē" (eḻu-44) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ meykkuk kuṟumai neṭumai
   yiṉmaiyāṉ uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya neṭiluṅ kuṟilumē moḻiyāmeṉṟu kūṟi,
   mīṭṭum ataṉaiyē iccūttirattāṉ ōreḻuttorumoḻi yeṉṟeṭuttu ataṉōṭē
   īreḻuttaiyum iraṇṭiṟantataṉaiyuṅ kūṭṭi moḻiyākak kōṭaliṉ, oṟṟiṉaik
   kūṭṭi eḻuttākak kōṭal āciriyarkkuk karuttaṉmai yuṇarka. aṉṟiyum
   "moḻippaṭut ticaippiṉum" (eḻu-53) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟukiṉṟavāṟṟāṉum
   uṇarka. "akaramutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy muppaḵteṉpa" (eḻu-1) eṉa oṟṟiṉaiyum
   eḻutteṉṟatu eḻuttiṉ taṉmai kūṟiṟṟu. īṇṭu moḻiyān taṉmai kūṟiṟṟu.
   -----------

                       2.4. eḻuttukkaḷiṉ iyakkam

   taṉimey iyakkam
   46. meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum
   itu taṉimeykaḷai akaram iyakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: meyyiṉiyakkam-taṉimeykaḷiṉatu naṭappu, akaramoṭu
   civaṇum-akarattōṭu porunti naṭakkum; e-ṟu.
   eṉavē oruvaṉ taṉimeykaḷai nāvāṟ karuttupporuḷkaḷākiya uruvāka iyakkum
   iyakkamum mūvakaiyāṟ kāṭcipporuḷākiya vaṭivāka iyakkum iyakkamum
   akarattōṭu porunti naṭakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
   e-ṭu: "valleḻut teṉpa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa" (eḻu-19) "kakāra ṅakāra mutaṉā
   vaṇṇam" (eḻu-89) eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa nāvāl iyakkiyavāṟukāṇka. eḻutik
   kāṭṭumiṭattuk kakaram mutaliyaṉa uyirpeṟṟu niṉṟavaṭivāka eḻutip piṉṉart
   taṉimeyyākkutaṟkup puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭukiṉṟa vāṟṟāṉ vaṭivai iyakkumiṭattum
   akaraṅ kalantu niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.
   iṅṅaṉam meykkaṇ akaraṅ kalantu niṟkumāṟu kūṟiṉāṟpōlap
   patiṉōruyirk-kaṇṇum akaraṅ kalantu niṟkumeṉpatu āciriyar kūṟārāyiṉār,
   annilaimai tamakkē pulappapaṭutalāṉum piṟarkku ivvāṟu uṇarttutal
   aritākalāṉu meṉṟuuṇarka. iṟaivaṉ iyaṅkutiṉaikkaṇṇum nilait tiṇaikkaṇṇum
   piṟavaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum avaṟṟiṉ taṉmaiyāy niṟkumāṟu ellārkkumoppa muṭintāṟ
   pōla akaramum uyirkkaṇṇun taṉimeykkaṇṇuṅ kalantu avaṟṟiṉ taṉmaiyāyē
   niṟku meṉpatu cāṉṟōrkkellām oppamuṭintatu. "akaramutal" eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷāṉ
   akaramākiya mutalaiyuṭaiya eḻuttukkaḷellām atupōla iṟaivaṉākiya
   mutalaiyuṭaittu ulakameṉa vaḷḷuvaṉār uvamai kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉuṅ, kaṇṇaṉ
   eḻuttukkaḷil akaramākiṉṟēṉ yāṉē yeṉak kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum piṟanūlkaḷāṉum
   uṇarka.
   itaṉāṉ uṇmaittaṉmaiyuñ ciṟitu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. itaṉai nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟātu
   īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār. "valleḻut teṉpa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa" (eḻu-19) eṉṟa iṭattut
   tāṉ iṭainiṉṟu oṉṟeṉpatōr poruḷai uṇartti moḻiyāntaṉmai eyti niṟṟaliṉ;,.
   (13)
   -----------
   uyirmey eṉṉum peyar
   47. tammiyal kiḷappiṉ ellā eḻuttum
   meynnilai mayakkam māṉa millai.
   itu muṉṉar meykkaṇ uyir niṉṟavāṟu kūṟi avvuyir meykkaṇ ēṟi uyirmeyyāy
   niṉṟakālattu ammeyyāṟ peyar peṟumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ellā eḻuttum-paṉṉīruyirum meynnilai tam iyal mayakkaṅ
   kiḷappiṉ-meyyiṉ taṉmaiyilē tammaṭaiya taṉmai mayaṅkiṟṟākap peyarkūṟiṉ,
   māṉamillai-kuṟṟamillai; e-ṟu.
   meyyayiṉ taṉmaiyāvatu vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimai, tammiyalāvatu uyirttaṉmai.
   eṉṟatu valleḻuttu melleḻattu iṭaiyeḻutteṉa uyirmeykkum peyariṭṭāḷutal
   kūṟiṟṟu. avai "valleḻuttiyaiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikumē" (eḻu-19) eṉavum
   "melleḻuttiyaiyiṉ iṟutiyō ṭuṟaḻum" (eḻu-20) eṉavum "iṭaiyeḻut teṉpa ya
   ra la va ḻa ḷa" (eḻu-21) eṉavum, piṟāṇṭum āḷpa. eḻuttai vaṉmai meṉmai
   iṭaimai yeṉa vicēṭitta ciṟappāṉ ippeyar kūṟiṉār.
   iḵtaṉṟip patiṉeṭṭu meyyun taṉmai kūṟumiṭattu meymmayakkaṅkūṟiya
   vakaiyāṉaṉṟi vēṇṭiyavāṟu mayaṅku meṉṟu kūṟi "avaṟṟuḷ laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar
   (eḻu-24) eṉpataṉaik kāṭṭil, aḵtu iru moḻik kaṇṇateṉa maṟukka. (14)
   ----------
   īroṟṟuṭaṉilai
   48. ya ra ḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟum oṟṟak
   ka ca ta pa ṅa ña na ma īroṟ ṟākum.
   iḵtu īroṟṟuṭaṉilaiyāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ya ra ḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟum oṟṟa-ya ra ḻa veṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟu
   puḷḷiyum oṟṟāy niṟpa, ka ca ta pa ṅa ña na ma īroṟṟākum. -ka ca ta
   pakkaḷum ṅa ña na makkaḷum vantu īroṟṟākum-ka ca ta pakkaḷum ṅa ña na
   makkaḷum vantu īroṟṟāy niṟkum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: vēykka vāycci pāyttal vāyppu eṉavum pīrkku nērcci vārttal ārppu
   eṉavum, vāḻkkai tāḻcci tāḻttal tāḻppu eṉavum, kāyṅkaṉi tēyñcatu
   cāyntaṉam kāympuṟam eṉavum, nērṅkal nērñcilai nērntilai nērmpuṟam
   eṉavum varum. ḻakārattiṟku vāḻntaṉam eṉa ikkālattu nakaravoṟṟu varum.
   ēṉaiya mūṉṟum ikkālattu vaḻaṅku meṉiṉ uṇarka.
   iṉit tāḻṅkulai tāḻñciṉai tāḻntiraḷ vīḻumpaṭai eṉa akkālattu
   vaḻaṅkumeṉṟu ittokaic coṟkaḷ kāṭṭalum oṉṟu. urai yāciriyarum
   irumoḻikkaṭ kāṭṭiyavaṟṟiṟku avvīṟukaṭōṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷ piṉṉar
   vēṇṭāmai uṇarka. iḵtu īroṟṟuṭaṉilaiyātaliṉ īṇṭu vaittār.
   iṉi neṭiṟkīḻē yaṉṟip pala veḻuttun toṭarntu niṉṟataṉ piṉṉum īroṟṟu
   varutal koḷka. avai vēntarkku aṉṉāykku eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavām.
   ----------
   kuṟiṟkī ḻoṟṟākā meykaḷ
   49. avaṟṟuḷ,
   rakāra ḻakāraṅ kuṟṟoṟ1 ṟākā.
   iḵtu eytiyatu oru maruṅku maṟuttal kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   īṇṭuk kuṟṟu eṉṟatu taṉikkuṟilai.
   i – ḷ: avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ, rakāra ḻakāram- rakāramum
   ḻakāramum, kuṟṟoṟṟākā - kuṟiṟkīḻ oṟṟākā, neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟām, kuṟiṟkīḻ
   uyirmeyyām; e – ṟu.
   kīḻeṉṉum urupu tokuttuk kūṟiṉār. ākātaṉavaṟṟiṟku utāraṇamiṉṟu.
   e – ṭu: kār vīḻ eṉa neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟāy vantaṉa. karu maḻu eṉak kuṟiṟkīḻ
   uyirmeyyāy vantaṉa. ivaṟṟai vilakkavē, yakaram poy eṉavum nōy eṉavum
   iraṇṭiṭattum oṟṟāy varutal peṟṟām. pukar pukaḻ pulavar eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavō
   veṉiṉ, moḻikku mutalām eḻuttiṉaic colvaṉavaṟṟiṟkē īṇṭu ārāycciyātalāl
   avai vēṇṭiyavāṟē varumeṉṟu uṇarka. aṉṟiyuṅ kuṟṟoṟṟeṉṟē cūttirañ
   ceytaliṟ kuṟiliṇai yoṟṟiṉaik kāṭṭik kaṭāvalākāmai uṇarka. itu
   varaiyaṟaiyiṉṟi uyirmeyyōṭu taṉimey mayaṅkuvaṉavaṟṟiṟ cila voṟṟiṟku
   varaiyaṟai īṇṭuk kūṟiyatu. (16)
   ------------
   toṭar moḻi iyalpu
   50. kuṟumaiyum neṭumaiyum aḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ
   toṭarmoḻi yellām neṭṭeḻut tiyala.
   iḵtu 'aḷamiṟan tuyirttalum' (eḻu - 33.) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyalai nōkkiya
   nūṉmarapiṟ cūttirattiṟkup puṟaṉaṭaiyāy ataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatōr aiyam
   akaṟṟukiṉṟatu; eṉṉai? uyirum uyirmeyyum aḷapiṟantu icaikkuṅkāṟ kuṟilō
   neṭilō icaippateṉa māṇākkarkku nikaḻvatōr aiyam aṟuttaliṉ.
   i – ḷ : kuṟumaiyum neṭumaiyum - eḻuttukkaḷatu kuṟiya taṉmaiyum neṭiya
   taṉmaiyum, aḷaviṟkōṭaliṉ - māttirai yeṉṉum uṟuppiṉaic cevi karuviyāka
   aḷakkiṉṟa aḷavu toḻilālē ceyyuṭkuk koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ, toṭarmoḻi yellām -
   ammāttirai tammuḷ toṭarntu niṟkiṉṟa coṟkaḷellām, neṭṭeḻut tiyala -
   neṭṭeḻuttu māttirai mikku naṭakkum paṭiyākat toṭarnta collām; e – ṟu.
   e - ṭu : 'varuvarkol vayaṅkiḻāay' (kali - 11) eṉavum, 'kaṭiyavē
   kaṉaṅkuḻāay' (kali - 11) eṉavuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām neṭṭeḻut tiṉai
   māttirai mikuttaṟkuk kūṭiyavāṟu uṇarka. ēṉaic ceyyuṭkaḷaiyum ivvāṟē
   kāṇka. eṉavē, māttirai aḷakkuṅkāl neṭṭeḻuttē māttirai peṟṟu mikku
   niṟkum eṉṟamaiyāṉ, etiratu pōṟṟa leṉṉum uttipaṟṟic ceyyuḷiyalai nōkkik
   kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu. īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār. neṭṭeḻuttu iraṇṭu māttiraiyiṉ ikantu
   varum eṉpatu aṟivittaṟku.
   aḷapeṉṟu māttiraiyaik kūṟātu aḷaveṉac cūttirañ ceytamaiyāṉ aḷavu
   toḻiṉmēl niṉṟatu. atu ceyyuḷiyaluḷ 'māttiraiyaḷavum' (ceyyuḷiyal - 2)
   eṉpataṉālum uṇarka. iyala veṉṟataṉaic ceyaveṉecca mākkip
   paṭuttalōcaiyāṟ kūṟuka.
   iṉit taṉṉiṉa muṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟiṟkum ivvāṟē koḷka. "kuraṅkukaḷaip
   polinta koycuvaṟ puravi" (akam-4) eṉṟa kuṟuñcīrvaṇṇattiṟku uriya
   kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām iṭaiyiṉiṉṟa oṟṟeḻuttai māttirai mikuttaṟkut
   kūṭiniṉṟavāṟu uṇarka. eṉavē, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷōṭum
   neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷōṭum kūṭi avaṟṟaiyē ōcaimikuttu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu.
   itaṉāṉē oṟṟicai nīṭalumeṉṟa oṟṟicai nīḷuṅkāṟ kuṟṟeḻuttāy nīḷu meṉṟār.
   iṉi urai yāciriyar pukar pukaḻ eṉak kuṟiliṇaikkīḻ īkāra ḻakāraṅkaḷ
   vanta toṭar moḻikaḷellān tār tāḻ eṉṟārpōla ōcaiyottu neṭṭeḻuttiṉ
   taṉmaiyavām eṉṟārāleṉiṉ, pukar pukaḻ eṉpaṉavaṟṟai neṭṭeḻutteṉṟē
   evviṭattum āḷāmaiyāṉum neṭṭeḻuttākak kūṟiya ilak kaṇattāl oru payaṉ
   koḷḷāmaiyāṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaluḷ ivaṟṟaik kuṟiviṇai oṟṟaṭutta
   niraiyacaiyākavun tār tāḻ eṉpaṉavaṟṟai neṭ ṭeḻuttu oṟṟaṭutta
   nēracaiyākavuṅ kōṭalāṉum atu poruḷaṉmai uṇarka.1 (17)
   -----------
   1. immaṟuppu avvaḷavu poruttamāyt tōṉṟavillai.

   ---------
   ceyyuḷiyal īroṟṟilakkaṇam
   51. ceyyuḷ iṟutip pōli moḻivayiṉ
   ṉakāra makāram īroṟ ṟākum.
   itu ceyyuṭkaṇ īroṟṟilakkaṇamāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ceyyuṭ pōli moḻi iṟutivayiṉ-ceyyuṭkaṇ pōlu meṉṉuñ colliṉ
   iṟutikkaṇ, ṉakāram makāram īroṟṟākum-ṉakāramum makāramum vantu īroṟṟu
   uṭaṉilaiyāy niṟkum.; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: "annūlai-munnūlāk koḷvāṉum pōṉm" (kalit-103) "citaiyuṅ kalattaip
   payiṉāl tiruttit-ticaiyaṟi mīkāṉum pōṉm" (pari-10-55) eṉa varum. pōlum
   eṉṉuñ ceyyumeṉṉum muṟṟu īṟṟumicai yukaram meyyoḻittuk keṭṭu lakāran
   tirin;tu niṉṟatu. iḵtu iṟutiyil muṟṟu; iṭaiyiṟ peyareccamākiya
   uvamavurupu 2. īṇṭu muṟṟeṉpār iṟutimoḻi eṉṟār. (18)
   ----------
   2. pōlum eṉṉum col; uyirpōluṅ kēḷir eṉa iṭaiyil niṟpiṉ peya
       reccamum mikāṉum pōlum mīkāṉum pōṉm eṉa iṟutiyil niṟpiṉ
       viṉaimuṟṟum ākum.

   ------------
   makaraṅ kuṟukumiṭam
   52. ṉakārai muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum.
   iḵtu araiyaḷapu kuṟukumeṉṟa makārattiṟkuk kuṟukum iṭam ituveṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ṉakārai muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum-muṟ kūṟiya ṉakarattiṉ muṉṉar vanta
   makaran taṉ arai māttiraiyiṟ kuṟuki niṟkum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: pōṉm eṉa muṉṉark kāṭṭiṉām. ṉakārai yeṉa iṭaiccol īṟutirintu
   niṉṟatu.
   iṉit taṉṉiṉa muṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ ṇakāravoṟṟiṉ muṉṉum makāraṅ kuṟukutal
   koḷka. "maruḷiṉu mellā maruṇm" eṉavarum. (19)
   -----------
   moḻikkaṇṇum māttirai vēṟupaṭāmai.
   53. moḻippaṭut ticaippiṉun terintuvē ṟicaippiṉum
   eḻuttiyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār pulavar.
   iḵtu oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum īṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷōṭu kūṭṭi eṇṇappaṭātu niṟku
   meṉpatūuñ ceyyuḷiyaluḷ eṇṇappaṭātu
   niṟku meṉpatu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: terintu-oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum poruḷtaru nilaimaiyai ārāyntu,
   moḻippaṭuttu icaippiṉum-collākac cērttuc colliṉum, vēṟu
   icaippiṉum-ceyyuḷiyaluḷ oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum poruḷtarumēṉum māttirai
   kuṟaintu niṟkum nilaimaiyai nōkki eḻutteṇṇappaṭāveṉṟu āṇṭaikku vēṟākak
   kūṟiṉum, eḻuttiyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār pulavar- avviraṇṭiṭattum araimāttirai
   peṟṟu niṟkum oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum muṟkūṟiya eḻuttān taṉmai tiriyāveṉṟu
   kūṟuvar palavar; e-ṟu.
   itaṉāṉ oṟṟum āytamum kuṟṟukaramum eḻuttāki niṉṟu poruḷ tantum,
   eḻutteṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟā veṉpatu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. terintu vēṟicaittal
   kuṟṟukarattiṟku iṉṟātaliṉ ēṟpuḻikkōṭalāṉ oṟṟiṟkum āytattiṟkuṅ koḷka.1
   --------
   1. ivvākkiyam muṉṉum piṉṉuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟiṟku muraṇākak kāṇkiṉṟatu.

   e-ṭu: al il uṇ eṇ ol eṉavum, kal vil muḷ cel col eṉavum, āl īr ūr ēr ōr
   eṉavum, kāl cīr cūl tēṉ kōṉ eṉavum uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya
   kuṟṟeḻuttaiyum neṭṭeḻuttaiyum oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ aṭuttuniṉṟu poruḷtantavāṟu
   kāṇka. kaṭam kaṭām uṭaiyāṉ tiruvārūr akattiyaṉār eṉa īreḻuttaiyum
   mūveḻuttaiyum nāleḻuttaiyum aiyeḻuttaiyum iṟutiyilum iṭaiyilum
   oṟṟaṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷtantavāṟu kāṇka. eḵku teḷku kokku kuraṅku
   eṉpaṉavum eḻut teṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟāta kuṟṟakaram aṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷ
   tantavāṟu kāṇka. "uyiril eḻuttum eṇṇap paṭāa-uyirttiṟam iyakkam iṉmai
   yāṉa" (ceyyuḷiyal-44) eṉpatu eḻuttu eṇṇap peṟāmaikku viti.
   iṉi iccūttirattiṟku eḻuttukkaḷaic collākkik kūṟiṉum piṟitākak kūṟiṉum
   māttirai tiriyāteṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, akaram eṉpuḻiyum a eṉpuḻiyum ālam
   eṉpuḻiyum ā eṉpuḻiyum kakaram eṉpuḻiyuṅ ka eṉpuḻiyuṅ kālam eṉpuḻiyuṅ kā
   eṉpuḻiyum ōcai ottu niṟkumeṉṟāl, atu muṉṉark kūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāṟ
   peṟappaṭutaliṟ payaṉil kūṟṟāmeṉka.1 (20)
   --------------
   1. immaṟuppu poruntuvataṉṟu.

   ----------

                                 2.5. pōli

   eḻuttup pōli
   54. akara ikaram aikāra mākum
   itu cilaveḻuttukkaḷ kūṭic cilaveḻuttukkaḷpōla icaikku meṉa eḻuttuppōli
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : akara ikaram aikāram ākum - akaramum ikaramuṅ kūṭic colla
   aikārampōla icaikkum, atu koḷḷaṟka; e – ṟu.
   pōla eṉṟatu tokkatu.
   e - ṭu : aivaṉam aivaṉam eṉa varum.1 ākumeṉṟataṉāl iḵtu
   ilakkaṇamaṉṟāyiṟṟu. (21)
   -----
   1. iṉi, in nūṟpāviṟku vayiram - vairam eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭalum oṉṟu.

   ---------
   55. akara ukaram aukāra mākum.
   ituvum atu.
   i - ḷ : akara ukaram aukāram ākum - akaramum ukaramum kūṭṭic colla
   aukārampōla icaikkum, atu koḷḷaṟka; e – ṟu.
   pōla eṉṟatu tokkatu.
   e - ṭu : auvai auvai eṉa varum.2 (22)
   ----
   2. innūṟpāviṟku, kavutāri - kautāri eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉum ākum.

   ----------
   56. akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
   ai eṉ neṭuñciṉai meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
   ituvum atu.
   i - ḷ : akarattimpar yakarap puḷḷiyum - akarattiṉ piṉ ikaramē yaṉṟi
   yakaramākiya puḷḷi vantālum, aiyeṉ neṭuñciṉai meypeṟat tōṉṟum -
   aiyeṉappaṭṭa neṭṭeḻuttiṉ vaṭivu peṟat tōṉṟum; e – ṟu.
   e - ṭu : aivaṉam ayvaṉam eṉa varum.3 mey peṟa eṉṟataṉāṉ akarattiṉ
   piṉṉar ukaramē yaṉṟi vakarappuḷḷiyum aukārampōla varumeṉṟu koḷka. auvai
   avvai eṉa varum.4 (23)
   --------
   3. ayvaṉam, ayyar eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa ceyyuḷil etukainōkki varum tiripu
       vaṭivaṅkaḷākum.
       4. 'avvai' muntiyavaṭivam. 'auvai' ataṉpōli.

   -------------
   ai au kuṟukutal
   57. ōraḷa pākum iṭaṉumā ruṇṭē1
   tēruṅ kālai moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
   iḵtu atikārattāṉ aikārattiṟkum aukārattiṟkum etiratu pōṟṟa leṉpataṉāṟ
   ceyyuḷiyalai nōkki māttiraiccurukkaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   ----------
   1. aikāra aukārak kuṟukka māttirai oṉṟarai eṉpar nēminātar. itu mutal
       kaṭaiyiṭaṅkaṭkēṟkum.

   i-ḷ: moḻivayiṉāṉa-oru colliṭattē niṉṟa aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ, tēruṅkālai
   -ārāyumiṭattu, ōraḷapākum iṭaṉu māruṇṭē-oru māttiraiyāy niṟkum iṭamum
   uṇṭu; e-ṟu.
   ummaiyāṉ iraṇṭumāttirai peṟutalē valivuṭattāyiṟṟu. iṭaṉu meṉṟatu oru
   colliṉ mutaliṭaikaṭai yeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattuṅ kuṟukum, atu ceyyuṭkaṇ ōcai
   iṭarppaṭ ṭolikkumiṭattuk kuṟuku meṉṟaṟku. uraiyiṟkōṭalāl aikāram
   mutaliṭaikaṭai yeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattuṅ kuṟukum, aukāram mutaṟkaṇ kuṟuku
   meṉakkoḷka.
   e-ṭu: aippaci kaippai iṭaiyaṉ kuvaḷai eṉa varum. "aṭaippai yāykōṟā"
   eṉavum, "puṉaiyiḷaṅ koṅkaiyāy varum" eṉavum piṟavāṟum varuvaṉa
   ceyyuḷiyaluṭ kāṇka. auvai kauvai eṉa varum. aukāram "kauvainīr
   vēlikūṟṟu" eṉat toṭai nōkkik kuṟukuvaṉavāṟuṅ kāṇka. tēruṅkālai
   yeṉṟataṉāṉ ōreḻut torumoḻiyuṅ kuṟukum. kai pai eṉa varum. (24)
   ------------
   pōliyil maṟṟoru vakai
   58. ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum
   ituvum pōlikūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ikarayakaram iṟuti viravum-ikaramum yakaramum orumoḻiyiṉ iṟutikkaṇ
   ōcai viravi varum, avvikāraṅ koḷḷaṟka; e-ṟu.
   nāy nāi eṉavarum.2 (25)
   -----------
   2. orukāl, nāyi eṉpatu paṇṭai vaḻakkā yiruntirukkalām.

   -------------

                       2.6. moḻimutal eḻuttukkaḷ

   moḻimutalākum uyirkaḷ
   59. paṉṉī ruyirum moḻimuta lākum.
   itu mēl eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu uṇartti ammoḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu ivai
   yeṉpatu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: paṉṉīruyirum-paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum, moḻi mutal ākum-moḻikku
   mutalām; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: aṭai āṭai ilai īyam uḷai ūrti eḻu ēṇi aivaṉam oḷi ōṭam auviyam
   eṉa varum. (26)
   -------------
   taṉi mey moḻimuta lākāmai
   60. uyirmey allaṉa moḻimuta lākā.
   iḵtu uyirmey moḻikku mutalām eṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uyirmeyyallaṉa moḻimutal ākā-uyirōṭu kūṭiya meyyallātaṉavākiya
   taṉi meykaḷ moḻikku mutalākā; e-ṟu.
   eṉavē, uyirōṭu kūṭiya meykaḷē moḻikku mutalāvaṉa eṉṟavāṟām. īṇṭu
   uyirmey yeṉṟatu vēṟṟumai nayam karutiṟṟu. oṟṟumai nayak karuttiṉ mēlaic
   cūttirattu uyirōṭukūṭi āmeṉṟal payaṉiṉṟām (27)
   -----------
   paṉṉīruyiruṭaṉ moḻi mutalātal
   61. katana pamaeṉum āvain teḻuttum
   ellā uyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē.
   itu mēṟ potuvakaiyāṉ eytuvitta irunūṟṟorupattāṟu eḻuttukkaḷaic
   ciṟappuvakaiyāṉ varaiyaṟuttu eytuvikkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ka ta na pa ma eṉum āvainteḻuttum-ka ta na pa ma eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa
   avvaintu taṉimeyyum, ellā uyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē-paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirōṭum
   moḻikku mutalātaṟkuc cellum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: kalai, kār, kiḷi, kīri, kuṭi, kūṭu, keṇṭai, kēḻal, kaitai,
   koṇṭal, kōṭai, kauvai, eṉavum; tantai, tāy, titti, tīmai, tuṇi, tūṇi,
   teṟṟi, tēṉ, taiyal, toṇṭai, tōṭu, tauvai, eṉavum; nantu, nārai, nilam,
   nīlam, nukam, nūl, neytal, nēmi, naivaḷam, nocci, nōkkam, nauvi,
   eṉavum; paṭai, pāl, piṭi, pīṭu, pukaḻ, pūmi, peṭai, pēṭai, paital, poṉ,
   pōtu, pauvam, eṉavum; maṭi, mālai, miṭaṟu, mīḷi, mukam, mūppu, melivu,
   mēṉi, maiyal, moḻi, mōttai, mauval, eṉavum varum. (28)
   ---------
   cakara mey oṉpatuyiruṭaṉ moḻimutalātal
   62. cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
   a ai au eṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē1
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: cakarakkiḷaviyum avaṟṟōra ṟṟē-cakaramākiya taṉimeyyum
   muṟkūṟiyavaipōla ellā uyirōṭuṅ kūṭi moḻikku mutalām, a ai au eṉum
   mūṉṟalaṅkaṭaiyē-
   akara aikāra aukārameṉṟu collappaṭṭa mūṉṟu uyirum allāta iṭattu; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: cāntu ciṟṟil cīṟṟam curai cūral cekku cēval col cōṟu eṉa varum.
   caṭṭi cakaṭam camaḻppu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa "kaṭicollillai" (eccaviyal-56)
   eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. caiyam cauriyam eṉpaṉavaṟṟai
   vaṭacolleṉa maṟukka. (29)
   ----------
   1cakaram moḻimutal varāteṉṟu kūṟuvatu tamiḻukkuc caṟṟum poruntātu. "a
       ai au eṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē" eṉṉum nūṟpā aṭikku "avai au eṉṉum
       oṉṟalaṅkaṭaiyē eṉṟu pāṭavēṟupāṭum uḷḷatu. cakakaṭṭi, cakkai,
       cakaṭu, cakti, caṅku, caṭṭeṉa, caṭṭam, caṭṭakam, caṭṭi, caṭṭai,
       caṭa caṭa, caṭai, caṭaivu, caṇṭi, caṇṭu, caṇṭai, caṇal, catuppu,
       cappaṭṭai, cappeṉṟu, cappāṇi, cappu, cappai, campu, cammaṭṭi,
       camaṭṭu, camma, camaḻa, camai, caraṭu, caraḷa, cari, caruku,
       carukkarai, carēleṉa, callaṭai, callari, calli, calāl, calaṅkai,
       cali, cavvu, cavam, cavā, cavai, caṉi, caḷakkeṉa, caḻakku, caḷḷeṉa
       ,caḷḷai, caḷai, caṟṟu, caṟukku, caṉṉam, mutaliya nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa
       taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷ aṭippaṭaiyāṉavum toṉṟu toṭṭavum iṉṟi
       yamaiyātaṉavum vērūṉṟiṉavum cērivaḻakkiṉavumāyirukka, avaṟṟaip piṟ
       kālattaṉaveṉṟu koḷḷuvatu peruntavaṟākum. cakkai, caṭṭi, caṇṭu,
       caṇṭai, catai, cappu, cali, caṟṟu, caṟukku mutaliya coṟkaḷ ettuṇai
       eḷimaiyum iyalpumāṉavai eṉpatu collāmalē viḷaṅkum. caṇṭu, caruku
       mutaliya cila coṟkaḷ paṇṭu cakara mutalaṉavāyiruntiruttal
       kūṭumeṉiṉum, cakku, caṭār, caṭō caraṭṭu calarala carēla calavu
       caḷakku caḷarā caḷ mutaliya olik kuṟippuc coṟkaḷum
       avaṟṟiṉaṭippiṟantavum tuvakkantoṭṭuc cakara mutavaṉavāyē
       yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. cāppiṭu eṉṉum ulakavaḻak keḷimaic col cappu
       eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyatākum. cappu+iṭu = cappīṭu -cāppiṭu.
       cuvai eṉṉum collum cavai eṉpataṉ tiripākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. cettāṉ
       eṉṉum iṟantakāla viṉaimuṟṟu paṇṭaik kālattil cattāṉ eṉṟē
       iruntiruttalvēṇṭum. ō.kō.: kāṇ-kaṇṭāṉ. kō- nontāṉ. neṭiṉa mutalāṉa
       viṉaippakuti iṟantakāla muṟṟil mutala* kuṟu kumpōtu iṉakkuṟilāy
       kuṟukuvatē marapu. teluṅkilum caraciṉāṭu (cettāṉ) caccippōyiṉāṭu
       (cettuppōṉāṉ) eṉṟē colvar. mēlum "muḻu mutal araṇum", "varupakai
       pōṇār āreyilum" amaittukkoṇṭa tol kāppiyam kālattamiḻar caṭṭiceyyat
       teriyātiruntaṉar eṉpatu perunakaik kiṭamāṉatākum. caṭṭi eṉpatu
       camaiyalukku iṉṟiyamaiyātatum, eḷinilaiyāṉatum , maṟupeyaraṟṟatu
       māṉa kalavakai.

   -----------
   vakara mey eṭṭuyiruṭaṉ moḻimutalātal
   63. uū oō eṉṉum nāṉkuyir
   va eṉ eḻuttoṭu varuta lillai.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: u ū o ō eṉṉum nāṉkuyir-u ū o ō eṉṟu collappaṭṭa nāṉku uyirum, va
   eṉ eḻuttoṭu varutalillai-va eṉṟu collappaṭuntaṉa meyyeḻuttōṭu
   kūṭimoḻikku mutalāy varuta lillai; e-ṟu.
   eṉavē, oḻintaṉa moḻikku mutalām eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu.
   e-ṭu: vaḷai vāḷi viḷari vīṭu veḷḷi vēṭkai vaiyam vauvutal eṉa varum.
   (30)
   ------------
   "caricamaḻppuca* caṭṭi caruku cavaṭi
   caḷicakaṭu caṭṭai cavaḷi-cavicaraṭu
   cantu cataṅkai caḻakkāti yīriṭattatum
   vantaṉavāṟ cammuta lumavai" eṉpatu naṉṉūl mayilai nātarurai mēṟkōḷ.
   ----------
   ñakaramey mūṉṟuyiruṭaṉ moḻimutalātal
   64. ā e o
   eṉṉum mūvuyir ñakārat turiya.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: ā e ō eṉum mūvuyir-ā e o eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟu uyirum,
   ñakārattukku uriya-ñakāra oṟṟoṭu kūṭi moḻikku mutalātaṟku uriya; e-ṟu.
   eṉavē, ēṉaiya uriyavalla eṉpatām.
   e-ṭu: ñālam ñeṇṭu ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉavarum.
   "ñamali tanta maṉavuccū luṭumpu" (perumpāṇ-132) eṉpatu ticaiccol.
   ñaḻiyiṟṟu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa iḻivaḻakku,. (31)
   ------------
   yakaram ākārattuṭaṉē moḻimutalāmeṉal.
   65 āvō ṭallatu yakara mutalātu,
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: āvōṭu allatu yakaram mutalātu-ākārattōṭu kūṭi yallatu yakara voṟṟu
   moḻikku mutalākātu; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: yāṉai yāṭu yāmam eṉa varum.
   yavaṉar yutti yūpam yōkam yauvaṉam eṉpaṉa vaṭacolleṉamaṟukka. (32)
   --------
   ellā eḻuttum mutalām iṭam
   66. mutalā ēṉa tampeyar mutalum
   itu moḻikku mutalākātaṉavum orō vaḻi āmeṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: mutalāvum-moḻikku mutalākā eṉṟa oṉpatu meyyum, ēṉavum-moḻikku
   mutalā meṉṟa oṉpatu meyyum paṉṉiraṇṭuyirum, tam peyar mutalum-tattam
   peyar kūṟutaṟku mutalām ;e-ṟu.
   mutalāvum eṉavum eṉṟa ummaikaḷ tokkuniṉṟaṉa.
   e-ṭu: ṅakaramum ṭakaramum ṇakaramum rakaramum lakaramum ḻakaramum
   ḷakaramum ṟakaramum ṉakaramum eṉa moḻikku mutalākāta oṉpatum
   mutalāmāṟu, ṅakkaḷaintār ṭapperitu ṇannaṉṟu eṉavarum. ivvāṟē
   ēṉaiyavaṟṟaiyum oṭṭuka. iṉi eṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ kakkaḷaintār tapperitu ak
   kuṟitu āneṭitu eṉa moḻikku mutalāṉavaṟṟaiyun tampeyar kūṟutaṟku
   mutalāmāṟu oṭṭik koḷka. varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭu moḻikku mutalākātu niṉṟa
   meykkum ivviti koḷka. avai cakarattu mūṉṟam vakarattu nāṉkum ñakarat
   toṉpatum yakarattup patiṉoṉṟumām. (33)
   -------------
   kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻi mutalāmāṟu.
   67. kuṟṟiya lukaram muṟaippeyar maruṅkiṉ
   oṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum1
   iḵtu eḻuttukkaḷai moḻikku mutalāmāṟu kūṟi muṟaiyē kuṟṟiyalukaram
   moḻikku mutalāmāṟu kūṟakiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟaippeyar maruṅkiṉ-kuṟṟiyalukaramāṉatu muṉṉilai
   muṟaippeyariṭattu, oṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum-taṉimeyyāy
   niṉṟa nakarattu mēṉiṉṟa nakartoṭu kūṭi moḻikku mutaleṉṟār,. itu
   ceyyuḷiyalai nōkkik kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu. (34)
   ---------
   moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ iyalpu.
   68. muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāa
   tappeyar maruṅkiṉ nilaiyiya lāṉa.
   itu mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: appeyarmaruṅkiṉ nilaiyiyalāṉa-ammuṟaip peyariṭattē
   niṟṟalilakkaṇamāṉa kuṟṟiyalukaram, muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷ
   vēṟupaṭāatu-itaḻ kuvittuk kūṟum vaḻi varum muṟṟukarattōṭu avviṭattuk
   kuṟṟukaram poruḷ vēṟupaṭumāṟupōla īṇṭup poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu nillātu; e-ṟu.
   kātu, kaṭṭu, kattu, murukku, teruṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramum kuṟṟu
   karamumāyp poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu niṉṟāṟpōla2 nuntai yeṉṟu itaḻ kuvittu muṟṟak
   kūṟiyaviṭattum itaḻ kuviyāmaṟ kuṟaiyak kūṟiyaviṭattum oru poruḷē
   tantavāṟu kāṇka. nuntāy eṉpatōveṉiṉ aḵtu itaḻ kuvittē kūṟavēṇṭutaliṟ
   kuṟṟukaramaṉṟu. iyaleṉṟataṉāṉ iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum iraṇṭiṟkum
   vēṟupāṭiṉṟeṉṟu koḷka. itaṉāṉē moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttut toṇṇūṟṟu nāṉkeṉṟu
   uṇarka. (35)
   -----------
   1. "kuṟṟiya lukara.................nakaramoṭu mutalum " eṉa āciriyar
       tolkāppiyaṉār ivvāṟu kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻikku mutalāmeṉṟārālō veṉiṉ "
       **tukataiyukaraṅ kuṟukimoḻi mutaṟkaṇ vanta teṉiṉuyirmey
       yāmaṉaittuñ-cantik, kuyirmutalā vantaṇaiyu meyppuṇarcci yiṉṟi,
       mayalaṇaiyu meṉṟataṉaimāṟṟu" ivaṟṟai viritturaittu vitiyum
       vilakkuma, aṟintukoḷka. eṉpatu naṉṉūl mayilainātarurai mēṟkōḷ.
       (eḻu-105)
       2. kātu kaṭṭu mutaliyavai muṟṟukaramāyiṉ ēvala viṉaiyum
       kuṟṟukaramāyiṉ toḻiṟ peyarumākum.

   ---------

                     2.7. moḻiyiṟuti eḻuttukkaḷ

   moḻiyīṟākum uyirkaḷ
   69. uyirau eñciya iṟuti yākum
   iḵtu uyir moḻikku īṟāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uyir au eñciya iṟuti yākum-uyirkaḷuḷ aukāram oḻintaṉa vellām
   moḻikku īṟām :e-ṟu
   eṉavē aukāravuyir īṟākātāyiṟṟu. iḵtu uyirkkum uyir meykkum potu.
   ā ī ū ē ai ō eṉa ivai tāmē īṟāyiṉa. āa īi ūu ēe ōo eṉak kuṟilaintum
   aḷapeṭaikkaṇ īṟāyiṉa. kā tī pū cē kai kō eṉavum viḷa kiḷi maḻu eṉavum
   varum. ekara okaram mēlē vilakkupa. "aḷapeṭai mikūu mikara viṟupeyar"
   (col-125) eṉpārātaliṉ, aḷapeṭaippiṉ vanta kuṟṟeḻuttuṅ koḷvar āciriya
   reṉṟu uṇarka. neṭṭeḻuttēḻum mutaṉ moḻiyām eṉṉun tuṇaiyē
   muṉṉuṇarttutaliṉ īṇṭu avai īṟāmeṉṟum uṇarttiṉār. (36)
   ----------
   au īṟākum iṭam
   70. kavavō ṭiyaiyiṉ auvu mākum
   iḵtu īṟākāteṉṟa aukāram iṉṉuḻiyām eṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: auvum--muṉ īṟākāteṉṟa aukāramum, kavavōṭu iyaiyiṉ
   ākum-kakaravakarattōṭu iyainta vaḻi īṟām;e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu-: kau vau eṉavarum. eṉavē oḻinta uyirellān tāmē niṉṟum patiṉeṭṭu
   meykaḷōṭuṅ kūṭi niṉṟum īṟātal itaṉāṟ peṟṟām. itaṉāṉē aukāram ēṉai
   meykkaṇ vārāteṉa vilakkutalum peṟṟām. uyir ṅakarattōṭu kūṭi moḻikku īṟā
   meṉpatu itaṉāl eytiṟṟēṉum atu moḻikaku īṟākāmai tantu puṇarn
   turaittalāṉ uṇarka.1 ituvaraiyaṟai kūṟiṟṟu. (37)
   ---------
   1 tantu puṇarnturaittal-uḷ poruḷala*lataṉai uḷapōlat tantu kūṭa
       uṇarttal, iṅku, uyir ṅakarattōṭu kūṭi moḻikku īṟāvatupōlak kūṟiyatu
       tantu puṇarnturaittal.

   --------
   ekara vuyir meyyō ṭīṟākāmai
   71. e eṉa varumuyir meyyī ṟākātu.
   iḵtu ekaran tāṉē niṉṟavaḻi yaṉṟi meyyōṭu kūṭiṉāl īṟākāteṉa
   vilakkukiṉṟatu,
   i-ḷ: e eṉa varum uyir mey īṟākātu-e eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum uyir tāṉē
   īṟāvataṉṟi yāṇṭum meykaḷōṭu iyaintu īṟākātu. e-ṟu. (38)
   ------------
   okaram nakaramey oṉṟuṭaṉ īṟātal
   72. ovvum aṟṟē navvalaṅ kaṭaiyē
   itu vilakkum varaiyaṟaiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ovvum aṟṟu-okaramum muṉcoṉṉa ekaramum pōlattāṉē īṟāvataṉṟi
   meykaḷōṭu iyaintu īṟākātu, navvalaṅkaṭaiyē-nakaravoṟṟōṭu allāta
   iṭattil; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: no koṟṟā "no alaiyaṉiṉṉāṭṭainī" eṉavarum. (39)
   ------------
   ē, ō ñakara meyyuṭa ṉīṟākāmai
   73. ē ō eṉum uyir ñakārat tillai.
   itu cila uyir cila uṭalōṭēṟi muṭiyāteṉa vilakkukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ē ō eṉum uyir ñakārattillai-ē ō eṉṟukūṟappaṭṭa iraṇṭuyirun tāmē
   niṉṟum piṟameykaḷōṭu niṉṟum īṟātalaṉṟi ñakārattōṭu īṟātalillai.;ē-ṟu.
   eṉavē ēṉaiyuyirkaḷ ñakārattōṭu īṟāmeṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
   e-ṭu: uriña, uriñā, uriñī, uriñī, uriñu, uriñū ivai eccamum
   viṉaippeyarum paṟṟi varum. aññai, maññai ivai peyar. ēṉai yaintum
   vilakkappaṭṭaṉa. uriñō eṉpatu "kaṭicollillai" eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. (40)
   ----------
   u ū nakara vakaraṅkaḷuṭaṉ īṟākāmai
   74. u ū kāram navavoṭu navilā,.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: u ūkāram-ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ tāmē niṉṟum piṟameykaḷōṭuniṉṟum
   payilvataṉṟi, navavōṭu navilā-nakara voṟṟōṭum vakaravoṟṟōṭum payilā;
   e-ṟu.
   eṉavē ēṉai yuyirkaḷ nakara vakaraṅkaḷōṭu varumāyiṉa
   e-ṭu: nakaram poruna eṉa viṉaippeyarākiyum, nā nī nē eṉap peyarākiyum
   nai no nō eṉa viyaṅkōḷākiyum varum. porunai eṉṟuṅ kāṭṭupa. vakaram uva
   vē eṉa viyaṅkōḷākiyum, uvā cevvi vī vai eṉap peyarāyum varum. oruva
   oruvā oruvi oruvī auvai eṉṟuṅ kāṭṭupa. īṇṭu vilakkāta ēṉai yuyirkaḷōṭu
   vanta nakaravakaraṅkaḷ akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉaveṉṟu kōṭum. ivvitiyāl iṉi
   navilā eṉṟataṉāṉē vakara vukaram katavu turavu kuvavu puṇarvu nukarvu
   navvu kavvu eṉap payiṉṟu varutaluṅ koḷka. (41)
   -----------
   cakara vukaram irumoḻikku īṟātal
   75. ucca kāram irumoḻik kurittē
   itu cakāra ukāram palacoṟku īṟāy vārātu irucoṟku īṟāmeṉṟu varaiyaṟai
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uccakāram-ukarattōṭu kūṭiya cakāram, iru moḻikkē urittu-iraṇṭu
   moḻikkē īṟām; e-ṟu.
   eṉavē paṉmoḻikku īṟākā teṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. urittē yeṉṉumēkāram moḻikkē
   yeṉak kūṭṭuka.
   e-ṭu: 24; iḵtu uḷuviṉ peyar. mucu; itu kuraṅkiṉuḷ oru cāci. pacu eṉpatō
   veṉiṉ, aḵtu āriyac citaivu. kaccu kuccu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram.
   ukaram ēṟiya cakaram irumoḻikku īṟāmeṉavē ēṉai uyirkaḷ ēṟiya cakaram
   paṉmoḻikku īṟāmāyiṟṟu. uca ucā vici cē kaccaicō eṉap peyarāyun , tuñca
   eñcā eñci mūci mūcū eṉa eccamāyum varum. accō eṉa viyappāyum varum.
   iṉṉum ivai vaḻakkiṉkaṭ palavāmāṟum uṇarka. (42)
   ---------
   pakara vukaram oru moḻikkē īṟātal
   76. uppa kāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa
   iruvayiṉ nilaiyum poruṭṭā kummē.
   iḵtu orucol varayaṟaiyum aḵtu ōcai vēṟṟumaiyāl iru poruḷ tarumeṉavuṅ
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uppakāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa-ukarattōṭu kūṭiya pakaram orumoḻikkallatu
   paṉmoḻikku īṟākā teṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar, iruvayiṉilaiyum
   poruṭṭākummē-atutāṉ taṉviṉai piṟaviṉai yeṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattum nilaipeṟum
   poruṇmaittām.; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: tapu eṉa varum.itu paṭuttuk kūṟa nī cā veṉat taṉ viṉaiyām.
   eṭuttukkūṟa nī oṉṟaṉaic cāvappaṇṇeṉap piṟaviṉaiyām.1 uppu tappu eṉṟāṟ
   pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram. ukarattōṭu kūṭiya pakaram oṉṟeṉavē ēṉai uyirkaḷōṭu
   kūṭiya pakaram paṉmoḻikku īṟāyp palaporuḷ tarum eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. maṟantapa
   tuppā eṉa eccamāyum, nampi cempū pē petumpai eṉap peyarāyum, pō eṉa
   ēvalāyum varum. ivaṟṟaip piṟa coṟkaḷōṭum oṭṭuka. ēṉai īkāra pakaram
   iṭakkarāy vaḻaṅkum. (43)
   --------
   1 itaṉāl āṅkilattiṟpōla paṇṭaittamiḻilum acaiyaḻuttam (accent) oru
       colliṉ vakaiyaiyum poruḷaiyum vēṟupaṭuttiṟ ṟeṉpatai aṟiyalām.

   ----------
   īṟākā uyirmey iṉṉavakaiyil āmeṉal
   77. eñciya vellām eñcutal ilavē
   itu muṉṉar moḻikku īṟām eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ eñci niṉṟaṉa moḻikku īṟāmāṟum
   moḻikku īṟākā eṉṟavai tampeyar kūṟuṅkāṉ moḻikku īṟāmāṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: eñciyavum eñcuta lila. "kavavōṭiyaiyiṉ" (eḻu-70) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṟ
   patiṉōruyirum patiṉeṭṭu meykkaṇṇum vantu moḻikku īṟā meṉṟa potu vitiyiṟ
   piṉṉai vicēṭittuk kūṟiyavaṟṟai oḻintaṉavum moḻikku īṟātaṟku oḻivila,
   ellām eñcutalila-moḻikku īṟākāteṉṟa uyirmeykaḷun tampeyar kūṟumvaḻi
   īṟātaṟku oḻivila; e-ṟu.
   ellā meṉṟatu colliṉeccañ colliyāṅ kuṇarttal eṉṉum utti1. ummai virikka.
   īṇṭu eñciya veṉṟatu muṉṉar utāraṇam kāṭṭiya ñakaramum nakaramum
   vakaramuñ cakaramum pakaramum oru moḻikkum īṟākāta ṅakaramum oḻinta
   paṉṉiraṇṭu meykkaṇṇum ekaramum okaramum aukāramum oḻinta oṉpatuyirum
   ēṟi moḻikku īṟāy varuvaṉavaṟṟai yeṉṟu uṇarka.
   ----------
   1. "colliṉeccañcolliyāṅ kuṇarttala"-colliṉāṟṟalāṟ peṟappaṭum poruḷaiyum
       eṭuttōtiyāṅkuk koḷḷa vaittal; aḵtu "eñciyavellā meñcutalilavē"
       (eḻu- 77) eṉpuḻi, ellāmeṉpataṉai eccappaṭuttaṟku ākātaṉa irupattāṟu
       koṇṭavaḻiyum ataṉai eṭuttōtiṟ ciṟappiṉṟeṉṟu koḷḷaṟkaeṉpatu"
       (pērāciriyam)

   e-ṭu: varuka pukā vīkki pukī ceku pukū īṅkē maṅkai eṅkō eṉavum, kaṭṭa
   kaṭā maṭi maṭī maṭu paṭū paṭai eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi
   varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka) maṇṇa eṇṇā kaṇṇi uṇī kaṇu kaṇṇū paṇṇai eṉavum
   (itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka) ata putā pati vatī
   atu kaitū tantai antō eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāram ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka)
   kama neṉmā ammi mī cemmukoṇmū yāmai kāttumvammō eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāram
   ēṟivaruvaṉa uḷa vēṟ koḷka) ceya kāyā koyyū aiyai aiyō eṉavum (itaṟku
   ikara īkāra ukara ēkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka) vara tārā pari
   kurī karu verū nārai eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ
   koḷka) cila palā vali valī valu kollū vallē kalai eṉavum (itaṟku ōkāram
   ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka) toḻa viḻā nāḻi vaḻī maḻu eḻū tāḻai eṉavum
   (itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka), uḷa uḷḷā veḷḷi kuḷī
   uḷu eḷḷū kaḷai eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ
   koḷka) kaṟṟa kaṟṟā uṟi uṟī maṟu uṟū kaṟṟai eṟṟō eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāram
   ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka) kaṉa kaṉā vaṉṉi tuṉī muṉṉu tuṉṉū eṉṉē aṉṉai
   aṉṉō eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟuṭ peyarāyum viṉaiyāyum varuvaṉa uṇarka.
   ivaṟṟuṭ kakara ṉakaraṅkaḷ vilakkāta oṉpatum vantaṉa. āka īṟu
   nūṟṟunāṟpattu mūṉṟum utāraṇamillāta patiṉeṭṭum āka nūṟṟaṟupattoṉṟu. 2
   ṅakaram moḻikku īṟākāteṉpatu perumpāṉmai yātaliṟ kūṟiṟṟilar. iṉi
   ṅakaramum aukāramum ēṟāta mey patiṉaintum ekaramum okaramum ēkāramum
   ōkāramum ukaramum ūkāramum ēṟāta meykaḷun tampeyar kūṟuṅkāṉ moḻikku
   īṟāmāṟu *ṅapperitu cauaḻakitu ñautītu eṉa varum. ēṉaiyavaṟṟōṭum ivvāṟē
   oṭṭuka. kekkuṟaintatu kottītu ñevvaḻakitu ñottītu tunnaṉṟu nūpperitu
   vucciṟitu vūpperitu eṉa ellāvaṟṟaiyum ivvāṟē oṭṭuka. iṉṉum ellām
   eṉṟataṉāṉē kannaṉṟu ānaṉṟa eṉa moḻikku īṟāvaṉavun tampeyar kūṟum vaḻi
   āmeṉṟu koḷka (44)
   -----------
   2. "itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟivaruvaṉa uḷavēṟkoḷka" eṉap pala meykkuk
       kūṟiyiruppataṉālum eṉṉē aṉṉō, aiyō eṉṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷ kāṭṭākak
       koḷḷappaṭṭiruppataṉālum; aṭē, vīṇē, aiyē, eṟṟē, arō mutaliya
       vaṭivaṅkaḷ vaḻakkiliruppataṉālum kāṭṭuka kāṭṭappaṭāta ēkāra ōkāra
       uyir meyyīṟukaṭkellām kāṭṭuṇṭeṉa aṟika. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar īṟṟiṉ
       mottattokai kūṟumiṭattu "āka īṟu nūṟṟu nāṟpattatu mūṉṟum
       utāraṇamillāta patiṉeṭṭum āka" eṉpatu āka īṟu nūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉṟum
       utāraṇamillāta irupatum eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum.

   -------------
   moḻiyīṟām meykaḷ
   78. ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum
   appati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti.
   itu muṉṉar uyir īṟāmāṟu uṇarttip puḷḷikaḷuḷ īṟāvaṉa ivai yeṉkiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum appatiṉoṉṟē puḷḷiyiṟuti
   - ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa veṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa patiṉoṉṟumē
   puḷḷikaḷil moḻikku īṟāvaṉa: e – ṟu.
   e – ṭu : uriñ, maṇ, porun, tirum , poṉ, vēy, vēr, vēl, tev, vīḻ, vēḷ
   eṉa varum. ṉakaram īṟṟu vaiyātu makarattōṭu vaittatu vaḻakkup
   payiṟciyum mayakka iyaiyum nōkki. (45)
   -----------
   nakaramey irumoḻikku īṟātal
   79. ucca kāramoṭu nakārañ civaṇum.
   itu mēṟ potuvakaiyāṉ īṟāvaṉavaṟṟuḷ varaiyaṟaippaṭuvatu ituveṉkiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : uccakāramoṭu nakārañ civaṇum – ukārattōṭu kūṭiya cakaram
   irumoḻik kīṟāyavāṟu pōla nakaravoṟṟum irumoḻikkallatu īṟākātu; e – ṟu.
   e - ṭu : porun verin eṉa varum. (46)
   -------------
   ñakaramey oru moḻikkē īṟātal
   80. uppa kāramoṭu ñakāraiyum aṟṟē
   apporuḷ iraṭṭā tivaṇai yāṉa.
   ituvum atu.
   i - ḷ : uppakāramoṭu ñakāraiyum aṟṟē – ukārattōṭu kūṭiya pakarattōṭu
   ñakaramum ottu oru moḻikku īṟām, ivaṇaiyāṉa apporuḷ iraṭṭātu -
   ivviṭattu ñakārattiṉ kaṇṇāṉa apporuḷ pakarampōla iruporuṭ paṭātu;
   e – ṟu.
   e - ṭu : uriñ eṉa varum. ñakāram oru moḻikku īṟātaliṉ nakarattiṉpiṉ
   kūṟiṉār. ivaṇai yeṉṉum aikāram acai. (47)
   ----------
   vakara mey nāṉmoḻikku īṟātal
   81. vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi īṟṟatu.
   ituvum atu.
   i – ḷ : vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi īṟṟatu – vakaramākiya eḻuttu nāṉku
   moḻiyiṉ īṟṟatām; e – ṟu.
   e - ṭu : av iv uv tev eṉa varum. kiḷavi ākupeyar, eḻuttuk kiḷaviyātaṟku
   urittāmātaliṉ. (48)
   -----------
   aḵṟiṇaikkaṇ ṉakaravīṟṟu moḻikaḷ oṉpatu
   82. makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta
   ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵ teṉpa
   pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇai mēṉa.1
   ituvum atu; varaiyaṟai kūṟutaliṉ.
   ---
   1. "makarat toṭarmoḻi………. aḵṟiṇai mēṉa*" eṉṟu, ekiṉ, cekiṉ, eyiṉ,
       vayiṉ, kuyiṉ, aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, pulāṉ*, kaṭāṉ eṉa varum oṉpatum
       mayaṅkātaṉa
       veṉakkoḷḷiṉ*, paliyaṉa, valiyaṉa, vayāṉa, ka*pāṉa, alavaṉa,
       kalavaṉa,
       kaluḻaṉa, maṟaiyaṉa, cekilaṉa mutaliyaṉa mayaṅkappeṟāveṉa maṟukka"
       eṉpatu naṉṉūl mayilainātarurai. (nū. 121)

   i - ḷ : pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇai mēṉa – kuṟṟamaṟac collappaṭṭa
   aḵṟiṇaippeyariṭattu, makarattoṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta
   ṉakarattoṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵtu eṉpa - makara īṟṟut toṭarmoḻiyōṭu mayaṅkā
   teṉṟu varaiyaṟaippaṭṭa ṉakara īṟṟut toṭarmoḻi oṉpateṉṟu kūṟuvar
   āciriyar; e – ṟu.
   āytam vikāram.
   e - ṭu : ekiṉ, cekiṉ, viḻaṉ, payiṉ, kuyiṉ, aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, kaṭāṉ, vayāṉ
   eṉa varum. ekiṉ ekiṉam eṉṟāṟpōla vēṟōr peyarāyt tirivaṉavuñ cantiyāl
   tirivaṉavumāy īṟṟuḷ tiripuṭaiyaṉa kaḷaintu oṉpatum varumēṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
   nilam nilaṉ, pilam pilaṉ, kalam kalaṉ, valam valaṉ, ulam ulaṉ, kulam
   kulaṉ, kaṭam kaṭaṉ, polam polaṉ, pulam pulaṉ, nalam nalaṉ, kuḷam kuḷaṉ,
   vaḷam vaḷaṉ eṉa ittoṭakkattaṉa tammuḷ mayaṅkuvaṉa. vaṭṭam kuṭṭam, ōṭam
   pāṭam ivaipōlvaṉa mayaṅkātaṉa. varaiyaṟai ṉakarattiṉmēṟ cellum.
   mayaṅkāveṉavē mayakkamum peṟṟām.
   moḻimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.

                               3. piṟappiyal
           [eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappilakkaṇam uṇarttuvatu.]
               3.1. eḻuttukkaḷ potuvākap piṟakkumāṟu

   83. unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟit
   talaiyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilaiip
   pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum
   aṇṇamum(1) uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟai nilaiyāṉ
   uṟuppuṟ ṟamaiya neṟippaṭa nāṭi
   ellā eḻuttum colluṅ kālaip
   piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟuvē ṟiyala
   tiṟappaṭat teriyuṅ kāṭci yāṉa.
   eṉpatu cūttiram. ivvōttu eḻuttukkaḷiṉatu piṟappu uṇarttutaliṟ piṟappiya
   leṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟattu eḻuttuntaṉimeyyum moḻiyi ṉaṉṟi
   uṇarttalākāmaiyiṉ avai piṟakkum moḻiyai moḻimarapiṭai uṇarttip piṟappu
   uṇartta vēṇṭutaliṉ nūṉmarapiṉ piṉṉar vaiyātu itaṉai moḻimarapiṉ piṉṉar
   vaittār.
   iccūttiram eḻuttukaḷiṉatu potuppiṟavi ittuṇai nilaikkaḷattu niṉṟu
   pulappaṭu meṉkiṉṟatu.
   --------------------
   (1) eḻuttai piṟappittaṟku iṭamum uṟuppum eṉa iraṇṭu vēṇṭum. eḻuttiṉ
       iṉattaṉmaiyait tōṟṟum neñcu toṇṭai mutaliyavai iṭam; taṉittaṉi
       eḻuttait tōṟṟum nā pal mutaliyavai uṟuppu. mūkkait tolkāppiyar
       uṟuppeṉṟār; naṉṉūlār iṭameṉṟār. iruvarum talaiyaiōr iṭamākak
       koṇṭaṉar. iḵtu uṭal nūlukkum oli nūlukkum poruntuvatāyat
       tōṉṟavillai.

   i - ḷ: ellā eḻuttum piṟappiṉ ākkañ colluṅkālai -- tamiḻeḻuttu
   ellāvaṟṟiṟkum āciriyaṉ kūṟiya piṟappiṉatu tōṟṟaravai yāṅ kūṟumiṭattu,
   untimutalāt tōṉṟi muntu vaḷi -- koppūḻaṭiyākat tōṉṟi muntukiṉṟa
   utāṉaṉeṉṉuṅ kāṟṟu, talaiyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilaii --
   talaiyiṉkaṇṇum miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum neñciṉkaṇṇum nilaipeṟṟu, pallum itaḻum
   nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟai nilaiyāṉ uṟuppuṟu -- pallum
   itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamu meṉṟa aintuṭaṉē akkāṟṟu, niṉṟa talaiyum
   miṭaṟum neñcuṅ kūṭa eṭṭākiya muṟaimaiyaiyuṭaiya taṉmaiyōṭu kūṭiya
   uṟuppukkaḷōṭu oṉṟuṟṟu, amaiya – iṅṅaṉam amaitalāṉē, vēṟuvēṟu iyala –
   avveḻuttukkaḷatu tōṟṟaravu vēṟu vēṟu palappaṭa vaḻaṅkutalaiyuṭaiya,
   kāṭciyāṉa nāṭi neṟippaṭa - ataṉai yaṟivāṉ ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyilē
   maṉampaṭa, tiṟampaṭat teriyum - appiṟappu vēṟu pāṭukaḷellāṅ kūṟupaṭa
   viḷaṅkum; e-ṟu.
   colluṅkālai vaḷi nilaipeṟṟu uṟuppukkaḷuṟṟu iṅṅaṉam amaitalāṉē avai
   vaḻaṅkutalaiyuṭaiya; avaṟṟiṉ vaḻakkam avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyilē maṉan tiṟappaṭat
   teriyumeṉak kūṭṭi uraittuk koḷka. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟavē muyaṟciyum muyaluṅ
   karuttāvum uṇmai peṟṟām. (1)
   ------------

3.2. uyireḻuttukkaḷ piṟakkumāṟu ellā uyirkaḷum potuvākap piṟakkum vakai

   84. avvaḻip
   paṉṉī ruyirum tannilai tiriyā
   miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷiyiṉ icaikkum.
   iḵtu uyireḻuttiṟkup potuppiṟavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: paṉṉīruyirun tannilai tiriyā - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyiruntattam māttirai
   tiriyāvāy, avvaḻip piṟanta - avvunti yiṭattup piṟanta, miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyiṉ
   icaikkum - miṭaṟṟiṉ kaṇ nilaipeṟṟa kāṟṟāṉ olikkum; e-ṟu.
   eṉavē kuṟṟiyalikaramuṅ kuṟṟiyalukaramun tannilai tiriyu meṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
   avveḻuttukkaḷaik kūṟi uṇarka. (2)
   ---------------
   a, ā piṟakkumāṟu
   85. avaṟṟuḷ,
   a ā āyiraṇ ṭaṅkān tiyalum.
   iḵtu avvuyirkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟappup piṟavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirkaḷuḷ, a ā āyiraṇṭu - akara
   ākāraṅkaḷākiya avviraṇṭum, aṅkāntu iyalum - aṅkāntu kūṟum muyaṟciyāṟ
   piṟakkum; e-ṟu.
   muyaṟci uyirkkiḻavaṉkaṇṇatu. a ā eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.

   100. melleḻut tāṟum piṟappi ṉākkañ
   colliya paḷḷi nilaiyiṉa vāyiṉu
   mūkkiṉ vaḷiyicai yāppuṟat tōṉṟum.
   itu melleḻuttiṟkuc ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu āṟum piṟappiṉ ākkañ colliya 2paḷḷi nilaiyiṉa
   āyiṉum - melleḻuttukkaḷ āṟun tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkañ colliya iṭattē
   nilai peṟṟaṉavāyiṉum, mūkkiṉ vaḷiyicai yāppuṟat tōṉṟum - ōcai kūṟuṅkāl
   mūkkiṉkaṇ uḷatākiya vaḷiyiṉicaiyāṉ yāppuṟat tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
   avai aṅṅaṉamātal kūṟik kāṇka. yāppuṟa eṉṟataṉāṉ iṭaiyiṉattiṟku
   miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyum valliṉattiṟkut talaivaḷiyuṅ koḷka. (18)

                          3.2. paḷḷi - iṭam.

   101. cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat
   tērntuveḷip paṭutta vēṉai mūṉṟun
   tattañ cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi
   yotta kāṭciyiṟ ṟammiyal piyalum.
   itu cārpiṟṟōṟṟaṅkaḷ piṟakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   itaṉ poruḷ: cārntu variṉ allatu - cila eḻuttukkaḷaic cārntu
   tōṉṟiṉallatu, tamakku iyalpu ila eṉa – tamakkeṉat tōṉṟutaṟku
   ōriyalpilaveṉṟu, tērntu veḷippaṭutta tammiyalpu mūṉṟum - ārāyntu
   veḷippaṭukkappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ tammuṭaiya piṟappiyalpu mūṉṟiṉaiyuṅ
   kūṟuṅkāl, tattañ cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi iyalum - tattamakku uriya
   cārpākiya meykaḷatu ciṟappup piṟappiṭattē piṟattalōṭu porunti naṭakkum,
   ēṉai otta kāṭciyiṉ iyalum - oḻinta āytan tamakkup poruntiṉa
   neñcuvaḷiyāṟ piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
   kāṭci yeṉṟatu neñciṉai. kēṇmiyā nāku nuntai eṉavum eḵku eṉavum varum.
   āytattiṟkuc cārpiṭaṅ 'kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar' (eḻu - 38) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār.
   iṉi āytan talaivaḷiyāṉum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉum piṟakku meṉpārum uḷar.
   moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ
   aḷapeṭaiyum uyirmeyyun tammai ākkiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappiṭamē iṭamāka
   varumeṉṟu uṇarka. (19)
   ----------
   102. ellā veḻuttum veḷippaṭak kiḷantu
   colliya paḷḷi yeḻutaru vaḷiyiṟ
   piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi yuṟaḻcci vārat
   takatteḻu vaḷiyicai yariṟapa nāṭi
   yaḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟaittē
   yaḵtiva ṇuvalā teḻuntupuṟat ticaikku
   meyteri vaḷiyicai yaḷavunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē.
   iḵtu eḻuttukkaṭam piṟappiṟkup puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   itaṉ poruḷ: ellā veḻuttuṅ kiḷantu veḷippaṭa – āciriyaṉ
   ellāveḻuttukkaḷum piṟakkumāṟu muntunūṟkaṇṇē kūṟi veḷippaṭukkaiyiṉālē,
   colliya paḷḷi piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi - yāṉum avvāṟē kūṟiya eṇvakai nilattum
   piṟakkiṉṟa piṟappōṭē avveḻuttukkaḷaik kūṟumiṭattu, eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ
   uṟaḻccivārattiṉ aḷapu kōṭal - yāṉ kūṟiyavāṟu aṉṟi untiyil tōṉṟuṅ
   kāṟṟiṉatu tiritaruṅ kūṟṟiṉkaṇṇē māttirai kūṟik kōṭalum, akattu
   eḻuvaḷiyicai aril tapa nāṭik kōṭal - mūlātārattil eḻukiṉṟa
   kāṟṟiṉōcaiyaik kuṟṟamaṟa nāṭik kōṭalum, antaṇar maṟaittē - pārppāratu
