   vētattu uḷatē ; annilaimai āṇṭu uṇarka, aḵtu ivaṇ nuvalātu - aṅṅaṉam
   kōṭalai īṇṭuk kūṟalākāmaiyiṉ in nūṟkaṭ kūṟātē, eḻuntu puṟattu icaikkum
   - untiyiṟṟōṉṟip puṟattē 1pulappaṭṭu olikkum, mey teri vaḷiyicai aḷavu
   nuvaṉṟiciṉē - poruṭeriyuṅ kāṟṟiṉatu tuṇiviṟkē yāṉ māttirai kūṟiṉēṉ;
   avaṟṟiṉatu māttiraiyai uṇarka eṉṟavāṟu.
   itaṉai iraṇṭu cūttiramākkiyum uraippa.
   itu piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟaleṉṉum uttikku iṉam. eṉṉai? untiyil eḻunta kāṟṟiṉaik
   kūṟupaṭutti māttirai kūṭṭik kōṭalum mūlātāram mutalākak kāṟṟeḻumāṟu
   kūṟalum vētattiṟku uḷateṉṟu iv vāciriyar kūṟi am matampaṟṟi avar
   koḷvatōr payaṉ iṉṟeṉṟaliṉ. untiyil eḻunta kāṟṟu muṉṉart talaikkaṭ ceṉṟu
   piṉṉar miṭaṟṟilē vantu piṉṉar neñcilē niṟṟalai uṟaḻcci vārattu eṉṟār.
   akatteḻuvaḷi yeṉavē mūlātāra meṉpatu
   peṟṟām.
   iṉ cāriyaiyai attuc cāriyaiyōṭu kūṭṭuka. ēkāran tēṟṟam.
   meyterivaḷiyeṉavē 2poruṭeriyā muṟkum vīḷaiyum muyaṟciyāṉāmeṉiṉum
   poruṭeriyāmaiyiṉ avai kaṭiyappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, collap piṟantu coṟku
   uṟuppām ōcaiyai ivar eḻutteṉṟu vēṇṭuvareṉa uṇarka;
   3 'nilaiyum vaḷiyum muyaṟciyu mūṉṟu
   miyala naṭappa teḻutteṉap paṭumē.'
   eṉṟārākaliṉ. (20)
   ------------
   1. pulappaṭal - cevikkup pulappaṭal.
       2. poruḷ teriyā eṉpataṟku eḻuttākiya poruḷ teriyāta eṉpatu poruḷ.
       iṉṟēl, muṟkum vīḷaiyum poruḷuṇarttumēṉum eṉṟu ivar muṟkūṟiyatōṭu
       māṟupaṭum.
       3. nilai eṉṟatu iṭattai, vaḷi eṉṟatu kāṟṟai. muyaṟci eṉṟatu
       muyaṟcip piṟappai.

   piṟappiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
   -----------

                               4. puṇariyal
         (eḻuttukkaḷiṉ puṇarcci yilakkaṇam uṇarttuvatu.)
                    4.1. moḻikaḷiṉ mutalum īṟum

   moḻikku mutalilum iṟutiyilum varum eḻuttukkaḷ
   103. mūṉṟutalai yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ
   iraṇṭutalai yiṭṭa mutalā kirupaḵtu
   aṟunāṉ kīṟṟoṭu neṟiniṉ ṟiyalum
   ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum
   meyyē uyireṉṟu āyī riyala.
   eṉpatu cūttiram. moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya moḻikaḷaip potuvakaiyāṟ puṇarkkum
   muṟaimai uṇarttiṉamaiyiṟ puṇariya leṉṟu ivvōttiṟkup peyarāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu
   muṟaimai yeṉṟatu mēṟ ceykai yōttukkaḷuṭ puṇartaṟku uriyavāka īṇṭuk
   kūṟiya karuvikaḷai.
   iccūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttum
   moḻikku īṟā meḻuttum ittuṇai yeṉṟalum, ellā moḻikkum īṟum mutalum
   meyyum uyiru mallatu illaiyeṉṟu varaiyaṟuttalum, īṟum mutalumāka eḻuttu
   nāṟpattāṟu uḷavō veṉṟu aiyuṟṟārkku eḻuttu muppattu mūṉṟumē aṅṅaṉam īṟum
   mutalumāy niṟpateṉṟu aiyamaṟuttalum nutaliṟṟu.
   i-ḷ: mutal iraṇṭu talaiyiṭṭa irupaḵtu īṟu aṟunāṉkākum mūṉṟu talaiyiṭṭa
   muppatiṟṟeḻuttiṉoṭu-moḻikku mutalā meḻuttu iraṇṭai muṭiyilē yiṭṭa
   irupaḵtum moḻikku īṟāmeḻuttu irupattunāṉku mākiṉṟa mūṉṟai muṭiyilē
   yiṭṭa muppatākiya eḻuttukkaḷōṭē, neṟiniṉṟu iyalum ellā moḻikkum-vaḻakku
   neṟikkaṇiṉṟu naṭakkum mūvakai moḻikkum meyyē uyireṉṟu āyīriyala
   iṟutiyum mutalum-meyyum uyiru meṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu
   iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya eḻuttukkaḷē īṟum mutalum āvaṉa; e-ṟu.
   irupattiraṇṭu mutalāvaṉa paṉṉīruyirum oṉpatu uyirmeyyum moḻimutaṟ
   kuṟṟiyalukaramumām. irupattunāṉku īṟāvaṉa paṉṉīruyirum patiṉoru
   puḷḷiyum īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramumām. meyyai muṟkūṟiṉār nālvakaip
   puṇarcciyum meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu uyirkkaṇ nikaḻā veṉṟaṟku.
   e-ṭu: maram eṉa meymutalum meyyīṟum, viḷa eṉa mey mutalum uyi uyirīṟum,
   āl eṉa uyirmutalum meyyīṟum, viḷa eṉa mey mutalum uyirīṟumām.
   moḻiyākkam iyalpum vikāramu meṉa iraṇṭām. uyir tāmē niṉṟu mutalum īṟu
   mātal iyalpu. avai moyyōṭu kūṭi niṉṟu aṅṅaṉamātal vikāram (1)
   -------
   moḻiyiṟuti meykaḷ puḷḷi peṟutal
   104. avaṟṟuḷ
   meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal
   itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl taṉimey mutalāvāṉ ceṉṟataṉai vilakkaliṉ eytiyatu
   vilakkiṟṟu.
   i-ḷ: avaṟṟuḷ-muṟkūṟiya meyyum uyirumeṉṟa iraṇṭiṉuḷ, meyyīṟu ellām
   puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal-meymoḻikku īṟāyavai yellām puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟkum; e-ṟu
   eṉavē moḻikku mutalāyiṉavai yellām puḷḷi yiḻantu uyirēṟi
   niṟku meṉṟārāyiṟṟu. iṉṉum īṟṟu mey puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟataṉāṉē
   uyirmutaṉmoḻi tammēl vantāl avai uyirēṟa iṭaṅ koṭuttu niṟku meṉpatūuṅ
   kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. ivviti muṟkūṟiyataṉṟōveṉiṉ āṇṭut taṉimey patiṉeṭṭum
   puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟku meṉṟum avaitām uyirēṟuṅkāṟ puḷḷi yiḻantu niṟku meṉṟuṅ
   kūṟiṉār; īṇṭu meymutal meyyīṟeṉap poruḷuraikka vēṇṭiṉamaiyiṉ moḻimutaṉ
   meykaḷum puḷḷipeṟumō veṉṟu aiyuṟṟa aiyam akaṟṟak kūṟiṉā reṉṟa uṇarka.
   maram eṉap puḷḷipeṟṟu niṉṟatu ariteṉa vantuḻi maramariteṉṟu ēṟi
   muṭintavāṟu kāṇka. (2)
   ---------
   kuṟṟukaram oṟṟeḻut tottal
   105. kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa.
   itu muṉṉarp puḷḷiyiṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittiyalā teṉṟa meykku eytuvikkiṉṟa
   karuviyai etirata pōṟṟi uyirkkum eytuvikkiṉṟa karuvic cūttiram.
   i-ḷ: kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa-īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum
   puḷḷiyīṟupōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukku1 meṉṟukūṟuvar pulavar; e-ṟu
   --------
   1 "kuṟṟiyalukaram...moḻipa" eṉpataṟku kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟupōlap
       puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatē pōnta poruḷām. "meyyiṉiyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu
       nilayala...." ekāra okarattiyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē" eṉṟu āciriyar māṭṭiya
       viṭattum ekara okaram puḷḷipeṟu meṉpatē karuttātal teḷivām. ātalāl
       iḷam pūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷiyīṟupōla
       uyirēṟa iṭaṅ koṭukkumeṉṟu uraikūṟuvatu māṭṭēṟṟiṟku muṟṟilum
       poruntātu. ituvē pērāciriyarkkum civañāṉamuṉivarkkum caṅkayāp
       puṭaiyārkkum karuttām. "tollaivaṭiviṉ...puḷḷi" eṉṉum naṉṉūl
       nūṟpāvuraiyil "āṇṭu eṉṟa mikaiyāṉē...kuṟṟukarak
       kuṟṟikaraṅkaḷukkumēṟ puḷḷi koṭuppārum uḷareṉak koḷka" eṉṟu
       mayilainātar kūṟuvatuṅ kāṇka.

   immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭaic cēṟal puḷḷi peṟāmaiyiṉ. aṅṅaṉam uyirēṟuṅkāṟ
   kuṟṟukaraṅ keṭṭuppōka niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl uyirēṟiṟṟeṉṟu koḷḷaṟka.
   nākariteṉpuḻi muṉṉark kuṟṟukara vōcaiyum piṉṉar uyirōcaiyum peṟṟu
   avviraṇṭuṅkūṭi niṉṟallatu apporuḷuṇartta lākāmaiyiṉ, iḵtu uyirōṭuṅ kūṭi
   niṟku meṉṟār. (3)
   ------------
   uyir meyyīṟu uyirīṟṟatu,
   106. uyirmey īṟum uyirīṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
   itu "meyyē yuyireṉṟāyīriyala" (eḻu-103) eṉṟa uyirkkaṇ nikaḻvatōr aiyam
   akaṟṟiyatu; uyirmey yeṉpatōr īṟu uṇṭēṉum atu puṇarkkappaṭātu, atuvum
   uyirāyē aṭaṅkumeṉṟaliṉ.
   i-ḷ: uyirmeyyīṟum-uyirmey moḻiyiṉatu īṟṟiṉkaṇ niṉṟatum,
   uyirīṟṟiyatē-uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpai yuṭaittu;e-ṟu
   ummaiyāṉ iṭainiṉṟa uyirmeyyum uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpai yuṭaittu eṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
   ummai eccavummai. īṟṟiṉum iṭaiyiṉum niṉṟaṉa. uyiruḷ aṭaṅku meṉavē mut
   niṉṟaṉa meyyuḷ aṭaṅku meṉṟār. ita ṉāṉē mēl viḷa eṉṟāṟ pōlum. uyirmeyka
   ḷellām akara vīṟeṉṟu puṇarkkumāṟu uṇarka. varaku itaṉai mēl uyirttoṭar
   moḻi yeṉpa. muṉṉar "meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu" (eḻu-18) eṉṟatu ōreḻuttiṟ keṉṟu
   uṇarka. ittuṇaiyum moḻimarapiṉ oḻipu kūṟiṟṟu. (4)
   ---------
   puṇarccikkuriya irumoḻikaḷiṉ iyalpu
   107. uyiriṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
   uyiriṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum
   meyyiṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
   meyyiṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyumeṉṟu
   ivveṉa aṟiyak kiḷakkuṅ kālai
   niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyeṉṟu
   āyī riyala puṇarnilaic cuṭṭē,
   itu mēṟkūṟum puṇarccikaḷellām irumoḻip puṇarcciyallatu illai yeṉpatūum
   aḵtu eḻuttuvakaiyāṉ nāṉkāmeṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uyiriṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum-uyir taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ colliṉmuṉ
   uyir mutalākiya moḻi varum iṭamum, uyiriṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum-uyir
   taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ colliṉ muṉṉar mey mutalākiya moḻi varum iṭamum,
   meyyiṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum-mey taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ volliṉ muṉṉar
   uyirmutalākiya moḻi varum iṭamum, meyyiṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum- mey
   taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ colliṉ muṉṉar mey mutalākiya moḻivarum iṭamum, eṉṟu
   puṇarnilaiccuṭṭu-eṉṟu collappaṭṭa oṉṟeṉōṭoṉṟukūṭum nilaimaiyākiya
   karuttiṉkaṇ, ivveṉa aṟiyak kiḷakkuṅkālai -avaṟṟai ittuṇai yeṉa
   varaiyaṟaiyai ellārum aṟiya yāṅ kūṟuṅkālattu, niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru
   kiḷavi yeṉṟu āyīriyala-muṉṉar niṟuttappaṭṭa collum ataṉai muṭittalaik
   kuṟittu varuñcollum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya; e-ṟu
   eṉavē, nāṉku vakaiyāṉuṅ kūṭuṅkāl irumoḻi yallatu puṇarcciyiṉṟu
   eṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
   e-ṭu: āvuṇṭu āvalitu ālilai ālvīḻntatu eṉa muṟaiyē kāṇka. viḷaviṉaik
   kuṟaittāṉ eṉṟavaḻic cāriyaiyum urupum nilai moḻiyāyē niṟkumeṉpatu nōkki
   ataṉai niṟuttacolleṉṟum muṭikkuñ collaik kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyeṉṟum
   kūṟiṉār, itaṉāṉē nilai moḻiyum varumoḻiyuṅ kūṟiṉār. muṉṉar "meyyē
   yuyir" (eḻu-103) eṉṟatu oru moḻikku , iḵtu irumoḻikkeṉṟu uṇarka. (5)
   -----------

                         4.2. puṇartaliṉ iyalpu

   moḻi puṇariyalpu nāṉku
   108. avaṟṟuḷ
   niṟutta colliṉ īṟā keḻuttoṭu
   kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻut tiyaiyap
   peyaroṭu peyaraip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
   peyaroṭu toḻilaip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
   toḻiloṭu peyaraip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
   toḻiloṭu toḻilaip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
   mūṉṟē tiripiṭaṉ oṉṟē iyalpeṉa
   āṅkan nāṉkē moḻipuṇar iyalpē.
   itu muṉṉar eḻuttuvakaiyāṉ nāṉku puṇarcci eytiya iruvakaic colluñ
   colvakaiyāṉum nāṉkāku meṉpatūum aṅṅaṉam puṇarvatu colluñ collum aṉṟu
   eḻuttum eḻuttumeṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: avaṟṟuḷ-nilaimoḻi varumoḻi yeṉṟavaṟṟuḷ, niṟutta colliṉ īṟāku
   eḻuttoṭu kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻuttu iyaiya-muṉṉar niṟuttappaṭṭa
   colliṉatu īṟākiṉṟa eḻuttōṭē ataṉai muṭikkak karuti varukiṉṟa colliṉatu
   mutaleḻuttupporunta, peyarōṭu peyaraip puṇarkkuṅ kālum-peyarccollōṭu
   peyarc collaik kūṭṭum iṭattum, payaroṭu toḻilaip puṇarkkuṅkālum-peyarc
   collōṭu toḻiṟcollaik kūṭṭum iṭattum, toḻiloṭu peyaraip puṇarkkuṅkālum
   -toḻiṟ collōṭu peyarccollaik kūṭṭum iṭattum, toḻiloṭu toḻilaip
   puṇarkkuṅkālum-toḻiṟcollōṭu toḻiṟcollaik kūṭṭum iṭattum, mūṉṟē tiripu
   iṭaṉ oṉṟē iyalpeṉa āṅku annāṉkē-tiriyum iṭam mūṉṟu iyalpu oṉṟu eṉṟu
   muntu nūliṟ kūṟiya annāṉku ilakkaṇamumē, moḻi puṇar iyalpu-īṇṭu moḻikaḷ
   tammuṭ kūṭum ilakkaṇam; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: cāttaṉkai cāttaṉuṇṭāṉ vantāṉcāttaṉ vantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa muṟaiyē
   kāṇka. ivai nāṉku iṉattōṭuṅ kūṭap patiṉāṟām. iṭaiyum uriyun tāmāka
   nillāmaiyiṟ peyarviṉaiyē kūṟiṉār. iṭaiccollum uriccollum puṇarkkuñ
   ceykaippaṭṭuḻip puṇarppuc ciṟupāṉmai. peyarppeyarum oṭṭuppeyaru meṉa
   iraṇṭu vakaippaṭum peyar. terinilaiviṉaiyuṅ kuṟippuviṉaiyu meṉa iraṇṭu
   vakaip paṭuntoḻil. nilaimoḻiyatu īṟṟeḻuttu muṉṉarp piṟantu keṭṭuppōka
   varumoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttup piṉ piṟantu keṭṭamaiyāṉ muṟaiyē piṟantu
   keṭuvaṉa oruṅkuniṉṟu puṇarumā ṟiṉmaiyiṟ puṇarcci yeṉpatu oṉṟiṉṟām piṟa
   veṉiṉ, accoṟkaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟōruṅ kēṭkiṉṟōrum avvōcaiyai
   iṭaiyaṟavupaṭāmai uḷḷattiṉkaṇṇē uṇarvarātaliṉ avvōcai kēṭiṉṟi
   uḷḷattiṉkaṇ nilaipeṟṟup puṇarntaṉavēyām. ākavē piṉṉark kaṇkūṭākap1
   puṇarkkiṉṟa puṇarcciyum muṭintaṉavēyā meṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi muyaṟkōṭu
   uṇṭeṉṟāl atu kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi yaṉmaiyiṟ puṇarkkappaṭātu.2 itutāṉ iṉ
   ṟeṉṟāṟ puṇarkkap-paṭumeṉṟu3 uṇarka. (6)
   -------
   1 kaṇkūṭāka -kaṭpulaṉāka varivaṭivil
       2 muyaṟkōṭu uṇṭu eṉṟāl poruḷiṉmaiyiṉ, uṇṭu eṉpatu muṭikkuñ
       collaṉṟu
       3 muyaṟkōṭu iṉṟu eṉṟāl poruḷuṇmaiyiṉ, iṉṟu eṉpatu muṭikkuñ col
       lākum. ākavē, puṇaruñ coṟkaṭku aṇmainilai maṭṭum pōtātu. poruṭ
       poruttamum vēṇṭum eṉpatu karuttu. uraiyil itu eṉṟatu muyaṟ
       kōṭṭai.

   ----------
   puṇarcciyil mūvakait tiripukaḷ
   109. avaitām
   meyppiṟi tātal mikutal kuṉṟaleṉṟu
   ivveṉa moḻipa tiriyu māṟē
   itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu tiripum āmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: avaitān tiriyumāṟu-muṉṉart tiripeṉṟu kūṟiya avaitān tirintu
   puṇarum neṟiyai, mey piṟitalāl mikutal kuṉṟa leṉṟu ivveṉa moḻipa-mey
   vēṟupaṭutal mikutal kuṉṟa leṉṟu kūṟappaṭum immūṉṟu kūṟṟai yuṭaiya
   veṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar: e-ṟu
   immūṉṟum allātatu iyalpāmeṉṟu uṇarka. ivai vikaṟpikkap patiṉāṟu
   utāraṇamām. maṭkuṭam malaittalai maravēr ivai peyarōṭu peyar puṇarnta
   mūṉṟu tiripu. maṇmalai eṉpatu iyalpu. coṟ kēṭṭāṉ palākkuṟaittāṉ
   maranaṭṭāṉ ivai peyaroṭu toḻil puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. koṟṟaṉ vantāṉ
   iḵtu iyalpu. vantāṉāṟ cāttaṉ, koṭāpporuḷ. ōṭunākam ivai toḻiloṭu peyar
   puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. vantāṉ cāttaṉ iḵtu iyalpu. vantāṟ koḷḷum, pāṭap
   pōyiṉāṉ, cāñāṉṟāṉ ivai toḻiloṭu toḻilpuṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. vantāṉ
   koṇṭāṉ iḵtu iyalpu. mūṉṟu tiripeṉṉātu iṭaṉeṉṟa taṉāṉ oru puṇarccikkaṇ
   mūṉṟum oruṅkēyum varappeṟu meṉṟu uṇarka. makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ iḵtu aṅṅaṉam
   vantavāṟu makara īṟṟu "nāṭpeyark kiḷavi" (eḻu-331) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ
   uṇarka. iraṇṭu varuvaṉavuṅ kāṇka. (7)
   ----------
   aṭai moḻikaḷōṭu puṇartal
   110. niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum
   aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilaik kuriya.
   itu nilaimoḻi aṭaiyaṭuttum varumoḻi aṭaiyaṭuttum avvirumoḻiyum
   aṭaiyaṭuttum puṇarumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum-nilai moḻiyāka niṟuttiṉa
   collum ataṉaik kuṟittuvaruñ collum, aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilaikku
   uriya-tāmē paṇarātu orōvōr col aṭaiyaṭuttu variṉum iraṇṭum aṭaiyaṭuttu
   variṉum puṇar nilaimaikku uriya; e-ṟu
   aṭaiyāvaṉa, ummait tokaiyum1 irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput tokai2yumām.
   -------
   1 paṉṉiraṇṭu kaṇ eṉṉumpōtu, paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉpatu pattum iraṇṭum eṉap
       poruḷpaṭuvatāl ummaittokai.
       2 patiṉāyirattoṉṟu eṉṉumpōtu, patiṉāyiram eṉpatu pattākiya āyiram
       eṉap
       poruḷpaṭutaliṉ paṇputtokaiyām. āṉāl iru peyaroṭṭup paṇput
       tokaiyaṉṟu. irupeyaroṭṭāyiṉ pattu āyiram eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ taṉittaṉi
       niṉṟum oru poruḷē yuṇarttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam uṇarttāmaiyum,
       paṉaimaram eṉṉum irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokaiyil irucollum taṉittaṉi
       orē poruḷai yuṇarttuvataiyum nōkkuka.

   e-ṭu: patiṉāyirattoṉṟu āyirattorupaḵtu patiṉāyirat tiru paḵtu eṉa
   varum. ivvaṭaikaḷ orucollē yām. vēṟṟumait tokaiyum uvamat tokaiyum
   muṭiyap1 paṇput tokaiyum viṉaittokaiyum
   piḷantu muṭiyāmaiyiṉ2 oru collēyām. aṉmoḻit tokaiyun3 taṉakku vēṟōr
   muṭipiṉmaiyiṉ oru collēyām. it tokaic coṟkaḷellām aṭaiyāy varuṅ kālattu
   oru collāy varu meṉṟu uṇarka. uṇṭacāttāṉ vantāṉ, uṇṭu vantāṉ cāttāṉ
   eṉpaṉavum4 oru collē yām. (8)
   -----
   1 muṭiya-iru collāyp piḷantu muṭiya. marakkiḷai eṉṉam vēṟṟumait tokai
       maram+kiḷai eṉṟum, muttuppal eṉṉum uvamaittokai muttu+ pal eṉṟum
       piḷantu muṭital kāṇka.
       2 centāmarai eṉṉum paṇputtokaiyum kolkaḷiṟu eṉṉum viṉaittokaiyum
       tiṭṭamāka allatu teḷivāka iru collāyp piḷantu muṭiyāmaiyum oru
       coṟṟaṉamaippaṭṭu niṟṟaluṅ kāṇka.
       3 aṉamoḻittokai eṉṟum veḷippaṭāta collāy ēṉait tokaikaḷiṉ īṟṟil
       piṟattalāṉum orucoṟṟaṉamaippaṭṭu niṟṟalāṉum oru collām.
       4 uṇṭa cāttaṉāy vantāṉ , uṇṭu vantavaṉākiya cāttaṉ eṉap poruḷpaṭum
       pōtu ivai oru coṟṟaṉamaippaṭum.

   -----------
   marūu moḻip puṇarcci.
   111. maruviṉ tokuti mayaṅkiyal moḻiyum
   uriyavai uḷavē puṇarnilaic cuṭṭē.
   itu marūuccoṟkaḷum puṇarccipeṟu meṉpatūum niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittu varu
   kiḷaviyumāyp poruḷiyaipillaṉavum puṇarcci peṟṟāṟ pōla niṟku meṉpatūum
   uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: maru moḻiyum-iru vakaiyāki maruviya coṟkaḷum, iṉṟokuti mayaṅkiyaṉ
   moḻiyum5 –cevikkiṉitākac coṟṟiraḷiṭattu niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru
   kiḷaviyumāy oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟu poruḷuṇarttātu pirintu piṉṉarc ceṉṟu
   oṭṭip poruḷuṇartta mayaṅkutal iyaṉṟa coṟkaḷum, puṇarnilaic cuṭṭu
   uriyavai uḷa-puṇarum nilaimaik karuttiṉkaṇ uriyaṉa uḷa ; e-ṟu
   -----
   5 innūṟpāviṉ mutalaṭiyai maruviṉ tokutiyum mayaṅkiyaṉ moḻiyum eṉap
       pirittup poruḷ koḷvatē ciṟantatu. marumoḻiyum iṉatokuti mayaṅkiyal
       moḻiyum eṉap pirippatu attuṇaic ciṟantataṉṟu.

   moḻiyu meṉpataṉai maruveṉpataṉōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. iṉṟokuti yeṉṟār, pāveṉṉum
   uṟuppu nikaḻap poruḷoṭṭāmaṟ cāṉṟōr coṟkaḷaic cērttaliṉ.
   e-ṭu: muṉṟil mīkaṇ ivai ilakkalaṇattōṭu poruntiya maru. ilakkaṇam allā
   maru "vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum" (eḻu-483) eṉpuḻik
   kāṭṭutum. iṉi,
   "irumpu tirittaṉṉa māyiru maruppiṟ
   paralaval aṭaiya iralai teṟippa." (akam-4)
   eṉpuḻi maruppiṉiralaiyeṉṟu oṭṭi iraṇṭāvataṉ tokaiyāyp poruḷ tantu
   "puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ ṉuyirtaṉit tiyalā" (eḻu-183) eṉṟu uyirēṟi muṭintu
   mayaṅki niṉṟatu. āyiṉ, maruppiṟparaleṉṟu mey piṟitāy oṭṭi niṉṟavā
   ṟeṉṉai yeṉiṉ maruppiṉaiyuṭaiya paraleṉa vēṟṟumait tokaip poruḷ
   uṇarttāmaiyiṉ aḵtu acceyyuṭku iṉṉōcai nikaḻtaṟkup pakarattiṉ muṉṉar
   niṉṟa ṉakaram ṟakaramāyt tirintu niṉṟa tuṇaiyēyāyp puṇarccip payaṉiṉṟi
   niṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam puṇarcci yeytiṉāṟ pōla māṭṭilakkaṇattiṉ kaṇṇum
   moḻimāṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum niṟṟal coṟku iyalpeṉṟataṟku aṉṟē āciriyar iṉṟokuti
   yeṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka. "karuṅkā lōmaik kāṇpiṉ peruñciṉai" (akam-2) eṉpuḻi
   ōmaicciṉai yeṉṟu oṭṭi ōmaiyiṉatu ciṉai yeṉap poruḷ
   tarukiṉṟatu iṉṉōcai tarutaṟkuk kakaravoṟṟu mikkuk kāṇpi ṉeṉ
   pataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟatu. "teyva mālvarait tirumuṉi yaruḷāl"
   (cilap-3) eṉpuḻit teyvavarai yeṉṟu oṭṭit teyvattaṉmai yuṭaiyavarai
   yeṉap poruḷ tarukiṉṟatu. iṉṉōcai tarutaṟku māleṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟpōlak
   kuṟaintu niṉṟatu. mūṉṟu tiripum vantavāṟu kāṇka. iṉi eccattiṉkaṇṇum
   "poṉṉōṭaip pukaraṇinutal
       tuṉṉaruntiṟal kamaḻkaṭāat
       teyiṟupaṭaiyāka eyiṟkataviṭāak
       kayiṟupiṇikkoṇṭa kaviḻmaṇimaruṅkiṟ
       peruṅkaiyāṉai irumpiṭarttalaiyiruntu
       maruntilkūṟṟat taruntoḻilcāyā" (puṟam-3)

   eṉa māṭṭāy oṭṭiniṉṟatu1 kayiṟupiṇikkoṇṭa eṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟu
   oṟṟaṭuttatu iṉṉōcai peṟutaṟku . piṟa cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ ivvāṟum piṟavāṟum
   puṇarcci yilvaḻip puṇarcci peṟṟāṟ pōla niṟpaṉa ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ituvē
   ōttākak koḷa;ka, (9)
   --------
   1 māṭṭāy oṭṭi niṉṟatu - eyiṟupaṭaiyāka eyiṟkataviṭāa maruntila kūṟṟat
       taruntoḻil cāyā eṉakkoṇṭu kūṭṭāy icaintu niṉṟatu.

   ---------
   iruvakaip puṇarcci
   112. vēṟṟumai kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi nilaiyum
   vēṟṟumai alvaḻip puṇarmoḻi nilaiyum
   eḻuttē cāriyai āyiru paṇpiṉ
   oḻukkal valiya puṇaruṅ kālai.
   itu mūvakaittiripuṉuḷ mikkup puṇarum puṇarcci iruvakaiya eṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: puṇaruṅkālai-nālvakaip puṇarcciyuḷ mikka puṇarcci puṇaruṅ kālattu,
   vēṟṟumai kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi nilaiyum-vēṟṟumaip poruṇmaiyiṉaik kuṟitta
   puṇar moḻiyiṉatu taṉmaiyum, vēṟṟumai alvaḻip puṇarmoḻi
   nilaiyum-vēṟṟumaiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattup puṇarum moḻiyiṉatu taṉmaiyum,
   eḻuttē cāriyai āyiru paṇpiṉ oḻukkal valiya-eḻuttu mikutaluñ cāriyai
   mikutalumākiya avviraṇṭu kuṇattiṉāṉuñ cellutalait tamakku
   valiyākavuṭaiya; e-ṟu
   eṉavē ēṉaip puṇarccikaḷukku ittuṇai vēṟupāṭu iṉṟeṉa uṇarka.
   e-ṭu: viḷaṅkōṭu iḵtu eḻuttup peṟṟatu. makaviṉ kai, itu cāriyai peṟṟatu.
   iṉi alvaḻikkaṇ viḷakkuṟitu, iḵtu eḻuttup peṟṟatu, paṉaiyiṉ kuṟai itu
   cāriyai peṟṟatu. itaṟkup paṉai kuṟainta teṉpatu poruḷām. iḵtu
   aḷavuppeyar. oḻukkal valiya eṉṟataṉāṉ ik kūṟiya iraṇṭum eḻuttuñ
   cāriyaiyum uṭaṉpeṟutaluṅ koḷka. avaṟṟuṭ kōṭeṉpatu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iraṇṭum
   peṟṟatu. kalattuk kuṟai yeṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ iraṇṭum peṟṟatu. itaṟkuk
   kalaṅ kuṟainta teṉpatu poruḷām. iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ ivviraṇṭum
   uṭaṉpeṟutaliṉṟu. alvaḻi muṟkūṟātatu vēṟṟumai yallātatu alvaḻi yeṉa
   vēṇṭutaliṉ. eḻuttuppēṟu yāppuṭaimaiyāṉum1 eḻuttiṉāṟ cāriyai yātalāṉum
   eḻuttu muṟkūṟiṉār. vēṟṟumai mēlaiccūttirattē kūṟukiṉṟār.
   alvaḻiyāvaṉa avvurupukaḷ tokkum virintum nillātu puṇarvaṉa. avai
   eḻuvāyvēṟṟumai āṟu payaṉilaiyōṭum2 puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, viḷivēṟṟumai
   taṉporuḷōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, muṟṟup peyarōṭum viṉaiyōṭum puṇarnta
   puṇarcciyum, peyareccamum viṉaiyeccamum peyarōṭum viṉaiyōṭum puṇarnta
   puṇarcciyum, uvamat tokaiyum ummait tokaiyum irupeyaroṭṭup
   paṇputtokaiyam iṭaiccollum uriccollum peyarōṭum viṉaiyōṭum puṇarnta
   puṇarcciyum, aṉ moḻittokai poruḷōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcciyum
   paṇaputtokaiyum viṉaittokaiyum virintuniṉṟavaḻip puṇarnta puṇarcciyu
   meṉa uṇarka.
   -----
   1. yāppu-vali. 2. āṟu payaṉilai-poruṇmai cuṭṭal, viyaṅkoḷa varutal,
       viṉainilai, yuraittal, viṉāviṟkēṟṟal, paṇpukoḷa varutal, peyarkoḷa
       varutal eṉpaṉa.

   ----------

                            4.3. urupu puṇarcci

   vēṟṟumai yurupukaḷ
   113. aioṭu kuiṉ atukaṇ eṉṉum
   avvā ṟeṉpa vēṟṟumai yurupē
   itu mēl vēṟṟumai yeṉappaṭṭa avaṟṟatu peyarum muṟaiyun tokaiyum
   uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: vēṟṟumaiyurupu-muṟkūṟiya vēṟṟumaiccoṟkaḷai, ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ
   eṉṉum avvāṟeṉpa-ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṟu colalappaṭum avvāṟu
   urupumeṉṟu colvar āciriyar; e-ṟu
   mēṟ collatikārattu eḻuvāyaiyum viḷiyaiyuṅ kūṭṭi vēṟṟumai eṭṭeṉpārākaliṉ
   ai mutaliya vēṟṟumaiyāṟun tokkum virintum perumpāṉmaiyum
   pulappaṭṭuniṉṟu peyarpporuḷaic ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliyaṉavāka vēṟupāṭu
   ceytu puṇarcci yeytuvikku meṉṟaṟku īṇṭu āṟeṉṟār. āṇṭu eḻuvāyum viḷiyuñ
   ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟun tammai vēṟupaṭuttup poruḷmāttiram
   uṇarttiniṉṟum viḷiyāy etirmukamākki niṉṟum iṅṅaṉañ ciṟu pāṉmaiyāyp
   pulappaṭa nillā vēṟupāṭu uṭaiyavēṉum, avaiyum oruvāṟṟāṉ vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉa
   veṉṟaṟku āṇṭu eṭṭeṉṟāreṉa uṇarka. (11)
   ----------
   valimutalurupu puṇarunilai
   114. valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumai yurupiṟ
   kolvaḻi oṟṟiṭai mikutal vēṇṭum.
   itu nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum urupiyalai nōkkiyatōr karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumaiyurupiṟku - valleḻuttu aṭiyāy niṉṟa
   nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum, olvaḻi oṟṟu iṭaimikutal vēṇṭum -
   poruntiyavaḻi valloṟṟāyiṉum melloṟṟāyiṉum iṭaikkaṇ mikkup puṇartalai
   virumpum āciriyaṉ; e-ṟu.
   varaiyātu oṟṟeṉavē valloṟṟum melloṟṟum peṟṟām.
   e-ṭu: maṇikku maṇikkaṇ tīkku tīkkaṇ maṉaikku maṉaikkaṇ eṉavum, vēykku
   vēykkaṇ ūrkku ūrkkaṇ pūḻkku pūḻkkaṇ eṉavum, uyirīṟu mūṉṟiṉum puḷḷiyīṟu
   mūṉṟiṉum perumpāṉmai valloṟṟu mikku varum. taṅkaṇ naṅkaṇ nuṅkaṇ eṅkaṇ
   eṉa mel loṟṟu mikkatu. ivaṟṟiṟku nilaimoḻi makarakkēṭu urupiyaliṟ
   kūṟupa. āṅkaṇ īṅkaṇ ūṅkaṇ eṉpaṉa cuṭṭeḻuttu nīṇṭu niṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku
   oṟṟukkēṭu
   kūṟutaṟku oṟṟiṉṟu.
   iṉi nāṉkaṉurupiṟku melloṟṟu mikāteṉṟu uṇarka.
   iṉi olvaḻi yeṉpataṉāṉ ēḻā murupiṉkaṇ nampikaṇ eṉa ikara īṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum
   naṅkaikaṇ eṉa aikāra īṟṟiṉkaṇṇun tāykaṇ eṉa yakara īṟṟiṉkaṉṇum
   aracarkaṇ eṉa rakara īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum oṟṟu mikāmai koḷka.
   iṉi meypiṟitātalai muṉṉē kūṟātu mikutalai muṟkūṟiya ataṉāṉē poṟku
   poṟkaṇ vēṟku vēṟkaṇ vāṭku vāṭkaṇ eṉattirintu muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. itaṉāṉē
   avaṉkaṇ avaḷkaṇ eṉa uyartiṇaip peyarkkaṇ ēḻaṉurupu iyalpāy varutaluṅ
   koḷka. ivaṟṟiṟkuk kuṉṟiya puṇarcci varumēṉuṅ koḷka.
   koṟṟikku koṟṟikaṇ kōtaikku kōtaikaṇ eṉa viravup peyarkkum itaṉāṉē
   koḷka. (12)
   ------------
   atu urupiṉ akaram keṭum
   115. āṟaṉ urupiṉ akarak kiḷavi
   īṟā kakaramuṉaik keṭutal vēṇṭum.
   iḵtu āṟāvataṟkut tokaimarapai nōkkiyatōr karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: āṟaṉurupiṉ akarakkiḷavi - atuveṉṉum āṟaṉurupiṉkaṇ niṉṟa
   akaramākiya eḻuttu, īṟāku akaramuṉaik keṭutal vēṇṭum - neṭumutal kuṟuku
   moḻikaṭku 'īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu nilaiyum' (eḻu - 161) eṉa
   vitittataṉāl
   uḷatākiya akarattiṉ muṉṉart tāṉ keṭutalai virumpum āciriyaṉ; e-ṟu.
   tamatu namatu ematu numatu taṉatu eṉatu niṉatu eṉa varum. itu
   nilaimoḻikku ōr akaram peṟumeṉa vitiyātu urupu akaram ēṟi muṭiyumeṉa
   vitittāl varuṅ kuṟṟam uṇṭō veṉiṉ, 'niṉava kūṟuva leṉava kēṇmati' (puṟam
   - 35) eṉṟāṟpōla āṟāvataṟku uriya akara urupiṉ muṉṉarum ōr akara
   eḻuttuppēṟu nilaimoḻikkaṇ varutaluḷatākak karutiṉārātaliṉ,
   āṟaṉurupiṟkum nāṉkaṉurupiṟkum potuvāka nilai moḻikkaṇ akarappēṟu
   vitittu, atuveṉṉum, orumaiyurupu vantāl āṇṭup peṟṟu niṉṟa akarattiṉ
   muṉṉar atu veṉpataṉkaṇ akaraṅkeṭuka veṉṟu īṇṭuk kūṟiṉārātaliṉ ataṟkuk
   kuṟṟam uṇṭeṉṟu uṇarka. (13)
   -----------
   vēṟṟumai yurupukaḷ niṟkumiṭam
   116. vēṟṟumai vaḻiya peyarpuṇar nilaiyē.
   itu vēṟṟumai peyarkkaṇ niṟkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: vēṟṟumai peyarvaḻiya - vēṟṟumaikaḷ peyariṉ piṉṉiṭattaṉavām,
   puṇarnilai - avaṟṟōṭu puṇarum nilaimaikkaṇ; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: cāttaṉai cāttaṉoṭu cāttaṟku cāttaṉiṉ cāttaṉatu cāttaṉkaṇ eṉa
   varum. maṟṟu itu 'kūṟiya muṟaiyiṉ' (co - 69) eṉṉum vēṟṟumaiyōttiṟ
   cūttirattāṟ peṟutumeṉiṉ, peyaroṭu peyaraip puṇarttal mutaliya nālvakaip
   puṇarcciyiṉaiyum vēṟṟumai alvaḻi yeṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakkutaliṉ toḻiṟ piṉṉum
   urupu varumeṉa eytiyataṉai vilakkutaṟku īṇṭuk kūṟiṉā reṉka. āyiṉ
   ivvilakkutal viṉaiyiyaṉ mutaṟ cūttirattāṟ peṟutumeṉiṉ, atu
   mutaṉilaiyaik kūṟiṟṟeṉpatu āṇṭu uṇarka. (14)
   ----------
   peyarkaḷiṉ peyar, muṟai, tokai
   117. uyartiṇaip peyarē aḵṟiṇaip peyareṉ
   ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa peyarnilaic cuṭṭē.
   itu muṟkūṟiya peyarkaṭkup peyarum muṟaiyun tokaiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: cuṭṭunilaip peyar-poruḷai oruvar karututaṟ kuk kāraṇamāṉa
   nilaimaiyaiyuṭaiya peyarkaḷai, uyartiṇaip peyarē aḵṟiṇaip peyareṉṟu
   āyiraṇṭeṉpa-uyartiṇaip poruḷai oruvaṉ karututaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa peyarum
   aḵṟiṇaip poruḷai oruvaṉ karututaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa peyarum eṉṉum
   avviraṇṭeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar.; e-ṟu
   peyariyaluḷ avaṉ ivaṉ uvaṉ eṉpatu mutalāka uyartiṇaip peyarum atu itu
   utu eṉpatu mutalāka aḵṟiṇaip peyarum āmāṟu avaṟṟiṟku ilakkaṇaṅ
   kūṟukiṉṟār, īṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭṭāḷutal māttiraiyē kūṟiṉāreṉṟu uṇarka. iṉik
   koṟṟaṉ koṟṟi eṉṟāṟ pōlum viravuppeyaruṅ koṟṟaṉ kuṟiyaṉ koṟṟi kuṟiyaḷ
   koṟṟaṉ kuḷampu koṟṟi kuṟitu eṉap piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟāl tiṇai teritaliṉ iru
   tiṇaippeyariṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum. koṟṟaṉcevi koṟṟicevi eṉpaṉavum piṉṉar
   varukiṉṟa viṉaikaḷāl tiṇai viḷaṅki aṭaṅkumāṟu uṇarka.1 iṉi "aḵṟiṇai
   viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē" (eḻu-155) eṉṟāṟ pōlap piṟāṇṭum ōtutal
   paṟṟi nilai yeṉṟataṉāṉ viravuppeyar kōṭalum oṉṟu. (15)
   -------
   1. koṟṟaṉ cevi neṭiyaṉ eṉṟa viṭattu uyartiṇai eṉṟum koṟṟaḷ cevi
       neṭitu eṉṟa viṭattu aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟum tiṇai viḷaṅkiyamai kāṇka.

   -----------

                          4.3. cāriyaippuṇarcci.

   cāriyai varumiṭam
   118. avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ cāriyai varumē
   itu cāriyai varum iṭaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ-ac collappaṭṭa iruvakaip peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉākiya
   iṭattē, cāriyai varum-cāriyaic coṟkaḷ varum; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: āṭūuviṉkai makaṭūuviṉkai palavaṟṟukkōṭu eṉap puṇariyaṉilaiyiṭaip
   poruṇilaikku utavi vantaṉa, cāriyai yeṉṟataṉ poruḷ vēṟāki niṉṟa
   irumoḻiyun tammiṟ cārtaṟ poruṭṭu iyaintu niṉṟatu eṉaṟavāṟu. (14)
   ----------
   cāriyaikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟaiyum tokaiyum
   119. avaitām,
   iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē
   oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē
   aṉṉeṉ kiḷavi uḷappaṭap piṟavum
   aṉṉa eṉpa cāriyai moḻiyē.
   iḵtu accāriyaikaṭkup peyarum muṟaiyun tokaiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: avaitām-muṉṉarc cāriyai yeṉap paṭṭa avaitām; iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē
   oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē aṉṉeṉ kiḷavi uḷappaṭa aṉṉa eṉpa- iṉṉum vaṟṟum attum
   ammum oṉṉum āṉum akkum ikkum aṉṉeṉṉuñ collōṭu kūṭa oṉpatākiya attaṉmai
   yuṭaiyaṉavum, piṟavuñ cāriyai moḻi eṉpa-avai yoḻintaṉavuñ cāriyaic
   collā meṉpar āciriyar; e-ṟu.
   piṟavāvaṉa tam nam um ñāṉṟu keḻu e* ai eṉpaṉavām. ivaṟṟuḷ ñāṉṟu
   oḻintaṉa eṭuttōtuvar āciriyar. "eṭutta naṟaviṉ kulaiyalaṅ kāntaḷ"1
   (kali-40) itu viṉaittokai; cāriyai yaṉṟu. iṉcāriyai vaḻakkup payiṟciyum
   palakāl eṭuttōtappaṭutalum potuvakaiyāṉ ōtiya vaḻit tāṉē cēṟalu2 mākiya
   ciṟappu nōkki muṉ vaittār. vaṟṟum attum iṉpōla mutal tiriyumākalāṉuñ
   ceykai yoppumaiyāṉum ataṉpiṉ vaittār. am īṟutiriyumātaliṉ tiripupaṟṟi
   ataṉpiṉ vaittār. oṉ īṟutiriyumēṉum vaḻakkuppayiṟci yiṉṟi nāṉkā
   murupiṉkaṇ tiritaliṉ ataṉpiṉ vaittār. āṉ poruṭpuṇarccikku urupu
   puṇarccikkum varumeṉṟu ataṉpiṉ vaittār. akku īṟu tiriyumēṉum urupu
   puṇarccikkaṇ vārāmaiyiṉ ataṉpiṉ vaittār. ikku mutal tiriyumēṉuñ
   ciṟupāṉmaipaṟṟi ataṉpiṉ vaittār. aṉ iṉpōlac ciṟattaliṟ piṉ vaittār.
   ----------
   1 kulaiyalaṅ kāntaḷ eṉpatu kulaiyalaṅku kāntaḷ eṉpataṉ tokuttal.
       2 potu vakaiyāṉ ōtiyavaḻi tāṉē cēṟal-ic collukku iṉṉacāriyai eṉa
       vitiyātu potuvākac cāriyaip pēṟu kūṟiya viṭattum tāṉē cāriyaiyāy
       amaital.
       āṉurupiṟkum āṉcāriyaikkum iṉṉurupiṟkum iṉcāriyaikkum vēṟṟumai
       yāteṉiṉ, avai cāriyaiyāṉa iṭattu yātāṉum ōr uru pēṟṟu muṭiyum;
       urupāyiṉa iṭattu vēṟōr urupiṉai ēlā veṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi makattukkai
       eṉpuḻit takaravoṟṟun takaravukāramum varu meṉṟu kōṭumeṉiṉ,
       iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉeṉṟāl attu eṉavē vēṇṭutaliṉ āṇṭum attuniṉṟē
       keṭṭateṉṟu kōṭum. akku ikku eṉpaṉavum pirittukkūṭṭak kiṭakkum.
       tāḻakkō leṉa akkup peṟṟu niṟṟalāṉum āṭikku eṉpuḻik kukaram
       nāṉkaṉuru pākāmainayāṉum ivai cāriyaiyāmāṟu uṇarka. (17)

   ------------
   120. avaṟṟuḷ,
   iṉṉiṉ ikaram āviṉ iṟuti
   muṉaṉark keṭutal urittu mākum.
   itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ iṉcāriyai mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: avaṟṟuḷ-muṟ kūṟiya cāriyaikaḷuḷ, iṉṉiṉ ikaram-iṉ cāriyaiyatu
   ikaram, āviṉ iṟuti muṉṉar- ā eṉṉum ōreḻut toru moḻi muṉṉar, keṭutaral
   urittumākum-keṭṭumuṭiyavum peṟum; e-ṟu.
   urittumāku meṉṟataṉāṟ keṭātu muṭiyavum peṟum eṉṟavāṟu. iḵtu oppak
   kūṟaleṉṉum utti.
   e-ṭu: āṉai āviṉai, āṉoṭu āviṉoṭu, āṟku āviṟku, āṉiṉ
   āviṉiṉ, āṉatu āviṉatu, āṉkaṇ āviṉkaṇ eṉavarum.
   iṉi muṉṉareṉṟataṉāṉē māviṟkum ivvāṟē koḷka. māṉai māviṉai, māṉoṭu
   māviṉōṭu, māṟku māviṟku eṉa oṭṭuka. ākāra īṟeṉṉātu āviṉiṟuti yeṉṟu
   ōtiṉamaiyiṉ mā ilēciṉāṟ koḷḷap paṭṭatu.
   iṉi āṉkōṭu āviṉkōṭu, māṉkōṭu māviṉkōṭu eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai
   poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻiyuṅ koḷka. (18)
   -----------
   iṉ cāriyai īṟu tiriyumiṭam
   121. aḷavākum moḻimutal nilaiiya uyirmicai
   ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉākiya nilaittē.
   iḵtu av iṉcāriyai īṟu tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: aḷavākum moḻi-aḷavup peyarāyp piṉṉiṟkum moḻikku, mutal nilaiiya
   uyirmicai ṉaḵkāṉ-muṉṉar niṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟu niṉṟa
   kuṟṟukarattiṉmēl vanta iṉcāriyaiyatu ṉakaram, ṟaḵkāṉākiya
   nilaittu-ṟakaramāyt tiriyum nilaimaiyai yuṭaittu; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: patiṟṟakal patiṟṟuḻakku. ivaṟṟaip patteṉa niṟutti
   "niṟaiyumaḷavum" (eḻu-436) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl iṉcāriyai koṭuttuk
   "kuṟṟiya lukara meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē" (eḻu-433) eṉṟataṉāṟ kuṟṟukaram
   meyyōṭuṅ keṭuttu vēṇṭuñ ceykai ceytu "muṟṟaviṉ varūum' (eḻu-433)
   eṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟiraṭṭittu muṭikka,.
   nilaiiya eṉṟataṉāṟ piṟavaḻiyum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka.
   patiṟṟeḻuttu, patiṟṟaṭukku oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu, patiṟṟoṉṟu, patiṟṟiraṇṭu,
   patiṟṟoṉpatu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka. ac cūttirattiṟ "kuṟaiyātākum"
   (eḻu-436) eṉṟataṉāṟ poruṭ peyarkkum eṇṇuppeyarkkum iṉkoṭukka. (19)
   -------------
   vaṟṟu mutal keṭutal
   122. vaḵkāṉ meykeṭac cuṭṭumutal aimmuṉ
   aḵkāṉ niṟṟal ākiya paṇpē.
   itu vaṟṟu mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: cuṭṭu mutal aimmuṉ-cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya aikāra īṟṟuc
   coṉmuṉ vaṟṟu1 varuṅkālai, vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa aḵkāṉ niṟṟalākiya paṇpu-av
   vaṟṟuc cāriyai yiṉatu vakaramākiya oṟṟuk keṭa āṇṭu ēṟiya akaram niṟṟal
   ataṟku uḷatākiya kuṇam; e - ṟu.
   ----
   1 vaṟṟuc cāriyaiyai naṉṉūlār aṟṟuc cāriyai eṉpar.

   e - ṭu : avaiyaṟṟai, ivaiyaṟṟai, uvaiyaṟṟai eṉa varum. iṉṉum ivaṟṟai,
   avai, ivai, uvai eṉa niṟuttic 'cuṭṭu mutalākiya aiye ṉiṟuti' (eḻu -
   177) eṉṟataṉāṉ vaṟṟum urupuṅ koṭuttu vēṇṭuñ ceykai ceyka. ivvāṟē ellā
   urupiṟkum oṭṭuka. avaiyaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ
   ceṉṟavaḻiyuṅ
   koḷka.
   ākiya paṇpu eṉṟataṉālē evaṉeṉpatu paṭutta lōcaiyāṟ peyarāyavaḻi(1)
   evaṉ eṉ niṟutti vaṟṟum urupuṅ* koṭuttu vaṟṟumicai yoṟṟeṉṟu ṉakaraṅ
   keṭuttu akaravuyir muṉṉar vaṟṟiṉ vakaraṅ keṭumeṉak keṭuttu evaṟṟai
   evaṟṟoṭu eṉa muṭikka. (20)
   --------
   (1) evaṉ eṉpatu paṭuttalōcaiyāṟ peyarākum eṉavē eṭuttalōcaiyāl
   viṉāviṉaik kuṟippākum eṉpatām.
   ------------
   ṉakara īṟṟuc cāriyai tiriyumiṭam.
   123. ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkāṉ nāṉkaṉuru piṟku.
   iḵtu iṉ oṉ āṉ aṉṉeṉṉum ṉakara īṟu nāṉkun tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : ṉaḵkāṉ nāṉakaṉurupiṟku ṟaḵkāṉ -- ṉakāra īṟṟu nāṉku cāriyaiyiṉ
   ṉakaramum nāṉkā murupiṟku ṟakāramāyt tiriyum; e - ṟu.
   e - ṭu : viḷaviṟku kōoṟku orupāṟku ataṟku eṉa varum. itaṉai 'aḷavāku
   moḻimutal' (eḻu - 121) eṉpataṉpiṉ vaiyātu īṇṭu vaittatu ṉakara
   īṟukaḷellām uṭaṉ tiriyu meṉṟaṟku. āṇṭu vaippiṉ iṉ cāriyaiyē tiriyu
   meṉpatu paṭum. 'oṉṟu mutalākap pattūrnta varūu -- mellā eṇṇum' (eḻu -
   199) eṉapataṉāṉ oru pāṟku eṉpataṉai muṭikka. (21)
   --------------
   poruṭ puṇarcciyil āṉ īṟu tirital.
   124. āṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉō raṟṟē
   nāḷmuṉ varūum vaṉmutaṟ ṟoḻiṟkē.
   iḵtu āṉiṉ īṟu poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ tiriyu meṉkiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : nāḷmuṉ varūum vaṉmutal toḻiṟku - nāṭpeyar muṉṉar varum
   valleḻuttai mutalāka uṭaiya toḻiṟ coṟku iṭaiyē varum, āṉiṉ ṉakaramum
   ataṉōraṟṟu -- āṉcāriyaiyiṉ ṉakaramum nāṉkaṉurupiṉkaṇ varum āṉcāriyai
   pōla ṟakara māyt tiriyum; e - ṟu.
   e - ṭu : paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa rum. 'nāṇmuṟ ṟōṉṟun
   toḻiṉilaik kiḷavikku' (eḻu - 247) eṉṟataṉāṉ āṉcāriyai koṭuttuc ceykai
   ceyka. iṉi ummaiyai iṟantatutaḻīiyatākki nāḷallavaṟṟumuṉ varum
   vaṉmutaṟṟoḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉōṭu okku meṉap poruḷuraittup
   paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ vaḷiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ṟakaramātal koḷka.
   iṉi, ñāpakattāl1 toḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaran tiriyu meṉavē peyarkkaṇ iṉṉiṉ
   ṉakaran tiritalun tiriyāmaiyuṅ koḷka. kuṟumpiṟ koṟṟaṉ, paṟampiṟ pāri
   eṉat tirintu vantaṉa; kurukiṉ kāl, eruttiṉ puṟam eṉat tiriyātu vantaṉa.
   ------
   1. ñāpakam - ñāpakaṅ kūṟal eṉṉum utti. atāvatu, nūṟpāvaic coṟ
       curuṅkavum poruḷ viḷaṅkavum ceyyātu aritum peritumākac ceytu ataṉāl
       vēṟu pala poruḷuṇarttal.

   -----------
   attu mutal keṭutal
   125. attiṉ akaram akaramuṉai yillai.
   iḵtu attu mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : attiṉ akaram - attuccāriyaiyiṉ akaram, akaramuṉai illai - akara
   īṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉar illaiyām; e – ṟu.
   'attavaṇ variṉum varainilai yiṉṟē.' (eḻu - 219) eṉpataṉāṉ makappeyar
   attuppeṟṟu niṉṟatu makattukkai yeṉa akaraṅkeṭṭu niṉṟatu. viḷavattuk
   kaṇṇeṉpuḻik keṭātu niṟṟal 'attē vaṟṟē' (eḻu - 133) eṉpataṉuḷ
   'teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟē' eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟupa. (23)
   -----------
   ikku mutal keṭutal
   126. ikkiṉ ikaram ikaramuṉai yaṟṟē.
   iḵtu ikku mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : ikkiṉ ikaram - ikkuc cāriyaiyiṉatu ikaram, ikaramuṉai aṟṟu -
   ikara īṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉar muṟ kūṟiya attup pōlak keṭum; e – ṟu.
   'tiṅkaṇ muṉvariṉ' (eḻu - 248) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟa ikku āṭikkuk koṇṭāṉ
   ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa ikaraṅ keṭṭu niṉṟatu. iḵtu iṭapporuṭṭu. (24)
   ---------
   127. aiyiṉ muṉṉarum avviyal nilaiyum.
   ituvum atu.
   i - ḷ : aiyiṉ muṉṉarum avviyal nilaiyum – ikkiṉ ikaram ikara īṟṟuc coṉ
   muṉṉaraṉṟi aikāra īṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉarum mēṟ kūṟiya keṭuta liyalpilē
   niṟkum; e – ṟu.
   'tiṅkaḷu nāḷu muntukiḷan taṉṉa' (eḻu - 286) eṉapataṉāṟ cittiraikkuk
   koṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻip peṟṟa ikku aikārattiṉ muṉṉark keṭṭavāṟu kāṇka. (25)
   ------------
   akku akaram niṟka ēṉaiya keṭumāṟu
   128. eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
   akkiṉ iṟutimeym micaiyoṭuṅ keṭumē
   kuṟṟiya lukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu.
   iḵtu akku mutal oḻiya ēṉaiya keṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: eppeyar muṉṉarum-evvakaippaṭṭa peyarccoṉ muṉṉarum , valleḻuttu
   varuvaḻi-valleḻuttu varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, akkiṉ iṟutik
   kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu-iṭai niṉṟa akkuc cāriyaiyiṉ iṟuti niṉṟa
   kuṟṟiyalukaram muṭiyat tōṉṟātu. meymmicai yoṭuṅ keṭum - akkuṟṟukaram
   ēṟiniṉṟa valloṟṟut taṉakkumēl niṉṟa
   valloṟṟōṭuṅ keṭum;e-ṟu
   "oṟṟunilai tiriyā takkoṭu varūum" (eḻu-418) eṉṟataṉāṉ akkuppeṟṟa
   kuṉṟakkūkai maṉṟappeṇṇai eṉpaṉavum, "vēṟṟumai yāyiṉēṉai yiraṇṭum"
   (eḻu-326) eṉpataṉāṉ akkuppeṟṟa īmakkuṭam kammakkuṭam eṉpaṉavum,
   "tamiḻeṉ kiḷaviyum" (eḻu-385) eṉpataṉāṉ akkuppeṟṟa tamiḻakkūttu
   eṉpatuvum akku īṟukeṭṭavāṟu kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam varutaliṉ eppeya reṉṟār.
   muṟṟa veṉpataṉāṉ vaṉkaṇa maṉṟi ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum ivviti koḷka. tamiḻ nūl,
   tamiḻ yāḻ, tamiḻvaraiyar eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē tamakkēṟṟa iyaipu
   valleḻuttuk koṭuttu muṭittukkoḷka. aṉṟik kēṭōtiya kakaravoṟṟu
   niṟkumeṉiṉ, cakaran takaram pakaram vantavaṟṟiṟkuk kakaravoṟṟākāmai
   uṇarka (26)
   ----------
   am īṟu tiriyu miṭam
   129. ammiṉ iṟuti kacatak kālait
   taṉ mey tirintu ṅañana ākum
   iḵtu am īṟu tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ammiṉ iṟuti-ammuc cāriyaiyiṉ iṟutiyākiya makaravoṟṟu,
   kacatakkālai-kacatakkaḷ varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, taṉmey tirintu ṅañana
   ākum-taṉ vaṭivu tirintu ṅañanakkaḷākum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: puḷiyaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl eṉa varum.
   itu "puḷimarak kiḷavikku" (eḻu-244) eṉpataṉāṉ ammuppeṟṟatu.
   kacatakkālait tiriyu meṉavē pakarattiṉkaṇ tiripiṉṟāyiṟṟu. meytirin
   teṉṉātu taṉmey eṉṟataṉāṉ ammiṉ makaramēyaṉṟit tam nam num um eṉṉuñ
   cāriyai makaramun tirital koḷka. ellār taṅkaiyum ellār naṅkaiyum ellīr
   nuṅkaiyum vāṉavari villun tiṅkaḷum eṉa varum. tuṟai kēḻūraṉ
   vaḷaṅkēḻūraṉ eṉak keḻu veṉṉuñ cāriyaiyatu ukarakkēṭum ekara nīṭciyuñ
   ceyyuṇ muṭipeṉṟa koḷka. (27)
   ---------
   am īṟu keṭumiṭam
   130. meṉmaiyum iṭaimaiyum varūum kālai
   iṉmai vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
   iḵtu ammīṟu iyalpukaṇattu muṉṉark keṭu meṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: meṉmaiyum iṭaimaiyum varūuṅkālai-meṉ kaṇamam iṭaikkaṇamum
   varumoḻiyāy varuṅ kālattu, iṉmai vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar-ammuc cāriyai
   iṟuti makara miṉṟi muṭitalai vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: puḷiyañeri nuṉi muri yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa varum. uraiyiṟkōṭa leṉpataṉāṟ
   puḷiyavilai yeṉa uyirvaruvaḻi īṟu keṭutalum puḷiyilaiyeṉa ammu
   muḻuvatuṅ keṭutaluṅ koḷka. puḷiyavilai yeṉṟatu "oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya
   vaḻakku"1 (eḻu-132) aṉṟu. meṉkaṇamum iṭaikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamun tammu
   ḷokkumēṉum ammu muḻuvatuṅ keṭṭu varutaliṉ uyirai eṭuttōtārāyiṉār.
   puḷiṅkāy eṉpatu maru muṭipu. (28)
   -------
   1 oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku-cāriyai poruntutaṟ kēṟpa naṭanta vaḻakku.

   -----------
   iṉ cāriyai muḻutum keṭumiṭam
   131. iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumai yurupiṟ
   kiṉṉeṉ cāriyai iṉmai vēṇṭum.
   iḵtu iṉcāriyai aintāmurupiṉkaṇ muḻuvatuṅ keṭu meṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumai yurupiṟku-iṉṉeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa vēṟṟumai
   yurupiṟku, iṉṉeṉ cāriyai iṉmai vēṇṭum-iṉṉeṉṉuñ cāriyai tāṉ iṉṟi
   muṭitalai virumpum āciriyaṉ; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: viḷaviṉ, palāviṉ, kaṭuviṉ, taḻuviṉ, cēviṉ, vauviṉ eṉa varum.
   ivaṟṟiṟku vīḻpaḻa meṉavum nīṅkiṉā ṉeṉavuṅ koṭuttu muṭivuṇarka.
   ūriṉīṅkiṉāṉ eṉa ēṉaiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. iṉi "avaṟṟuḷ iṉṉiṉikaram"
   (eḻu-120) eṉṟataṉpiṉ itaṉai vaiyāta muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ iṉcāriyai
   keṭātu vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇuñ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum niṟṟal koḷka. pāmpiṉiṟ kaṭitu tēḷ,
   "kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpalavutavi", "akaṭucērpu porunti yaḷaviṉil tiriyāta"
   (malaipaṭu-33) eṉavarum. iṉi iṉmaiyum vēṇṭumeṉṉum ummai tokku
   niṉṟatākki ataṉāṉ ivai kōṭalum oṉṟu. (29)
   ---------
   puṇarmoḻikaḷiṟ cāriyai varutal
   132..peyarun toḻilum pirintoruṅ kicaippa
   vēṟṟumai urupu nilaipeṟu vaḻiyun
   tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik kaṇṇum
   oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇic
   coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ vaḻivantu viḷaṅkā
   tiṭainiṉ ṟiyaluñ cāriyai iyaṟkai
   uṭaimaiyum iṉmaiyum oṭuvayiṉ okkum.
   itu muṟkūṟiya cāriyaikaḷellām puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷē varumeṉpatūum ammoḻi tām
   ivai yeṉpatūum avai vārāta moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉpatūuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: peyarun toḻilum-peyarccollun toḻiṟ collum, pirintu icaippa oruṅku
   icaippa-peyarun toḻilumāyp pirinticaippap peyarum peyarumāyk
   kūṭiyicaippa, vēṟṟumai yurupu nilaipeṟu vaḻiyum-vēṟṟumai ceyyum
   urupukaḷ tokātu nilaipeṟṟa iṭattum, tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik
   kaṇṇum-avvēṟṟumai yurupukaḷ tōṟṟutal vēṇṭātu tokka iṭattum1 oṭṭutaṟku
   oḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi-tām poruntutaṟ kēṟpa naṭanta vaḻakkōṭē
   porunti, coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ-cāriyai peṟum puṇar moḻikaḷaip pirittuk
   kāṇumiṭattu, cāriyai iyaṟkai vaḻi vantu viḷaṅkātu iṭainiṉṟu
   iyalum-accāriyaiyiṉatu taṉmai accoṟkaḷiṉ piṉṉē vantu viḷaṅkātu naṭuvē
   niṉṟu naṭakkum, uṭaimaiyum iṉmaiyum oṭuvayiṉ okkum - accāriyai
   uṇṭātalum illaiyātalum oṭuvurupiṉiṭattu ottuvarum; e-ṟu
   oṭuviṟ kokkum eṉavē ēṉaiya ovvāvāyiṉa.
   ----
   1. innūṟpāviṟku, peyarum peyarum peyarum viṉaiyum vēṟṟumai viriyil iru
       collāyp pirinticaikkum eṉṟum, vēṟṟumait tokaiyil oru colpōla
       oruṅkicaikkum eṉṟum, poruḷ koḷvā** naṉṉūlāruḷḷiṭṭa āciriyar cilar.

   e-ṭu: viḷaviṉaikkuṟaittāṉ, kūḻiṟkuk kuṟṟēval ceyyum-ivai pirinticaittu
   urupu nilaipeṟṟaṉa. "aṉṉeṉ cāriyai" (eḻu-194) eṉpataṉaik
   "kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟuti" (eḻu-115) eṉpataṉaic cēra vaittataṉāl iṉcāriyai
   varutal koḷka. ivviraṇṭurupuñ cāriyai niṟpap perumpāṉmaiyun tokā veṉṟu
   uṇarka. viḷaviṉaik kuṟaittavaṉ. kaṭicūttirattiṟkup poṉ-ivai oruṅkicaipa
   urupu nilaipeṟṟaṉa., vāṉattiṉ vaḻukki, vāṉattu vaḻukki eṉac cāriyai
   peṟṟup pirinticaittu aintāmurupu nilaipeṟṟum nilaipeṟātum vantatu.
   vāṉattiṉ vaḻukkal, vāṉattu vaḻukkal-ivai "melleḻut tuṟaḻum"(eḻu-312)
   eṉṉuñ cūttirattu "vaḻakkattāṉa" eṉpataṉāṉ attuk koṭuttu makaraṅkeṭukka
   oruṅkicaittaṉa. viḷaviṉatu kōṭu, viḷiṉ kōṭu eṉa oruṅkicaittuc cāriyai
   peṟṟavaḻi āṟaṉurupu tokātun tokkum niṉṟatu. itaṟkup pirinticaitta
   liṉṟu, marattukkaṭ kaṭṭiṉāṉ, marattuk kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉappirinticaitta
   vaḻiyum, marattukkaṭ kuraṅku, marattuk kuraṅku eṉa oruṅkicaittavaḻiyuñ
   cāriyai niṉṟavaḻi ēḻaṉurupu tokātun tokkum niṉṟatu. "kiḷaippeya rellām"
   (eḻu-307) eṉṟataṉuṭ "kau" eṉṟataṉāṉ ṇakāram ṭakāra māyiṟṟu. "nilāveṉ
   kiḷavi yattoṭu civaṇum" (eḻu-228) eṉa vititta attu nilākkatir
   nilāmuṟṟam eṉṟavaḻip peṟātāyiṟṟu. aḵtu "oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku"
   aṇmaiyiṉ. nilāttuk koṇṭāṉ, nilāttuk koṇṭavaṉ eṉpaṉa uruputokkuḻi iru
   vaḻiyum peṟṟaṉa. ellār tammaiyum eṉac cāriyai īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum varutaliṉ
   iṭai niṟṟal perumpāṉmai yeṉṟaṟku iyalu meṉṟār,. pūviṉoṭu virinta
   kūntal, pūvoṭu virinta kūntal eṉa uṭaimaiyum iṉmaiyam oṭuvayiṉ ottatu.
   iṉi iyaṟkai yeṉṟataṉāṉ oṭu urupiṉkaṭ peṟṟum peṟāmaiyum varutalaṉṟip
   peṟṟē varutaluṅ koḷka.palavaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum. (30)
   ----------
   attu vaṟṟu varumiṭattu nikaḻum vikāram
   133. attē vaṟṟē āyiru moḻimēl
   oṟṟumey keṭutal teṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē
   avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē.
   iḵtu attu vaṟṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku nilaimoḻiyatu oṟṟuk kēṭum varumoḻi
   vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ oṟṟuppēṟumākiya ceykai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: attē vaṟṟē āyiru moḻimēloṟṟu-attum vaṟṟumākiya avviraṇṭu
   cāriyaimēl niṉṟa oṟṟu, meykeṭutal teṟṟaṉṟaṟṟu-taṉ vaṭivu keṭutal
   teḷiyappaṭṭatu, avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē-avviru cāriyai
   muṉṉum varum valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: kalattukkuṟai, avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa varum. "attiṭai varū uṅ kalameṉ
   ṉaḷavē" (eḻu-168) "cuṭṭumutal vakaram aiyu meyyum" (eḻu-183)
   eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ attum vaṟṟum peṟṟuvarum makara vakara īṟukaṭku
   īṟṟuvalleḻuttuviti iṉmaiyiṉ avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumeṉṟu
   cāriyai valleḻuttu vitittār. valleḻuttu iṉṟit tirintu muṭivaṉa
   ṇakāramum, ṉakāramum, lakāramum, ḷakāramumām. makara īṟṟiṟku attum
   vakara īṟṟiṟku vaṟṟum varumeṉpatu accūttiraṅkaḷāṟ peṟṟām. vaṟṟē yattē
   yeṉṉāta muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṟ puḷḷiyiṟṟiṉ muṉṉar attiṉmicai yoṟṟuk
   keṭātu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.
   e-ṭu: viṇṇattun toṭkum veyilattuc ceṉṟāṉ, iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
   meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ attiṉ akaram akara muṉṉarēyaṉṟip piṟa uyirmuṉṉaruṅ keṭum
   orōviṭat teṉṟukoḷka. aṇṇāttēri, tiṭṭāttukkuḷam eṉa ākārattiṉ muṉṉarum
   varum attiṉ akaraṅ keṭṭatu. ivaṟṟai akara īṟākkiyam muṭippa. iṉit
   teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟē eṉṟataṉāṉ attiṉ akaran teṟṟeṉak keṭātu niṟkum iṭamuṅ koḷka.
   atavattukkaṇ, viḷavattukkaṇ eṉa varum (31)
   -------------

                          4. 5. eḻuttuc cāriyai

   eḻuttuc cāriyaikaḷiṉ peyar
   134. kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
   nērat tōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyai.
   itu moḻiccāriyaiyai viṭṭu eḻuttukkaṭku varuñ cāriyaikaḷatu peyarum
   muṟaiyun tokaiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi-kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu porunti,
   eḻuttiṉcāriyai nērat tōṉṟum - eḻuttiṉkaṇ varuñ cāriyaiyātaṟku ellā
   āciriyarāṉum uṭampaṭat tōṉṟum; e-ṟu
   kāramuṅ karamum eṭuttuc colliyavaḻi iṉiticaittalāṉuñ vaḻakkup payiṟci
   yuṭaimaiyāṉum, vaṭaveḻuttiṟkum uriyavātalāṉuñ cērak kūṟiṉār. kāṉ
   attaṉmai yiṉmaiyiṉāṟ piṉ vaittār. nērat tōṉṟu meṉavē nērattōṉṟātaṉavum
   uḷavāyiṉa. avai āṉam ēṉam ōṉam eṉka. ivai citainta vaḻakkēṉuṅ
   kaṭiyalākāvāyiṉa. (32)
   ---------
   neṭṭeḻuttuc cāriyai.
   135. avaṟṟuḷ,
   karamuṅ kāṉum neṭṭeḻut tilavē.
   iḵtu avaṟṟuṭ cila cāriyai cila veḻuttōṭu vārā veṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
   i-ḷ: avaṟṟuḷ-muṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ, karamuṅ kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttila-karamuṅ kāṉum
   neṭṭeḻuttiṟku varutaliṉṟu; e-ṟu
   eṉavē, neṭṭeḻuttiṟkuk kāram varumāyiṟṟu. ākāram īkāram eṉa oṭṭuka.
   aikāram aukāra meṉac cūttiraṅkaḷuḷ varumāṟu kāṇka.
   ----------
   kuṟṟeḻuttuc cāriyai
   136. varaṉmuṟai mūṉṟuṅ kuṟṟeḻut tuṭaiya.
   iḵtu aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; eṉṉai? neṭṭeḻuttiṟkuc cila cāriyai
   vilakkiṉāṟpōlak kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkum vilakkaṟpāṭu uṇṭō veṉa aiyam nikaḻtaliṉ.
   i-ḷ: varaṉmuṟai mūṉṟum-varalāṟṟu muṟaimaiyaiyuṭaiya mūṉṟu cāriyaiyum,
   kuṟṟeḻuttuṭaiya -kuṟṟeḻuttup peṟṟu varutalaiyuṭaiya; e-ṟu
   akāram akaram maḵkāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. "vakāra micaiyum"."akara ikaram",
   "vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa" eṉavum piṟavuñ cūttiraṅkaḷuṭ kāṇka.
   iḵkāṉ oḵkāṉ eṉpaṉa peruvaḻak kaṉṟu. varaṉmuṟai yeṉṟataṉāṉ aḵkāṉ eṉpuḻi
   āytam peṟutal koḷka. itu "kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarāytappuḷḷi" (eḻu-38)
   eṉpataṉāṟ peṟātāyiṟṟu, moḻiyāy nillāmaiyiṉ. (34)
   ----------
   ai, auviṉ cāriyai
   137. aikāra aukāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum
   iḵtu "avaṟṟuṭ karamuṅ kāṉum" eṉpataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: aikāra aukāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum-neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷuḷ aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ
   muṉ vilakkappaṭṭa kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum; e-ṟu
   aikāṉ aukāṉ eṉa varum. ummai iṟantatu taḻīiyaṟṟu, kārattaik
   karututaliṉ. (35)
   -----------

                4.6. uyireḻuttiṉ puṇarcci yiyalpukaḷ

   138. puḷḷi īṟṟumuṉ uyirtaṉit tiyalātu
   meyyoṭuñ civaṇum avviyal keṭuttē.
   itu puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta kālattup puṇarum muṟaimai
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittu iyalātu - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc coṉmuṉṉar vanta
   uyirmutaṉmoḻiyiṉ uyir taṉittu naṭavātu, meyyoṭuñ civaṇum-appuḷḷiyōṭum
   kūṭum, avviyal keṭuttu-tāṉ taṉittu niṉṟa avviyal piṉaik keṭuttu;1 e-ṟu
   eṉavē, nīrōṭu kūṭiya pālpōla niṉṟateṉṟu oṟṟumai kūṟiṉār.
   īṇṭu itaṉāṉē uyirmeyyeṉap peyarpeṟṟatu.
   e-ṭu: pālaritu pālāḻi, ālilai, poruḷīṭṭam, vāṉulaku, vāṉū,ṭu
   vēleṟintāṉ, vēlēṟṟāṉ, poruḷaiyam, poruḷoṉṟa, nāṇōṭiṟṟu,
   collauviyam eṉa varum.,
   oṉṟeṉa muṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ iyalpallāta puḷḷi muṉṉar uyir vantālum ivviti
   koḷka. ataṉai ataṉoṭu nāṭuri eṉa varum. ivaṟṟaik "cuṭṭumuta lukara
   maṉṉoṭu" (eḻu-176) "urivaru kālai nāḻik kiḷavi" (eḻu-240) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ
   muṭikka. puṭṭiyīṟṟu muṉṉumeṉa ummaiyai māṟi eccavummaiyākkik
   kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉṉarum eṉa avviti koḷka. eṉavē, "kuṟṟiya lukaramu
   maṟṟu" (eḻu-105) eṉṟataṉōṭum poruntiṟṟām. nākaritu, varakaritu eṉa
   varum. (36)
   -----------
   1 "avviyal keṭuttu" eṉpataṟku, puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkum ammeyyiṉ iyalpaik
       keṭuttu eṉṟē poruḷ koḷvatu takkatu. "meyyuyir nīṅkiṉ taṉṉuruvākum"
       eṉṟu aṭutta nūṟpāvuṅ kūṟutal kāṇka.

   -----------
   uyir pirinta meyyiṉ iyalpu
   139. meyuyir nīṅkiṉ taṉṉuru vākum.1
   iḵtu uyirmey puṇarccikkaṇ uyirnīṅkiyavaḻip paṭuvatōr viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: mey uyir nīṅkiṉ-mey taṉṉōṭu kūṭiniṉṟa uyir puṇarcciyiṭattup
   pirintu vēṟu niṉṟatāyiṉ, taṉ uruvākum-tāṉ muṉṉarp peṟṟu niṉṟa
   puḷḷivaṭivu peṟum; e-ṟu
   āl ilai ataṉ ai eṉa varum
   uyir iṉṉa vaṭiviṟṟeṉṟu āciriyar kūṟāmaiyiṉ uyirkkaṇ ārāycci yiṉṟu.
   iṉi, ekara okaraṅkaḷaip puḷḷiyāṉ vēṟṟumai ceytaliṉ, toṉṟu toṭṭu
   vaḻaṅkiṉa vaṭivuṭaiya eṉṟu kōṭalumām. puṇarcciyuḷ uyir meyyiṉaip
   pirippārātaliṉ, itu kūṟākkāṟ kuṉṟak kūṟalām eṉṟu uṇarka. (37)
   -------
   1 ivviyalpu oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉṉum uttiyāl kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum ēṟkum.

   --------
   uṭampaṭu mey tōṉṟum iṭam
   140. ellā moḻikkum uyirvaru vaḻiyē
   uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvukoḷal varaiyār.
   iḵtu uyirīṟum uyirmutaṉmoḻiyum puṇarumvaḻi nikaḻvatōr karuvi
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ellā moḻikkum - nilaimoḻiyum varumoḻiyumāyp puṇarum evvakai
   moḻikkum, uyir varuvaḻi-uyir mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, uṭampaṭumeyyiṉ
   uruvu koḷal varaiyār-uṭampaṭumeyyiṉatu vaṭivai uyirīṟu kōṭalai nīkkār,
   koḷvār āciriyar; e-ṟu
   avai yakaramum vakaramumeṉpatu mutaṉūlpaṟṟik kōṭum;
   "uṭampaṭu meyyē yakāra vakāram
   uyirmutaṉ moḻivarūuṅkālai yāṉa*"
   eṉavum,
   "iṟutiyummutalum uyirni*lai vatiṉē
   uṟumeṉa moḻipa uṭampaṭu meyyē"
   eṉavuṅ kūṟiṉārākaliṉ, uyirkaḷuḷ ikara īkāra aikāra īṟu yakara uṭampaṭu
   mey koḷḷum. *ekāram yakāramum vakāramuṅ koḷḷum. allaṉa vellām vakara
   uṭampaṭumeyyē koḷḷumeṉṟu uṇarka.
   e-ṭu: kiḷiyaḻakitu, kurīiyōppuvāḷ, varaiyaramakaḷir eṉavum,
   viḷavaḻakitu, palāvaḻakitu, kaṭuvaḻakitu, pūvaḻakiru, kōvaḻaki,tu
   kauvaṭaintatu eṉavum oṭṭuka. 'ēe yivaḷorutti pēṭiyōveṉṟār' (cīvaka –
   652) ēvāṭal kāṇka eṉa ēkārattiṟku iraṇṭum vantaṉa.
   oṉṟeṉa muṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ vikārappaṭṭa moḻikkaṇṇum uṭampaṭumey koḷka.
   maravaṭi āyirutiṇai eṉa varum. varaiyā reṉṟataṉāṉ uṭampaṭumey kōṭal
   orutalai yaṉṟu. kiḷi aritu. mūṅkā illai1 eṉavum varum. oṉṟeṉa muṭitta
   leṉpataṉāl 'viṇvattuk koṭkum' eṉac ciṟupāṉmai puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉum varum.
   celvuḻi uṇpuḻi eṉpaṉa viṉait tokai yeṉa maṟukka.2 (38)
   ---------
   1 ittoṭarkaḷāl, tamiḻum oru kālattil āṅkilattaip pōla puṇarcciyiṉṟi
   eḻutappaṭṭamai ūkikkappaṭum.
   2 immaṟuppu cariyāṉatāyt tōṉṟavillai. celavuḻi uṇa*puḻi eṉpaṉa, viṉait
   tokai yalla vātaliṉ toḻiṟ peyarum uyirīṟum puṇarnta viṉaiyeccaṅkaḷātal
   vēṇṭum. viṉaittokaiyāyiṉ celluḻi uṇṇuḻi eṉṟa vaṭivukaḷē koḷḷal vēṇṭum ;
   aṅṅaṉa miṉṟic celavuḻi uṇapuḻi-yeṉṟiruttaliṉ ivai viṉaittokaiyākā veṉṟē
   koḷḷutal takum.
   ------------

             4. 7. puṇarcciyiṟ poruḷ vēṟupaṭumiṭam

   oli vēṟṟumaiyāṟ poruḷ vēṟupaṭal
   141. eḻuttō raṉṉa poruḷteri puṇarcci
   icaiyil tirital nilaiiya paṇpē.
   iḵtu eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu palavā meṉa eytāta teytuvikkiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : eḻuttō raṉṉa poruḷ teri puṇarcci - eḻuttu orutaṉmaittāṉa poruḷ
   viḷaṅka niṟkum puṇar moḻikaḷ, icaiyil tirital nilaiiya paṇpu - eṭuttal
   paṭuttal nalita leṉkiṉṟa ōcai vēṟṟumaiyāṟ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal
   nilaipeṟṟa kuṇam ; e – ṟu.
   e - ṭu : cempoṉ patiṉṟoṭi, cemparutti, kuṟumparampu, nākaṉ ṟēvaṉpōttu,
   tāmaraikkaṇiyār, kuṉṟēṟāmā3 eṉa ivai icaiyil tirintaṉa. (39)
   ------
   3 cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi = cempoṉ + patiṉṟoṭi, cempu + oṉpatiṉṟoṭi; cemparutti
       = cem + parutti, cempu + arutti; kuṟumparampu = kuṟum + parampu.
       kuṟumpara + ampu; nākaṉṟēvaṉpōttu = nākaṉṟēvaṉ + pōttu, nākaṉṟē +
       vaṉapōttu; tāmaraikkaṇiyār = tāmarai + kaṇiyār, tām + araikku +
       aṇiyār; kuṉṟēṟāmē = kuṉṟēṟā + mā, kuṉṟēṟu + āmā.

   -----------
   142. avaitām,
   muṉṉap poruḷa puṇarcci vāyiṉ
   iṉṉa eṉṉum eḻuttukkaṭaṉ ilavē.
   itu mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : avai tām - pala poruṭkup potu veṉṟa puṇarmoḻikaḷ tām, muṉṉap
   poruḷ - kuṟippāṉ uṇarum poruṇmaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya, puṇarccivāyiṉ iṉṉa veṉṉum
   eḻuttuk kaṭaṉ ila-puṇarcciyiṭattu ittaṉmaiya veṉṉum eḻuttu muṟaimaiyai
   uṭaiyavalla; e-ṟu
   ceṉpoṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉṟuḻip poṉṉārāycci yaḷavaḻip poṉṉeṉavuñ cempārāycci
   yuḷavaḻic cempeṉavuṅ kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭṭatu. icaiyil tirita leṉṟatu
   oliyeḻuttiṟ keṉavum eḻuttukkaṭaṉila veṉṟatu varivaṭiviṟ keṉavuṅ koḷka.
   (40)
   puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu
   --------------

                               5. tokaimarapu
(eḻuttup puṇarcciyuḷ, tokuttuk kūṟutaṟkuriya puṇarcci yilakkaṇaṅkaḷai
                               uṇarttuvatu)
            5. 1. uyirīṟu meyyīṟukaḷiṉ potup puṇarcci

   melliṉan tōṉṟu miṭam
   143. kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēṟ ṟōṉṟum
   melleḻut tiyaṟkai colliya muṟaiyāṉ
   ṅañanama veṉṉum oṟṟā kummē
   aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
   eṉpatu cūttiram.
   uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum mēlai akattōttiṉuḷ muṭikkumvaḻi īṟukaṭōṟum virittu
   muṭippaṉavaṟṟai īṇṭu orōvōr cūttiraṅkaḷāl tokuttu muṭipu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ,
   ivvōttut tokaimara peṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēl mūvakai moḻiyum
   nālvakaiyāṟ puṇarvuḻi, mūṉṟu tiripum ōriyalpum eyti vēṟṟumai alvaḻi
   yeṉa irupakutiyavāki eḻuttuñ cāriyaiyum mikkup puṇarumāṟu itu veṉṟu
   uṇartti avaitām virinta cūttirap poruḷavaṉṟiyun tokkup puṇarumāṟu
   kūṟutaliṉ, ivvōttup puṇariyalōṭu iyaipuṭaittāyiṟṟu. it talaic cūttiram
   uyir mayaṅkiyalaiyum puḷḷi mayaṅkilaiyum nōkkiyatōr varumoḻik karuvi
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēṟ ṟōṉṟum iyaṟkai melleḻuttu-uyirīṟum
   puḷḷiyīṟum muṉṉar niṟpak kacata pakkaḷai mutalākavuṭaiya moḻikaḷ vantāl
   avaṟṟiṟku mēlē tōṉṟi niṟkum iyalpākiya melleḻuttukkaḷ, colliya muṟaiyāṉ
   ṅañanama eṉṉum oṟṟākum-neṭuṅkaṇakkiṟ poruntak kūṟiya muṟaiyāṉē
   kacatapakkaḷukku ṅañanama veṉṉum oṟṟukkaḷ niraṉiṟaivakaiyāṉām, aṉṉa
   marapiṉ moḻivayiṉāṉa-attaṉmaittākiya muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
   moḻikaḷiṭattu; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: viḷaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.itu "marappeyarkkiḷavi"
   (eḻu-217) eṉpataṉāṉ melleḻuttup peṟṟatu. maraṅ kuṟitu ciṟitu tītu
   peritu eṉa alvaḻikkaṭ ṭiriyumāṟu "alvaḻi yellām" (eḻu-314) eṉpataṉāṟ
   peṟutumēṉum īṇṭut tōṉṟu meṉṟataṉāl nilaimoḻikkaṭ ṭōṉṟi niṉṟa oṟṟut
   tirital koḷka. aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyaṉmaiyiṉ viḷakkuṟumai1 viḷakkuṟaittāṉ
   eṉpuḻi melleḻuttup peṟāvāyiṉa, ivai ēḻāvatum iraṇṭāvatun tiritaliṉ.
   iṅṅaṉam eḻuttup peṟuvaṉavun tirivaṉavu mellām varumoḻiyēpaṟṟi varumeṉṟu
   uaiṇarka. (1)
   -----
   1 viḷakkuṟumai eṉpatu āṟām vēṟṟimaiyumām.

   -------------
   varumoḻi yiyalpātal
   144. ñanama yavaveṉum mutalāku moḻiyum
   uyirmuta lākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa
   aṉṟi yaṉaittum ellā vaḻiyam
   niṉṟa colmuṉ iyalpā kummē.
   itu muṟkūṟiya nālvakaip puṇarcciyuḷ iyalpu puṇaruṅkāl ikkūṟiya patiṉē
   ḻeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy vanta iṭattu irupattunāṉ kīṟṟiṉ muṉṉarum
   vēṟṟumaiyilum alvaḻiyilum varumoḻi iyalpāy muṭikaveṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ñanamayava eṉum mutalākumoḻiyum –ñanamayava eṉṟu collappaṭum
   eḻuttukkaḷ mutalāy niṟkuñ coṟkaḷum, uyirmutalākiya moḻiyum
   uḷappaṭa-uyireḻuttu mutalāy niṉṟa coṟkaḷun tammiṟkūṭa, aṉṟi
   yaṉaittum-appatiṉēḻākiya varumoḻikaḷum, ellāvaḻiyum-vēṟṟumaiyum
   alvaḻiyumākiya ellā iṭattum, niṉṟa coṉmuṉ-irupattunāṉku īṟṟavāy niṉṟa
   peyarccoṉmuṉṉar, iyalpākum -tiripiṉṟi iyalpu puṇarcciyāy niṟkum; e-ṟu
   uyirīṟṟiṉkaṇ ekara okaram oḻintaṉa koḷka.
   e-ṭu: viḷa palā kiḷi kurī kaṭu pū cē kai cō kau eṉa niṟutti, ñāṉṟatu
   nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntaiyatu eṉa meymmutaṉ moḻi varuvittu,
   poruḷ tarutaṟku ēṟpaṉa aṟintu kūṭṭuka. cō eṉpatu araṇ. ataṟkuc cō
   ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉak koḷka. kauveṉpataṟkuk kauñekiḻntatu nīṭiṟṟu eṉka. iṉi
   ivaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar uyir mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl aḻakitu āyiṟṟu illai īṇṭiṟṟu
   uṇṭu ūṟiṟṟu eḻuntatu ēyntatu aitu oṉṟu ōṅkiṟṟu auviyattatu eṉa varum.
   ivaṟṟuṭ cōvukku iṭintatu īṇṭaiyatu uḷḷatu ūṟiṟṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu muṟ
   kūṟiyavaṟṟaiyum oṭṭuka. iṉi vēṟṟumaikkaṇ viḷa mutaliyavaṟṟai niṟutti
   ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmai aḻaku ākkam iḷamai īṭṭam uyarvu ūṟṟam
   eḻucci ēṟṟam aiyam oḻivu ōkkam auviyam eṉa oṭṭuka. ēlātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṟ
   kūṟiyavāṟupōla ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu oṭuka.
   iṉip puḷḷiyīṟṟu ṇakāramum ṉakāramum mēṟkūṟupa. ēṉaiya īṇṭuk kūṟutum.
   e-ṭu: uriñ verin eṉa niṟutti, ñekiḻntatu nīṭiṟṟu aḻakitu āyiṟṟu eṉavum,
   ñekiḻcci nīṭṭippu aṭaivu ākkam eṉavum varuvittu, ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
   maram vēy vēr yāḻ eṉa niṟutti, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu
   nuntaiyatu aḻakitu āyiṟṟu eṉavum,. ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmai
   aṭaivu ākkam eṉavum varuvittu, ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ makara īṟu
   vēṟṟumaikkaṭ keṭutal "tuvara" (eḻu-310) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. alvaḻikkaṭ
   keṭutal "alvaḻi yellām" (eḻu-314) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. nilaimoḻit tiripu
   īṇṭuk koḷḷāmai uṇarka. yakara īṟu yakarattiṉ muṉṉar iraṇṭiṭattuṅ
   keṭutal īṇṭu ellā meṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. vēl tev kōḷ eṉa niṟutti ēṟpaṉa
   koṇarntu iruvaḻiyum oṭṭuka. ṇakāra lakāra ḷakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar
   nakaram varumoḻiyāka vantuḻi annakaran tiritaliṉ attirinta utāraṇaṅkaḷ
   īṇṭuk koḷḷaṟka. ivaṟṟuḷ tirintu varuvaṉavuḷa; avai eṭuttōttāṉum1
   ilēcāṉum2 ēṉai yōttukkaḷuḷ muṭikkiṉṟavāṟṟāṉ uṇarka. iṉi, ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ
   uyirkkaṇamāyiṉ oṟṟiraṭṭiyum uṭampaṭu mey peṟṟum uyirēṟiyum muṭiyuṅ
   karuvittiri pukaḷ tiripeṉappaṭā3. ivviyalpiṉkaṇ ṇeṉṟu uṇarka. varaku
   ñāṉṟatu varaku ñāṟci eṉak kuṟṟukarattiṉkaṇṇum ivvāṟē koḷka.
   irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumaikkum alvaḻikkum akat
   tōttiṉuḷ nāṟpatteṭṭuc cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivaṉavaṟṟai oru cūttirattāṟ
   ṟokuttu muṭittār. mēlum ivvāṟē kūṟupa. ivviyalpu varumoḻi nōkkik kūṟiya
   teṉṟu uṇarka. ivviyalpu puṇarcci meyk kaṇ nikaḻumāṟu uyirkkaṇ
   nikaḻāmaiyiṉ mey muṟkūṟiṉār. (2)
   --------
   1 eṭuttōttu-veḷippaṭaiyāy eṭuttu ōtum viti.
       2 ilēcu-nūṟpāvil mikaiyāyirukkum eḻuttum collum
       3 uyir mutaṟcol varumoḻiyāyiṉ nilaimoḻiyīṟṟu oṟṟiraṭṭutalum iru
       moḻikkum iṭaiyil uṭampaṭumey tōṉṟutalum avviruvakai meyyiṉmēlum
       uyirēṟi muṭitalum karuvittiripukaḷ; nilaimoḻiyīṟum varumoḻiyīṟum
       tiritalum, varumoḻi mutaṉmey taṉṉoṟṟum iṉavoṟṟum mikappeṟutalum
       ceykait tiripukaḷ eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu,.

   ------------
   145. avaṟṟuḷ
   melleḻut tiyaṟkai uṟaḻiṉum varaiyār
   colliya toṭarmoḻi iṟuti yāṉa.
   itu muṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku ammuṭipu vilakkip piṟitu viti
   eytuvittatu.
   i-ḷ: avaṟṟuḷ- muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu kaṇakkiṉuḷ melleḻuttiyaṟkai uṟaḻiṉum
   varaiyār-melleḻuttu iyalpiyal pātalēyaṉṟi uṟaḻntu muṭiyiṉum nīkkār,
   colliya toṭarmoḻi iṟutiyāṉa -collappaṭṭa toṭar moḻiyīṟṟukakaṇ; e-ṟu
   ummai etirmaṟai, eṉavē uṟaḻāmai valiyuṭaittāyiṟṟu. katirñeri katirññeri
   nuṉi muri eṉavum, itaḻñeri itaḻññeri nuṉimuri eṉavum varum. varumoḻi
   muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl ōreḻut torumoḻi īreḻut torumoḻikaḷuḷḷuñ cila
   uṟaḻccipaṟṟu muṭital koḷka. pūñeri pūññeri nuṉi muri, kāyñeri kāyññeri
   nuṉi muri eṉavarum. colliyaveṉṟataṉāṉ ōreḻut torumoḻikaḷuṭ cila mikku
   muṭital koḷka. kaiññerittār nīṭṭiṉār maṟittār eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē
   īreḻaṟut torumoḻikkaṇ meyññāṉam nūl maṟantār1 eṉavarum. ivaṟṟai nalintu
   kūṟap piṟattaliṉ iyalpeṉpārum uḷar. pūñāṟṟiṉār eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa mikātaṉa.
   (3)
   ---------
   1 mey nūl eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷai nacciṉārkkiṉiyar iṅku īreḻuttoru moḻiyākak
   koṇṭatu, avā "ōreḻut torumoḻi"eṉṉum nūṟpāvuraiyil coṟkaḷai oṟṟum
   kuṟṟukaramum nīkki yeṇṇiya muṟaiyoṭu muraṇum.
   -----------
   ṇa ṉa muṉ yāvum ñāvum
   146. ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum
   viṉaiyō raṉaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
   itu yakara ñakara mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu nikaḻvatōr taṉmai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   ituvum puṇariyaloḻipāyk karuvippāṟpaṭum.
   i-ḷ: ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum-ṇakāra ṉakāra meṉṟu kūṟappaṭum
   puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta yāvum ñāvum mutalākiya viṉaiccoṟkaḷ,
   viṉaiyōraṉaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar-oru viṉai vanta taṉmaiyai okku meṉṟu
   colluvar pulavar; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: "maṇyātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē", "maṇñātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu
   tōṉṟumē" eṉavum, "poṉayātta tārppuravi parikkumē" , "poṉñātta
   tārppuravi parikkumē: eṉavum varum
   viṉaikkaṇ ṇeṉavē maṇyāmai maṇñāmai eṉap peyarkkaṇ vārāvāyiṉa. ñā
   muṟkūṟātu yā muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ ñācceṉṟavaḻi yāc cellātu yāc ceṉṟavaḻi
   ñāc cellu meṉṟu koḷka. maṇñāṉṟatu eṉṟavaḻi maṇyāṉṟatu eṉṟu vārāmai
   uṇarka. (4)
   ------------
   ṇaṉa īṟu alvaḻiyil iyalpātal
   147. moḻimuta lākum ellā eḻuttum
   varuvaḻi niṉṟa āyiru puḷḷiyum
   vēṟṟumai yalvaḻit tiripiṭaṉ ilavē
   itu ṇakāra īṟum ṉakāra īṟum alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyu meṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: moḻi mutalākum ellā veḻuttum varuvaḻi- moḻikku mutalāmeṉappaṭṭa
   irupattiraṇṭeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, niṉṟa āyiru
   puḷḷiyum-muṉṉarkkūṟi niṉṟa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum, vēṟṟumaiyalvaḻit
   tiripiṭaṉ ilavē-vēṟṟumaiyallāta iṭattut tiriyumiṭam ila; e-ṟu
   maṇ poṉ eṉa niṟuttik, kaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñekiḻntatu nīṇṭatu
   māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntaiyatu aṭaintatu āyiṟṟu illai īṇṭiṟṟu uṇṭu
   ūṭṭiṟṟu evviṭattatu ēṟiṟṟu aitu oḻukiṟṟu ōṅkiṟṟu auvaiyatu eṉa oṭṭuka.
   varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl ṇakārattiṟkuc ciṟupāṉmai tiripum uṇṭeṉṟu
   koḷka. cāṭkōl eṉa varum; itaṟkuc cāṇākiya kō leṉka. ivai "niṉṟacoṉmuṉ
   iyalpākum" (eḻu- 144) eṉṟa vaḻi aṭaṅkāvāyiṉa; atu varumoḻipaṟṟit
   tiriyāmai kūṟiyatātaliṉ. itu nilaimoḻipaṟṟit tiriyāmai kūṟiyatu,. (5)
   ---------
   avai vēṟṟumaiyilum iyalpātal
   148. vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum valleḻut talvaḻi
   mēṟkū ṟiyaṟkai āvayi ṉāṉa.
   itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāṉ vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tiripu eytiniṉṟavaṟṟai īṇṭu
   vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum valleḻut talvaḻit tiriyā veṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
   i-ḷ: āvayiṉāṉa-alvaḻikkaṇ aṅṅaṉan tiriyātu niṉṟa avvoṟṟukkaḷ,
   vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum-vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcci yiṭattum, valleḻuttalvaḻi
   mēṟkūṟiyaṟkai - valleḻuttallāta iṭattu mēṟkūṟiya iyalpu muṭipām; e-ṟu
   eṉavē, valleḻuttu vantuḻit tiriyu meṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
   e-ṭu: maṇ poṉ eṉa niṟutti, ñekiḻcci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmai nuntaiyatu
   aḻaku ākkam iṉmai eṉa ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu oṭṭuka. ituvuñ keykaic cūttiram.
   mēl nāṉku cūttirattāṟ kūṟiyaṉa valleḻuttu vantuḻit tiriyumāṟu tattam
   īṟṟuṭ kūṟupa. (6)
   ----------
   laṉa muṉ ta nakkaḷ tirit
   149. laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉart
   ta na eṉavariṟ ṟaṉavā kummē.
   itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyalai nōkkiyatōr varumoḻik karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: la ṉa eṉa varūum puḷḷimuṉṉar-lakāra ṉakārameṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa
   puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ta na eṉa variṉ -takāramum nakāramum mutaleṉṟu
   collumpaṭiyākac cila coṟkaḷ variṉ, ṟaṉavākum - niraṉiṟaiyāṉē avai
   ṟakāra
   ṉakāraṅkaḷākat tiriyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: kaḵṟītu kaṉṉaṉṟu poṉṟītu poṉṉaṉṟu eṉa varum. nilaimoḻittiripu
   tattam īṟṟuṭ kūṟupa. (7)
   --------
   ṇaḷamuṉ tana mey ṭaṇa vātal
   150. ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉat tōṉṟum.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ - ṇakāra ḷakāra meṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ
   muṉṉar atikārattāl takāra nakāraṅkaḷ varumeṉiṉ, ṭaṇa veṉat tōṉṟum -
   avai niraṉiṟai yāṉē ṭakāra ṇakāraṅkaḷāyt tirintu tōṉṟum: e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: maṇṭītu maṇṇaṉṟu muḵṭītu muṇṇaṉṟu eṉa varum. nilaimoḻittiripu
   tattam īṟṟuṭ kūṟupa. (8)
   ----------

          5.2. uyirīṟu meyyīṟukaḷiṉ ciṟappup puṇarcci

   muṉṉilai viṉaiccol muṭiyumāṟu
   151. uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum
   puḷḷi yiṟuti muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum
   iyalpā kunavum uṟaḻā kunavumeṉ
   ṟāyī riyala valleḻuttu variṉē.
   itu muṉṉilai viṉaiccol vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uyirīṟākiya muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum – uyirīṟāyvanta muṉṉilai
   viṉaiccoṟkaḷum, puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum - puḷḷiyīṟāy vanta
   muṉṉilai viṉaiccoṟkaḷum, valleḻuttuvariṉ - valleḻuttu mutalākiya moḻi
   variṉ, iyalpākunavum uṟaḻākunavumeṉṟu āyīriyala - iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum
   uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavu meṉa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: eṟikoṟṟā koṇākoṟṟā uṇkoṟṟā tiṉkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa ivai
   iyalpu. naṭakoṟṟā naṭakkoṟṟā īrkoṟṟā īrkkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa ivai
   uṟaḻcci. īṟeṉṟu ōtiṉamaiyiṉ viṉaiccollē koḷka. ivai muṉṉiṉṟāṉ
   toḻiluṇarttuvaṉavum, avaṉait toḻiṟ paṭuttuvaṉavu meṉa iruvakaiya. i ai
   āy mutaliyaṉa toḻiluṇarttuvaṉa.1 naṭa vā mutaliyaṉa uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟun
   toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉa.2 nilkoṟṟā niṟkoṟṟā eṉat tirin tuṟaḻntaṉavum,
   uṟaḻākunavu meṉṉum potuvakaiyāṉ muṭikka. iyalpu muṟaḻvumeṉ
   ṟiraṇṭiyalpiṉa eṉṉātu ākunavu meṉṟataṉāṉ tukkoṟṟā nokkoṟṟā ñeḷḷā nākā
   māṭā vaṭukā eṉa ōreḻut torumoḻi muṉṉilai viṉaiccol mikkē muṭital koḷka.
   (9)
   -------------
   1. toḻiluṇarttuvaṉa - ceytāy ceykiṉṟāy ceyvāy eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa mukkāla
       muṉṉilai viṉaimuṟṟu. 2. toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉa - ēval viṉaimuṟṟu.

   -----------
   ataṟkuc ciṟappu viti
   152. auveṉa varūum uyiriṟu collum
   ñanamava eṉṉum puḷḷi yiṟutiyuṅ
   kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟutiyum uḷappaṭa
   muṟṟat tōṉṟā muṉṉilai moḻikkē.
   iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu, muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cila ākātaṉavaṟṟai varaintu
   uṇarttaliṉ.
   i-ḷ: auveṉa varūum uyiriṟu collum-auveṉa varukiṉṟa uyirīṟṟuc collum,
   ñanamava eṉṉum puḷḷiyiṟutiyum -ñanamava eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷiyīṟṟuc
   collum; kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟutiyum-kuṟṟiyalukarattai iṟutiyilēyuṭaiya
   collum, muṉṉilai moḻikku uḷappaṭa muṟṟat tōṉṟā-muṉṉar muṉṉilai moḻikkup
   poruntak kūṟiya iyalpum uṟaḻcciyumākiya muṭipiṟku muṟṟat tōṉṟā; e-ṟu
   muṟṟaveṉṟataṉāṉ nilaimoḻi ukaram peṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭital koḷka.
   e-ṭu: kauvukoṟṟā kauvukkoṟṟā, vauvukoṟṟā vauvuk koṟṟā, tirumukoṟṟā
   tirumukkoṟṟā, tevvukoṟṟā tevvukkoṟṟā, kūṭṭukoṟṟā kūṭṭukkoṟṟā eṉa varum.
   (10)
   ------
   uyartiṇaip peyar puṇarumāṟu
   153. uyirī ṟākiya uyartiṇaip peyarum
   puḷḷi yiṟuti uyartiṇaip peyarum
   ellā vaḻiyum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
   iḵtu uyartiṇaippeyar vaṉkaṇam meṉkaṇam iṭaikkaṇam uyirk kaṇa meṉṉum
   nāṉku kaṇattiṉamum iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇaip peyarum-uyirīṟāy vanta uyartiṇaip
   peyarkaḷum, puḷḷi yiṟuti uyartiṇaip peyarum - puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiya
   uyartiṇaippeyarkaḷum, ellāvaḻiyum- nāṉku kaṇattu alvaḻiyum
   vēṟṟumaiyumākiya ellā iṭattum, iyalpeṉa moḻipa-iyalpāy muṭiyu meṉṟu
   kūṟuvar pulavar; e-ṟu
   vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷai "ña na ma ya va" (eḻu-144) eṉpataṉāṉ
   muṭippārum uḷar. atu poruntātu, ivvāciriyar uyartiṇaip peyarum
   viravuppeyarum eṭuttōtiyē muṭippārātaliṉ.
   e-ṭu: nampi avaṉ eṉavum, naṅkai avaḷ eṉavum niṟutti, alvaḻikkaṭ kuṟiyaṉ
   ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ eṉavum kuṟiyaḷ ciṟiyaḷ tīyaḷ periyaḷ eṉavum
   ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ eṉavum, ñāṉṟāḷ nīṇṭāḷ māṇṭāḷ eṉavum, yāvaṉ valiyaṉ
   eṉavum, yāvaḷ valiyaḷ eṉavum, aṭaintāṉ āyiṉāṉ auviyattāṉ eṉavum,
   aḻakiyaḷ āṭiṉāḷ auviyattāḷṉavum,oṭṭuka. iṉi vēṟṟumaikkaṇ kai cevi talai
   puṟam eṉavum ,ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉavum, yāppu vaṉmai eṉavum ,aḻaku
   auviyam eṉavum ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. oruvēṉ eṉat taṉmaip peyarkkaṇṇuṅ
   kuṟiyēṉ ciṟiyēṉ tīyēṉ periyēṉ eṉavum, kai cevi talai puṟam eṉavum
   oṭṭuka. nī muṉṉilai viravuppeyarātaliṉ īṇṭaik kākā.
   iṉi uyirīṟu puḷḷiyiṟuti eṉṟa mikaiyāṉē uyartiṇaip peyar tirintu
   muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. kapilaparaṇar, iṟaivaneṭuvēṭṭuvar, maruttuva
   māṇikkar eṉa ṉakara īṟu keṭṭu iyalpāy muṭintaṉa. ācīvakappaḷḷi nik
   kantakkōṭṭam eṉa ivai av vīṟu keṭṭu oṟṟu mikku muṭintaṉa. īḻa
   vakkatti vāṇikatteru aracakkaṉṉi kōlikakkaruvi eṉa ivai orumaiyīṟum
   paṉmaiyīṟuṅ keṭṭu mikku muṭintaṉa. kumarakōṭṭam kumarakkōṭṭam,
   piramakōṭṭam piramakkōṭṭam eṉa ivai īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu uṟaḻntaṉa.
   vaṇṇārappeṇṭir itu mikku muṭintatu. palcaṅkattār palcāṉṟōr pallaracar
   eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa rakaravīṟum ataṉ muṉṉiṉṟa akaramuṅ keṭṭup piṟa
   ceykaikaḷum peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.
   iṉi, ellāvaḻiyu meṉṟataṉāṉ uyartiṇai viṉaiccol iyalpāyun tirintum
   muṭivaṉa ellāṅ koḷka. uṇku uṇṭu varutu cēṟu uṇpal uṇṭēṉ uṇpēṉ eṉṉun
   taṉmai viṉaikaḷaik koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu oṭṭuka. uṇṭīr
   cāṉṟīr pārppīr eṉa muṉṉi laikkaṇṇum uṇpa uṇṭār cāṉṟār pārppār eṉap
   paṭarkkaikkaṇṇum oṭṭuka. ivai iyalpu. uṇṭaṉeñcāṉṟēm uṇṭēnām eṉṟāṟ
   pōlvaṉa tirintu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa. (11)
   ------
   ataṟkuc ciṟappu viti
   154. avaṟṟuḷ
   ikara iṟupeyar tiripiṭa ṉuṭaittē
   iḵtu uyartiṇaippeyaruṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.
   i-ḷ: avaṟṟuḷ ikara iṟupeyar-muṟkūṟiya uyar tiṇaippeyarkaḷuḷ ikara
   īṟṟuppeyar, tiripiṭaṉuṭaittu- iruvaḻiyun tirintu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭaittu;
   e-ṟu
   eṭṭi1ppū kāviti2ppū nampippēṟu eṉa ivvuyartiṇaip peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumaikkaṇ
   mikku muṭintaṉa. eṭṭi kāviti eṉpaṉa tēyavaḻakkākiya ciṟappuppeyar. eṭṭi
   maram aṉṟu. aḵtu 'eṭṭi kumara ṉiruntōṉṟaṉṉai' (maṇi. 4:58) eṉpataṉāṉ
   uṇarka. ivai eṭṭiyatupū eṭṭikkuppū eṉa viriyum. iṉi nampikkollaṉ
   nampiccāṉṟāṉ nampittuṇai nampippiḷḷai eṉavum, ceṭṭikkūttaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ
   pūtaṉeṉavum alvaḻikkaṇ uyartiṇaippeyar mikku muṭintaṉa.
   iṭaṉuṭaitteṉṟataṉāṉ ikara īṟallātaṉavum īṟu tiriyātu niṉṟu valleḻuttup
   peṟutal koḷka. naṅkaippeṇ naṅkaiccāṉi eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ ciṟupāṉmai aikāra
   īṟu mikkaṉa. ivvīṟṟaḵṟiṇaippeyar mikku muṭital uyir mayaṅkiyaluṭ
   kūṟupa. (12)
   ----------
   1 eṭṭi-vaṇikaruṭ ciṟantōrkku aracarāṟ koṭukkappaṭṭa paṭṭappeyar
       2 kāviti-uḻuvittuṇṇum vēḷāḷaruṭ ciṟantōrkku aracarāṟ koṭukkappaṭṭa
       paṭṭappeyar.

   ----------
   viravuppeyaruḷ iyalpu
   155. aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar iyalpumā ruḷavē.
   itu viravuppeyaruḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷaveṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar - uyartiṇaip peyarōṭu aḵṟiṇai viraviya
   viravuppeyar, iyalpu māruḷa – iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa, ummaiyāṉ
   iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉavum uḷa; e-ṟu.
   uyartiṇaippeyarōṭu aḵṟiṇai ceṉṟu viraviṟ ṟeṉṟa teṉṉai ? collatikārattu
   'irutiṇaic coṟkumō raṉṉa vurimaiyiṉ' (col -172) eṉṟu cūttirañ ceyvārā
   leṉiṉ, atuvum poruntumāṟu kūṟutum. cāttaṉ cātti, muṭavaṉ muṭatti eṉa
   varum viravup peyarkkaṇ uyartiṇaikku urittāka ōtiya āṇpālum peṇpālum
   uṇartti niṉṟaīṟṟeḻuttukkaḷē aḵṟiṇai yāṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttiṟ ṟeṉṟal
   vēṇṭum; eṉṉai* ? aḵṟiṇaikku orumaippālum paṉmaippālum uṇarttum īṟaṉṟi
   āṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttum īṟukaḷ uḷavāka āciriyar ōtāmaiyiṉ; aṅṅaṉam
   uyartiṇai irupālum uṇarttum īṟukaḷ niṉṟē aḵṟiṇai yāṉpālaiyum
   peṇpālaiyum uṇarttutaliṉ, aḵṟiṇai uyartiṇaiyōṭu ceṉṟu viraviṟṟeṉṟu
   avaṟṟiṉ uṇmait taṉmait tōṟṟaṅ kūṟuvāṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār. ivvāṟē
   viḷimarapiṉkaṭ 'kiḷanta viṟuti yaḵṟiṇai viravup peyar' (eḻu - 15*10)
   eṉpuḻiyum āciriyar uyartiṇaiyōṭu aḵṟiṇai viraviya viravup peyareṉa
   āṇṭum uṇmait taṉmait tōṟṟaṅ kūṟuvar. māṇākkaṉ iṉitu uṇartaṟku ivvāṟu
   viravup peyariṉatu uṇmait taṉmait tōṟṟam iraṇṭu atikārattuṅ kūṟi, av
   viravuppeyar vaḻakkiṉkaṇ irutiṇaip poruḷum uṇartti irutiṇaic collāy
   niṟṟaṟkum otta urimaiyavā meṉap pulappaṭa niṟkumāṟu kāṭṭiṉā reṉṟu
   uṇarka.
   iṉi, avai alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāy niṟkumāṟu:- cāttaṉ koṟṟaṉ cātti koṟṟi eṉa
   niṟuttik kuṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ kuṟiyaḷ ciṟiyaḷ tīyaḷ periyaḷ
   eṉavum, ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ yāvaṉ valiyaṉ ñāṉṟāḷ nīṇṭāḷ māṇṭāḷ yāvaḷ
   valiyaḷ eṉavum, aṭaintāṉ auvittāṉ aṭaintāḷ auvittāḷ eṉavum nāṉku kaṇat
   tōṭum oṭṭi uṇarka. iṉi vēṟṟumaikkaṇ kai cevi talai puṟam eṉavum, ñāṟci
   nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmai aḻaku auviyam eṉavum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ ṉakāram
   niṟpat takāra nakāram vantuḻit tiriyum utāraṇam īṇṭuk koḷḷaṟka. iṉic
   cāttaṉ kuṟitu cātti kuṟitu eṉa aḵṟiṇai muṭi pēṟpaṉavuṅ koḷka. ivaṟṟoṭu
   viṉaiccol talaippeyya ivai irutiṇaikkum uriyavām. āṇṭu nāṟpatteṭṭuc
   cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivataṉai īṇṭut tokuttār. iḵtu uyartiṇaikkum okkum.
   ummaiyāṉ iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉa ṉakāra īṟṟuṭ kāṭṭutum.
   ------------
   mūṉṟām vēṟṟumai muṭipu
   156. puḷḷi yiṟutiyum uyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
   valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟaiyāl
   tammi ṉākiya toḻiṟcol muṉvariṉ
   meymmai yākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
   ammuṟai yiraṇṭum uriyavai uḷavē
   vēṟṟumai maruṅkiṟ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.
   itu mēl uyirīṟṟiṟkum puḷḷiyīṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumaikkaṭ kūṟum muṭipu peṟātu
   niṟkum mūṉṟām vēṟṟumaimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: tammiṉākiya toḻiṟcol - mūṉṟāvataṟku uriya
   viṉaimutaṟporuḷāṉuḷavākiya toḻiṟcol, puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉum uyiriṟu kiḷavi
   muṉṉum variṉ - puḷḷiyīṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉarum uyirīṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉarum varumāyiṉ,
   meymmaiyākalum uṟaḻattōṉṟalum ammuṟai yiraṇṭum uriyavai uḷa - avaṟṟuḷ
   iyalpākalum uṟaḻattōṉṟalumākiya ammuṟai yiraṇṭum peṟutaṟku uriya
   uḷavātalāl, vēṟṟumai maruṅkiṟ colliya muṟaiyāṉ vēṇṭum valleḻuttumikuti
   - uyirmayaṅkiyaluḷḷum puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷḷum vēṟṟumaip puṇarccikkuc
   colliya muṟaiyāṉ virumpum valleḻuttu mikutiyai, pōṟṟal - īṇṭuk
   koḷḷaṟka; e-ṟu.
   meymmai paṭṭāṅkātaliṉ iyalpām.1
   e-ṭu: nāy puli eṉa niṟuttik kōṭpāṭṭāṉ cārappaṭṭāṉ tīṇṭap paṭṭāṉ
   pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa varuvittu iyalpāyavāṟu kāṇka. cūrkōṭpaṭṭāṉ
   cūrkkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷikōṭpaṭṭāṉ vaḷikkōṭ paṭṭāṉ, cārappaṭṭāṉ,
   tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivai uṟaḻntaṉa. ivai nāṟpatteṭṭuc
   cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivaṉavaṟṟait tokuttār.
   puḷḷiyiṟuti uyiriṟukiḷavi eṉṟataṉāṟ pēeykōṭpaṭṭāṉ pēeyk kōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉa
   ekarappēṟum uṟaḻccikkuk koṭukka. ammuṟai yiraṇṭu muriyavai yuḷavē
   eṉṟataṉāṟ pāmpukōṭpaṭṭāṉ pām*pukkōṭ paṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ nilaimoḻi
   yoṟṟut tiritaluṅ koḷka. ivvīṟukaḷ nāykkāl tērkkāl kiḷikkāl eṉa āṇṭu
   vēṟṟumaikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikumāṟu kāṇka. (14)
   --------
   1. paṭṭa+āṅku=paṭṭāṅku - *nērnta allatu uḷḷa vakai, uṇa*mai, uṇmai
       vēṟupāṭaṉmaiyiṉ iyalpām.

   ----------
   iraṇṭām vēṟṟumait tiripu
   157. melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippōṭu tōṉṟalum
   valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippōṭu tōṉṟalum
   iyaṟkai maruṅkiṉ mikaṟkai tōṉṟalum
   uyirmika varuvaḻi uyirkeṭa varutaluñ
   cāriyai uḷvaḻic cāriyai keṭutaluñ
   cāriyai uḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu nilaiyaluñ
   cāriyai yiyaṟkai yuṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
   uyartiṇai maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum
   aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyark kavviyal nilaiyalum
   meypiṟi tākiṭat tiyaṟkai yātalum
   aṉṉa piṟavun taṉṉiyal maruṅkiṉ
   meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvarain ticaikkum
   aikāra vēṟṟumait tiripeṉa moḻipa.
   iḵtu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumait tiripu tokuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum - 'marappeyark kiḷavi
   melleḻuttu mikumē' (eḻu - 217) eṉṟataṉāṉ viḷaṅkuṟaittāṉeṉa melleḻuttu
   mikumiṭattu viḷak kuṟaittāṉeṉa valleḻuttut tōṉṟutalum, valleḻuttu
   mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum - 'makaraviṟuti' )eḻu - 310) eṉpataṉāṉ
   marakkuṟaittāṉ eṉa valleḻuttu mikumiṭattu maraṅkuṟaittāṉ eṉa
   melleḻuttut tōṉṟutalum - iyaṟkai maruṅkiṉ mikaṟkai tōṉṟalum -
   'tāyeṉkiḷavi yiyaṟkai yākum' (eḻu - 358) eṉṟavaḻit tāykolai eṉa iyalpāy
   varumiṭattut tāykkolai eṉa mikuti tōṉṟalum, uyirmika varuvaḻi uyirkeṭa
   varutalum - 'kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum' (eḻu - 226) eṉavuṅ 'kuṟṟeḻuttimparum'
   (eḻu - 267) eṉavum 'ēyeṉiṟutikku' (eḻu - 277) eṉavuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāṉ
   uyirmikku varumiṭattup palākkuṟaittāṉ kaḻukkoṇarntāṉ ēkkaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉa
   uyir keṭa varutalum, cāriyai uḷ vaḻic cāriyai keṭutalum - 'vaṇṭum
   peṇṭum' (eḻu - 420) eṉpataṉāṟ cāriyaippēṟu uḷḷa iṭattu vaṇṭu koṇarntāṉ
   eṉac cāriyai keṭṭu niṟṟalum, cāriyai uḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu nilaiyalum -
   'vaṇṭum peṇṭum' eṉpataṉāṟ cāriyaip pēṟu uḷḷa iṭattu vaṇṭiṉaik koṇarntāṉ
   eṉat taṉṉurupu niṟṟalum (itaṟku valleḻuttup pēṟu īṟṟu vakaiyāṟ koḷka),
   cāriyai iyaṟkai uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum - 'puḷimarak kiḷavikku' (eḻu - 244)
   eṉavum 'paṉaiyumaraiyum' (eḻu - 283) eṉavum 'pūlvēleṉṟā' (eḻu - 375)
   eṉavum peṟṟa cāriyai peṟātu iyalpāy niṉṟu puḷikuṟaittāṉ puḷikkuṟaittāṉ
   paṉaitaṭintāṉ paṉaittaṭintāṉ pūlkuṟaittāṉ pūṟkuṟaittāṉ eṉa mikkun
   tirintum uṟaḻcciyākat tōṉṟalum, uyar tiṇai maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum -
   'uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇaip peyarum' (eḻu - 153) eṉpataṉāṉ vēṟṟumaikkaṇ
   iyalpāy varumeṉṟavai nampiyaik koṇarntāṉ naṅkaiyaik koṇarntāṉ eṉṟavaḻi
   iraṇṭaṉurupu tokātē niṟṟalum, (oḻiyāteṉṟataṉāṉ makaṟpeṟṟāṉ makaṭpeṟṟāṉ
   eṉavum āṭuuvaṟicol 'maḻavarōṭṭiya'(akam - 1) 'avarkkaṇ ṭemmuḷ' eṉavum
   oḻintum varumeṉṟu koḷka.)
   aḵṟiṇaiviravup peyarkku avviyal nilaiyalum - uyartiṇaiyōṭu aḵṟiṇai
   viravum peyarkkuk koṟṟaṉaik koṇarntāṉeṉa urupu tokātē niṟṟalum,
   (avviyal nilaiyalum eṉṟataṉāṉē makappeṟṟēṉeṉa viravup peyarkkaṇṇun
   tokutal koḷka. urupiyaluḷ 'tēruṅkālai' (eḻu -202) eṉṟa ilēcāṉ itaṟkum
   muṉṉaiyataṟkum valleḻuttuppēṟuṅkoḷka.) mey piṟitākiṭattu iyaṟkaiyātalum
   - puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ ṇakāra ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻuttiyaiyiṉ
   meypiṟitāmeṉṟa iṭattu meypiṟitākātu maṇ koṇarntāṉ poṉ koṇarntāṉ eṉa
   iyaṟkaiyāy varutalum, aṉṉa piṟavum - avaipōlvaṉa piṟavum, (avai
   'ekkaṇṭu peyaruṅ kālai yāḻaniṉ kaṟkeḻu ciṟukuṭi' eṉavum 'nappuṇarvu
   villā nayaṉillōr naṭpu' eṉavum varuvaḻi ekkaṇṭu nappuṇarvu eṉṉun
   toṭakkaṅkuṟukum uyartiṇaippeyarkaḷ melleḻuttup peṟutaṟku uriyaṉa val
   leḻuttup peṟutal koḷka. iṉṉun tiṉaipiḷantāṉ mayir kuṟaittāṉ taṟkoṇṭāṉ
   ceṟuttāṉ pukaḻntāṉ eṉa varum.) taṉ iyal maruṅkiṉ - taṉṉaiyē nōkkit
   tiripu
   naṭakkumiṭattu, meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvaraintu icaikkum - poruḷpeṟa
   eṭuttōtappaṭṭu ēṉai vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcciyatu potumuṭipiṉait tāṉ
   nīkki vēṟu muṭi piṟṟāy niṉṟu olikkum, aikāra vēṟṟumait tiripeṉa moḻipa
   - iraṇṭām vēṟṟumaiyatu vēṟupaṭṭa puṇarcci eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar; e-ṟu
   meypeṟa eṉṟataṉāṉē cāriyaiyuḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu nilaiyātu ceyyuṭkaṇ
   varuvaṉavum piṟavaṟṟiṉkaṇ uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. "maṟaṅkaṭinta
   varuṅkaṟpiṉ" eṉavum "cilcolliṟ pal kūntal" (puṟam- 166) eṉavum
   "āyirutiṇaiyi ṉicaikkumaṉ" (col-1) eṉavum piṟāṇṭum perumpāṉmaiyum
   varumeṉṟu koḷka. mai koṇarntāṉmaik koṇarntāṉ vilkōḷ viṟkōḷ eṉa varum,
   iṉi ivvāṟu tiriyātu akat tōttiṟ kūṟiya potu muṭipē tamakku muṭipāka
   varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. avai kaṭukkuṟaittāṉ ceppuk koṇarntāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa.
   "tammiṉākiya toḻiṟcoṉ muṉvariṉ"(eḻu-156) eṉṟa atikārattāṉ
   viṉaivantuḻiyē iṅṅaṉam perumpāṉmai tirivateṉṟu uṇarka. iṉit taṉṉiṉa
   muṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ ēḻāvataṟkum viṉaiyōṭu muṭivuḻittirital koḷka. atu
   "varaipāy varuṭai" (malaipaṭu-503) "pulampukkaṉaṉē pullaṇaṟkāḷai"
   (puṟam-258) eṉṟāṟpōla varum (15)
   -----------
   ikara aikāra īṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu
   158. vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi i ai yeṉṉum
   īṟṟuppeyark kiḷavi mūvakai nilaiya
   avaitām
   iyalpā kunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum
   uṟaḻā kunavum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
   iḵtu ikara īṟṟup peyarkkum aikāra īṟṟup peyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi-vēṟṟumai yallā iṭattu, i ai eṉṉum
   īṟṟuppeyarkkiḷavi mūvakai nilaiya-i ai eṉṉum īṟṟaiyuṭaiya peyarccoṟkaḷ
   mūvakaiyākiya muṭipu nilaiyai yuṭaiya, avai tām-ammuṭipukaṭām,
   iyalpākunavum-iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum, valleḻuttu mikunavum-valleḻuttu
   mikku muṭivaṉavum, uṟaḻākunavum-uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum, eṉmaṉār
   pulavar-eṉa ivaiyeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: parutti kuṟitu nārai kuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa ivai iyalpu;
   mācit tiṅkaḷ cittirait tiṅkaḷ alikkoṟṟaṉ pulaikkoṟṟaṉ kāvikkaṇ
   kuvaḷaikkaṇ eṉa ivai mikuti. kiḷikuṟitu kiḷikkuṟitu tiṉai kuṟitu
   tiṉaikkuṟitu eṉa ivai uṟaḻcci.
   peyarkkiḷavi mūvakainilaiya veṉavē peyarallāta iraṇ uṟṟu viṉaic collum
   iṭaiccollum uriccollum iyalpum mikutiyumākiya iruvakai nilaiyavām.
   ollaikkoṇṭāṉ eṉpatu aikāra īṟṟu viṉaic coṉ mikuti. "iṉi yaṇi" (eḻu-236)
   yeṉpataṉkaṇ ikara īṟṟu viṉaiyeccam eṭuttōtupa. ivaṟṟiṟkuiyalpuvantuḻik
   kāṇka. "ceṉmatipāka" iḵtu ikara īṟṟu iṭaiccolliyalpu. mikuti vantuḻik
   kāṇka. "tillaic collē" (col-253) iḵtu aikāra īṟṟu iṭaiccoṉ mikuti.
   iyalpu vantuḻik kāṇka. "kaṭikā" iḵtu ikara īṟṟu uric colliyalpu. mikuti
   vantuḻik kāṇka. paṇaittōḷ iḵtu aikāra īṟṟu uriccoṉ mikuti. iyalpu
   vantuḻik kāṇka. (16)
   -----------
   i ai īṟṟu ēḻām vēṟṟumai muṭipu
   159. cuṭṭumuta lākiya ikara iṟutiyum
   ekaramutal vāṉāviṉ ikara iṟutiyum
   cuṭṭucciṉai nīṭiya aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
   yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
   valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻā kunavuñ
   colliyal maruṅkiṉ uḷaveṉa moḻipa.
   iḵtu ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷuṇartti niṉṟa ikara aikāra īṟṟu iṭaiccoṉ
   muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: colliyal maruṅkiṉ-ikara aikāraṅkaṭku muṉṉark kūṟiya mūvakai
   yilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ iyalpai nīkki, cuṭṭu mutalākiya ikara
   iṟutiyum-cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya avvikara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, ekara
   viṉāviṉ mutal ikara iṟutiyum -ekaramākiya viṉāviṉai mutalākavuṭaiya
   avvikara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, cuṭṭucciṉai nīṭiya aiyeṉ iṟutiyum-cuṭṭākiya
   uṟuppeḻuttu nīṇṭa avvaikāra īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ
   iṟutiyum -yāveṉ viṉāviṉai mutaṟkaṇuṭaiya avvaikāra īṟṟu iṭaiccollum,
   valleḻuttu mikunavum-valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum,
   uṟaḻākunavum-uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum, uḷaveṉa moḻipa.-uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar
   pulavar; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: atōḷikkoṇṭāṉ itōḷikkoṇṭāṉ utōḷik koṇṭāṉ etōḷikkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ
   tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, āṇṭaik koṇṭāṉ īṇṭaikkoṇṭāṉ ūṇṭaik koṇṭāṉ yāṇṭaik
   koṇṭāṉ eṉavum ivai mikkaṉa. atōḷi avviṭameṉṉum poruṭṭu. avvaḻikoṇṭāṉ
   avvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ ivvaḻi koṇṭāṉ ivvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ uvvaḻikoṇṭāṉ uvvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ
   evvaḻikoṇṭāṉ evvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ eṉa uṟaḻantaṉa. cuṭṭucciṉai nīṇṭataṟkum
   yāviṉāviṟkum varum aikāra īṟṟukku utāraṇam akkālattu āyiṭaikoṇṭāṉ
   āyiṭaikkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum vaḻaṅkiṟṟuppōlum,. iṉi,
   āṅkavai koṇṭāṉ āṅkavaik koṇṭāṉ eṉpaṉa kāṭṭuvārum uḷar. avai
   tiripuṭaiyaṉavām. colliyal eṉṟataṉāṉē piṟa aikāra īṟu mikkumuṭivaṉa
   koḷka. aṉṟaik kūttar paṇṭaic cāṉṟō reṉavum oru tiṅkaḷaik kuḻavi
   orunāḷaik kuḻavi eṉavum varum. (17)
   -----------
   neṭilmuṉ kuṟilmuṉ oṟṟukkaḷ
   160. neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟumey keṭutaluṅ
   kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru iraṭṭalum
   aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa.
   itu puḷḷimayaṅkiya nōkkiyatōr nilaimoḻik karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟu mey keṭutalum – neṭ ṭeḻuttiṉmuṉ niṉṟa
   oṟṟut taṉvaṭivu keṭutalum, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉ uru iraṭṭalum -
   kuṟṟeḻuttiṉmuṉ niṉṟā oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu iraṭṭittalum, aṟiyat tōṉṟiya
   neṟiyiyal eṉpa - aṟiyumpaṭi vanta aṭip pāṭṭiyaleṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar ;
   e – ṟu.
   iṅṅaṉam neṭiyataṉ kuṉṉar oṟṟuk keṭuvaṉa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum
   makāramum lakāramum ḷakāramum eṉa aivakaiyavām.
   e - ṭu : kōṇimirntatu tāṉallaṉ tānallar vēṉaṉṟu tōṇaṉṟu eṉa nakaram
   varumoḻiyātaṟkaṇ neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟuk keṭṭatu. kōṟītu vēṟītu eṉat
   takaram varumoḻiyātaṟkaṇ lakāravoṟṟuk keṭṭatu. ēṉaiya vantuḻik kāṇka.
   ivaṟṟai 'laṉaveṉa varūum' (eḻu - 141) 'ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ' (eḻu - 150.)
   eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭittuk keṭumāṟu kāṇka. oṟṟiraṭṭuvaṉa ñakāra nakāra
   ḻakāram oḻintaṉa. kaṇṇaḻakitu poṉṉakal tammāṭai collaḻakitu eḷḷaḻakitu
   neyyakal tevvalaṉ eṉak kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru iraṭṭiṉa.
   mēlaic cūttirattu nāṉkaṉurupu piṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ oṟṟiraṭṭutal uyir
   mutaṉmoḻik kaṉṇateṉṟu uṇarka. kuṟiyatu piṟ kūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāl
   tammai emmai niṉṉai eṉa neṭiyaṉa kuṟukiniṉṟa vaḻiyuṅ kuṟiyataṉmuṉṉa
   roṟṟāy iraṭṭutalum, viraṉaṉṟu kuṟaṇimirntatu eṉak kuṟiliṇaiyiṉ muṉṉar
   vanta oṟṟuk keṭutalum, varāṟītu naṉṟu eṉak kuṟiṉeṭiṟkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟuk
   keṭutalum, atu ko ṟōḻi eṉavuṅ kuriciṟīyaṉ eṉavun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟu niṉṟa
   ōṟṟuk keṭutalum iṭaic collōṭu oṭṭuppaṭṭu niṟṟaluṅ,1 kāṟṟītu eṉavum
   viraṟṟītu eṉavum oṟṟu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka. iṉi aṟiya eṉṟataṉāṟ tēṉṟītu
   nāṇṭītu eṉṟāṟpōlavaṉa keṭāmai niṟṟaluṅ, keṭutalun takaranakaraṅkaḷ
   vantuḻi yeṉpatūuṅ koḷka. neṟiyiya leṉṟataṉāṟ cuṭṭiṉmuṉ uyirmutaṉ moḻi
   vantuḻi avvaṭai avvāṭai eṉa iṭai vakaravoṟṟu ilvaḻiyum iraṭṭutal koḷka.
   (18)
   ---------
   1 atu koṟōḻi eṉpatu iṭaiccollōṭu oṭṭuppaṭṭu niṉṟu toṭarmoḻiyāki
       īṟṟuniṉṟa oṟṟuk keṭṭatu ; kuriciṟīyaṉ eṉpatu
       iṭaiccollōṭuoṭṭuppaṭātu. iyalpāyat toṭarmoḻiyāyiruntu īṟṟuniṉṟa
       oṟṟuk keṭṭatu.

   ------------
   kuṟilmuṉ oṟṟu iraṭṭāta iṭam
   161. āṟaṉ urupiṉum nāṉkaṉ urupiṉuṅ
   kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lillai
   īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu nilaiyum
   neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻimuṉ ṉāṉa.
   iḵtu urupiyalai nōkkiyatōr nilaimoḻikkaruvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻimuṉ āṉa-neṭitākiya mutaleḻuttuk
   kuṟukimuṭiyum aṟuvakaippaṭṭa moḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta, āṟaṉurupiṉum
   nāṉkaṉurupiṉum -āṟām vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum nāṉkām vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum, kūṟiya
   kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭalillai-muṉṉar nilaimoḻikku iraṭṭu meṉṟa kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭi
   varutalillai, īṟākupuḷḷi akara moṭu nilaiyum-nilaimoḻi yīṟṟukkaṇ niṉṟa
   oṟṟukkaḷ akarampeṟṟu niṟkum; e-ṟu
